## APP App

#### APP-01

**Phone first** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every screen shall work at 375 px width without horizontal scrolling and on a desktop with the side navigation from 960 px, and the tab bar shall spread over however many tabs there are; no screen has an app top bar, each starts with its own title (the chat with its own bar), every section including Stack is in the tab bar, the live state is a dot on Stack, a bar shows at the top only while the app is offline or an update waits, and a page's main action fits on its title row; a card that stands for a page opens it on a tap, and an agent's text in a card is its formatted markdown.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_now_card.py::test_the_running_card_opens_its_mission_and_folds_each_finding_as_markdown`, `tests/test_now_card.py::test_a_tap_on_a_card_opens_its_page_but_not_on_its_own_links_or_folds`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_an_icon_in_text_is_one_line_high_and_no_page_has_an_app_top_bar`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_the_chat_has_one_top_bar_on_a_phone_with_the_live_state_in_it`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_the_wiki_s_new_button_is_one_line_on_the_title_row`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_the_tab_bar_spreads_however_many_tabs_there_are`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_the_section_headings_keep_their_gap_and_stay_on_one_line`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_the_swarm_s_views_are_under_agents_on_every_screen`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_new_chat_starts_afresh_and_attaching_is_a_clip`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.css`
- Findings: [FND-40](#fnd-40), [FND-41](#fnd-41), [FND-82](#fnd-82)

#### APP-02

**Every screen opens cold** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every app route shall render when it is opened directly (a link, a notification, a reload), without first visiting another screen.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-28: #/phone opened blank from a notification`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_every_screen_is_drawn_when_opened_cold`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_the_connections_card_is_started_by_render`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_view_scripts_load_before_app_js_and_take_no_arguments`
- Findings: [FND-30](#fnd-30)

#### APP-03

**Installable, with push and self-update** · must · functional · **implemented**

The app shall be installable as a PWA, deliver push notifications to devices that switched them on, and reload itself when a new version is deployed.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/sw.js`, `images/mission-control/web/manifest.webmanifest`, `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/icons.py`

#### APP-04

**Offline is said plainly** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the app cannot reach the stack for 8 seconds, then it shall say so (Tailscale off on this device, or the PC is down) instead of loading forever, and show when it last had a connection.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29: 'Loading forever like that'`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### APP-05

**Home answers three questions** · must · functional · **implemented**

Home shall show, in this order, what needs the owner, what is running now with what comes next, and a live feed of results grouped by day, with an ask bar at the top.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/01-welcome.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_home_work.py::test_home_is_needs_now_and_a_live_feed_with_an_ask_bar`, `tests/test_home_work.py::test_now_shows_work_in_progress_and_whats_next`, `tests/test_home_work.py::test_home_s_results_are_one_card_per_outcome_named_by_what_it_is_about`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-57](#fnd-57)

#### APP-06

**Needs you is only the owner's decisions** · must · functional · **implemented**

Needs you shall list only decisions that wait for the owner (builds to review, purchases, phone and Gmail approvals, grants, memories to confirm, setup steps only he can do), each one tap away, and say so in one line when there are none.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-09](#dec-09)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_home_work.py::test_needs_you_lists_only_decisions_and_says_so_when_there_are_none`
- Findings: [FND-08](#fnd-08)

#### APP-07

**Stack upkeep is visible but separate** · should · functional · **implemented**

Health alerts and the stack's own improvement ideas shall show under Now as Stack upkeep, not under Needs you, with readable rows.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-09](#dec-09)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_home_work.py::test_stack_upkeep_sits_under_now_not_needs_you`, `tests/test_home_work.py::test_list_row_chevrons_have_a_size`

#### APP-08

**Live updates without losing your place** · must · quality · **implemented**

The app shall update only the parts that changed when new state arrives, never replay entrance animations, never redraw an open timeline away, and never re-render forms, chat or reading views while the owner types or reads.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_live_redraws_do_not_replay_the_entrance_animation`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_an_open_timeline_is_not_redrawn_away`

#### APP-09

**Answers are never cached** · must · quality · **implemented**

Mission Control's API answers shall carry headers that forbid caching.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `incident: an old service worker served stale state`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_api_answers_are_never_cached`

#### APP-10

**Every value is escaped** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The front end shall escape every value it puts into HTML and sanitise rendered markdown with DOMPurify.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### APP-11

**One list of agents** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Agents tab shall list missions and crew jobs as one kind of agent, sorted by needs-you, running, then next run, with health as the card's colour and one reason line.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_systemd_state_becomes_crew_cards`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `ops/crew.py`

#### APP-12

**The Stack page shows the machine** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Stack page shall show the live architecture map, every service with its state, Windows and WSL memory, CPU and disk, each service's memory against its limit, scheduled jobs, backups, CI per repository, skills, costs and the apps, each section linking its guide chapter.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/12-stack-page.md`, [DEC-06](#dec-06)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_guide.py::test_live_map_boxes_point_at_real_sections`

#### APP-13

**The guide covers every feature** · must · quality · **implemented**

The in-app guide shall have a chapter for every screen, mention every assistant tool and every make target with help text, and every Try-it example and live block shall point at something real.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_guide.py::test_chapters_are_well_formed`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_every_app_screen_has_a_chapter`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_every_screen_s_how_this_page_works_opens_the_chapter_about_it`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_every_make_target_is_explained`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_examples_and_live_blocks_point_at_real_things`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_guide_api`

#### APP-14

**The guide says what is true** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every statement in the guide shall match the current behaviour; when behaviour changes, the guide shall change in the same commit.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/AGENTS.md`

#### APP-15

**Share anything to the stack** · should · functional · **implemented**

When the owner shares a link, text, picture or file to the installed app, Mission Control shall keep it for 14 days and open it with one-tap choices (summarise, ask, explain, save to knowledge, make a mission), doing nothing until he taps.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/02-first-ten-minutes.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_phone_share_is_kept_and_opened_in_the_app`

#### APP-16

**Activity links to its subject** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every activity-feed entry concerning a build, a run or an agent shall link to that build, run or agent.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-28`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_guide.py::test_activity_feed_links_build_events_to_their_build`

#### APP-17

**The live stream carries everything the screens need** · should · functional · **implemented**

The app's live state stream shall carry the AI phone's status alongside missions, runs, builds and the system, so that Home and the Phone page update without polling.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_the_live_stream_carries_the_ai_phone`

#### APP-18

**What's new writes itself** · could · functional · **implemented**

The What's new chapter shall list the stack's changes from its git history, newest first.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/16-whats-new.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/static/guide.js`

#### APP-19

**Nothing the owner reads is cut** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every text the owner reads (the app, Telegram, push, e-mail, the activity feed, health alerts, errors, logs, Ledger text, reviews, build output) shall be shown whole; a shortened form is allowed only as a preview with a visible marker one tap from the whole text, a list shall say how many more it holds and offer them, and a stored text that hits its cap shall say how much was cut.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-27](#dec-27)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_truncation.py::test_a_cut_says_what_it_cut_and_input_over_a_limit_is_refused`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_journal_lines_and_the_phone_s_approval_cards_are_shown_whole`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_the_app_wraps_text_and_shows_the_rest_of_a_list`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_a_crew_agent_s_claude_task_is_named_after_it_and_keeps_its_whole_question`
- Trace: `ops/textcap.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.css`, `ops/crew.py`
- Findings: [FND-38](#fnd-38)

#### APP-20

**The owner's input is refused, never cut** · must · quality · **implemented**

When the owner sends a text or a list over a limit, the stack shall refuse it with the limit named and shall never store or act on part of it.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-27](#dec-27)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_truncation.py::test_the_owner_s_long_input_is_refused_with_its_limit_not_cut`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_a_cut_says_what_it_cut_and_input_over_a_limit_is_refused`
- Trace: `ops/textcap.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Findings: [FND-38](#fnd-38)

#### APP-21

**One list of ideas** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every open idea to improve the stack or its agents (the crew's and the self-review's ideas and the Coach's agent changes) shall be in one ranked list under Agents, each marked with its kind, with the decided ones and the autonomy and pause rules below it; other screens shall only link to that list with the number waiting for the owner.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-29](#dec-29)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_every_idea_is_in_one_list_under_agents`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### APP-22

**Every chart moves, zooms and names its dots the same way** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every zoomable chart (the code graph, the wiki graphs, Who works with whom) shall move with two fingers or the mouse and zoom with two fingers, ctrl and the wheel or its + and − buttons, fit everything in with its fit button, and leave one finger and a plain wheel to the page (a chart never swallows a scroll); a live redraw shall keep its dots where they were and its zoom; every dot shall have a name, shown when it covers no busier dot's name, at the same size on screen at any zoom, and a tap on a dot shall say what it is.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-swarm.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_charts.py::test_every_chart_moves_and_zooms_through_one_helper_and_one_finger_scrolls_the_page`, `tests/test_charts.py::test_a_redraw_keeps_the_map_s_dots_and_zoom`, `tests/test_charts.py::test_names_never_cover_each_other_and_the_busiest_keep_theirs`, `tests/test_charts.py::test_the_map_names_every_dot_and_shows_open_ideas_when_asked`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.css`

#### APP-23

**No call of the app waits forever** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every call the app makes shall end within its time limit (45 s, 30 s for a request, 15 s for each check of a slow request's answer); a request that gets no answer shall say so and, for a new mission, tell the owner to look under Missions before trying again; a check of a slow request that stalls shall be retried, and a slow request still running after 5 minutes shall say that it goes on. The New mission and mission settings forms shall send their request for every mission type, and an error in the app shall be shown to the owner as well as reported.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_app_timeouts.py::test_a_request_that_never_answers_ends_with_a_clear_message_instead_of_spinning`, `tests/test_app_timeouts.py::test_a_poll_that_stalls_is_retried_and_a_finished_request_still_arrives`, `tests/test_app_forms.py::test_start_mission_sends_the_request_for_every_type_and_a_price_watch_runs_daily`, `tests/test_app_forms.py::test_mission_settings_save_without_a_type_field`, `tests/test_app_forms.py::test_an_error_in_the_app_is_told_to_the_owner_not_only_reported`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-51](#fnd-51), [FND-52](#fnd-52)

#### APP-24

**An error in the app is kept, raised and caught before it lands** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every error the app reports shall be kept (the newest 500, in the missions data) and, while any came in the last 24 hours, raised as one alert on the health board naming the most frequent one and its screen; every landing and every image build shall lint the app's scripts for undefined names and stop on one; and ops/ui_crawl.js shall click through every kind of screen at phone width, with forms filled in and every request recorded instead of sent.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_app_errors.py::test_errors_the_app_hit_are_kept_and_become_one_alert_on_the_health_board`, `tests/test_app_errors.py::test_the_server_keeps_each_error_and_the_checks_lint_the_app_s_scripts`, `tests/test_ux_review.py::test_the_stack_page_names_each_failed_job_and_links_it_and_the_crawl_is_kept`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/app_lint.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `ops/ui_crawl.js`, `images/builder/Dockerfile`, `images/ci-runner/Dockerfile`
- Findings: [FND-53](#fnd-53), [FND-54](#fnd-54)

#### APP-25

**What the owner asked for comes before the stack's diagnostics** · must · functional · **implemented**

A mission's page shall show its latest result before its run log unless a run is live, and fold the health board's notes under one line that says they need nothing from the owner; an agent's card shall show its alert, or else its result, and count its notes; Home's feed shall open on the owner's results, shopping and errors, with the stack's own work one chip away; a copy button shall say Copied only when the text was copied and say why when not; and the Stack page shall name and link each failed job.

#### APP-36

**A task's page tells its whole story, the result first** · must · functional · **implemented**

A task's page shall open on its result and then tell how it was done, most important first, each part opening a tab with the whole of it: who worked on it as a map of the agents (each agent a bubble coloured by its part and sized by its own tokens, joined by its hand-offs, calls and starts, each tappable for its part, runs, tokens, cost, models, tools and memory use, and the same in words); the definition of done with the verdict; what went wrong (failed steps, retries, failed or silent runs, errors, points not met); its sources (read, cited, by site, by step and agent); its memory use by store (wikis, notes, journals, long-term memory, the swarm's board, lessons) with what was read and kept, including what the stack read or kept around the agents (a refinement's wiki, a briefing's topic wiki pages, the answer); what it made (the answer, reports, wiki pages, files, findings, deliveries); its cost and time by agent and model; and the plan. A recurring task shall show one run at a time (its newest, or the one picked), and a task handed to Claude Code, a build, a chat or a mission shall show its agents too. The map's colours shall pass the colour-blind check on both themes.

#### APP-39

**What you make in a chat, you reach from the chat, and it leads back** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every chat answer in which Hermes made something (a mission, a build, a Claude task, a repository, a note in the vault, an e-mail) shall show it under the answer as a card with its kind, its name, its state now (being created, queued, running, done, waits for you, failed) and its result's headline, opening its page in one tap; a change to an item (paused, triggers set, sent back) shall be one line. ## AST Assistant

### AST-01

**One assistant in every channel** · must · functional · **implemented**

Hermes' main profile shall answer the owner in Mission Control's chat, on Telegram and in Open WebUI, with the same tools and memory.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `README.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### AST-02

**Chat sends only the new turn** · must · quality · **implemented**

Mission Control shall send Hermes only the new message with a Hermes session id per chat, and start a new session when a message is edited or regenerated.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/03-chat.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_chat_sends_only_the_new_turn_with_a_hermes_session`, `tests/test_server.py::test_edit_starts_a_new_session`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`
- Refined by: [APP-39](#app-39), [MIS-27](#mis-27)

### AST-03

**Answers survive a disconnect** · must · quality · **implemented**

Mission Control shall run each chat answer as a server-side task that is saved whether or not the phone stays connected, and the app shall follow it over a reconnecting stream.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_interrupted_chats_are_closed_on_start`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`

### AST-04

**Tool steps show while Hermes works** · should · functional · **implemented**

While Hermes answers, the chat shall show each tool it uses with a plain label.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/03-chat.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_tool_progress_labels`

### AST-05

**Model, effort and attachments per message** · should · functional · **implemented**

The chat shall let the owner pick the model and the reasoning effort per message (remembered per device) and attach images, sent as images, and documents (PDF, Word, Excel, CSV, text), sent as extracted text.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### AST-06

**The owner's chats are kept apart** · should · functional · **implemented**

Chats started by tests, agents or the AI phone shall be kept apart from the owner's own chats in the chat history.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-28`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_home_work.py::test_chats_from_tests_and_agents_are_kept_apart_from_yours`

### AST-07

**Every tool Hermes has is described** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every Mission Control MCP tool shall be registered with a description, explained in a guide chapter and given a plain-language line in the guide's tool help.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_mcp_tools_are_registered`, `tests/test_guide.py::test_every_assistant_tool_is_explained`

### AST-08

**Profiles carry only what they use** · should · quality · **implemented**

The scout, mission and builder profiles shall carry only the toolsets they use (no skills, no clarify on unattended profiles), to keep the fixed cost of every call low.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_agent_profiles_carry_only_what_they_use`
- Trace: `config/hermes/configure-scout.sh`, `config/hermes/configure-mission.sh`, `config/hermes/configure-builder.sh`

### AST-09

**Hermes delegates to Claude Code, read-only** · should · functional · **implemented**

When Hermes hands a question to Claude Code, the runner shall run it read-only on a copy of the repository's main branch (or on passed text and validated, redacted evidence), show its steps with thinking, tools and tokens, and wait for the reset if the Claude limit is reached.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`, [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_a_run_is_read_only_and_answers`, `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_steps_show_thinking_tools_and_tokens`, `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_a_usage_limit_waits_for_the_reset`, `tests/test_claude_tasks.py::test_evidence_is_validated_gathered_and_put_next_to_the_question`
- Trace: `ops/claude_tasks.py`

### AST-10

**Learned skills are reviewed** · must · functional · **implemented**

Hermes' skills shall be mirrored to git every 5 minutes, and a learned skill with risky capabilities shall be parked until the owner approves it.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/skills_sync.py`, `ops/systemd/skills-sync.timer`

### AST-11

**The skill curator is gated by the benchmark** · must · quality · **implemented**

The weekly skill curation shall keep its changes only if the frozen benchmark's gate subset scores at least as well afterwards, and roll back otherwise.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `eval/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/curate.py`, `eval/fixtures`
- Trace: `config/hermes/configure-curator.sh`

### AST-12

**Chat during a usage limit** · should · functional · **partial**

If the subscription's usage limit is reached, then the chat shall say so and when it expects to answer again, instead of failing with a bare error.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/14-safety.md`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_chat_limit.py::test_a_usage_limit_keeps_the_message_and_says_when_the_limit_resets`, `tests/test_chat_limit.py::test_after_the_reset_the_message_is_answered_by_itself_and_the_owner_is_told`, `tests/test_chat_limit.py::test_a_limit_that_lasts_a_day_stops_and_try_now_moves_the_retry`, `tests/test_chat_limit.py::test_a_normal_answer_about_rate_limits_is_not_a_usage_limit`
- Gap: The app's chat keeps the message, says when the limit resets, answers by itself afterwards and notifies the owner; on Telegram, Hermes' own gateway answers the limit with its own message, which the stack does not shape.

### AST-13

**Morning digest** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every morning at 07:30 the digest shall send the night's results to Telegram.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/07-delivery.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/digest.py`, `ops/systemd/digest.timer`

### AST-14

**Hermes never approves** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Hermes' tools shall be able to read builds, request changes and re-run checks, and shall never be able to approve a build, a purchase, a grant or a phone action.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-07](#cst-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_hermes_can_review_and_request_changes_but_never_approve`, `tests/test_server.py::test_purchases_are_decided_only_by_the_owner`

## AUT Autonomy

### AUT-01

**Each kind of action holds a licence** · must · functional · **proposed**

Every kind of action that can wait for the owner shall belong to an action class with a licence at one of three levels (ask, do and tell with an undo window, do quietly), decided in one place that every door calls, with the class's exam, bars, highest level and the evidence for its current level on the class's page.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: Earned autonomy ... Design this out.`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -
- Rationale: The Memory Keeper showed the pattern (DEC-40); the owner's taps queue up in nine places.

### AUT-02

**A licence is earned by exam, shadow and record, and lost at once** · must · functional · **partial**

A class shall rise one level only when its answer-key exam passes on its last three runs, its shadow decisions agreed with at least 90 % of the owner's last 20 decisions in that class and never chose to act where the owner refused, its acts of 14 days had no undo or failed check, and the owner allows the step once; it shall drop one level at once, saying why, on a failed exam, such a disagreement, an undo or a failed act, and go back under exam when the agent, prompt or code it runs on changes.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_shadow_records_once_and_then_the_owner_s_outcome`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`
- Gap: The shadow records, from 2 Nov 2026, what the stack would decide for each yes-or-no decision and the owner's outcome where its source keeps it, and shows the agreement per class on #/decide. No class is promoted or demoted by it yet: that needs the exams (TRL-16) and two weeks of answers.
- Findings: [FND-112](#fnd-112)

### AUT-03

**Some actions are never licensed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Money above the ceiling the owner set, a new payee, writing to a person in the owner's name, deleting data that cannot be restored, security settings, credentials, accounts, public exposure and anything on the AI phone outside its listed routines shall stay at ask whatever a class's scores; no code path shall raise them.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_floor_stays_at_ask_whatever_its_configured_level`
- Trace: `config/autonomy.yaml`, `ops/decisions.py`

### AUT-04

**Every unasked act is logged with its evidence and can be undone where possible** · must · functional · **proposed**

Each act a licence allowed shall be recorded with the class, the level and the evidence that allowed it, shown in the run record and the day's digest, and offer an undo for its window where the act can be undone.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### AUT-05

**One decision queue** · must · functional · **partial**

Everything that waits for the owner (a task's start or questions, a deploy, a purchase, phone steps, an unsettled memory dispute, an agent change, a skill, an idea, a licence promotion) shall be one ranked list in the app, each with what, why, the stack's recommendation, what happens without an answer and when, and one-tap answers; routine items of a class may be answered together.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: One daily decision queue on your phone, by voice too`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_every_source_s_waits_are_one_ranked_list_with_what_why_and_a_recommendation`, `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_server_adds_the_waits_only_it_sees_and_the_app_draws_the_list`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Gap: #/decide lists builds, purchases, AI phone cleanup steps and approval requests, Gmail's Trash batch, memories to confirm, tasks to start or answer, what agents ask, open ideas and disputes and agent changes, ranked, with the stack's recommendation (2 Nov 2026). Skills and licence promotions have no source yet; a task's start, a memory and the phone's cleanup steps are answered on the list, the rest by one tap to their page.

### AUT-06

**Decisions reach the owner once a day, and by voice** · should · functional · **proposed**

The queue shall notify once a day at the owner's time (sooner only for a decision expiring within two hours), and Hermes shall read it out and take yes, no or later by voice for every decision except money, which is confirmed by tap where the amount is shown.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### AUT-07

**The queue is measured** · should · quality · **implemented**

The app shall show decisions a day, the median time to answer and how many expired, with the target of at most five a day answered within twelve hours.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_decisions.py::test_the_queue_s_numbers_a_day_the_median_and_the_expired`
- Trace: `ops/decisions.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### AUT-08

**Low-risk code may deploy itself, and reverts itself** · should · functional · **proposed**

A build that changes under 300 lines, touches no security, compose, secrets, NAS, network or agent configuration path, lowers no requirement's status and passes the checks and both reviews may deploy at the coding-low-risk class's level, and shall be reverted automatically when the health check after the deploy fails.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

## BLD Builder

### BLD-01

**Assignments get their own branch** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a build is assigned, the runner shall validate it and work on a branch named builder/<id>-<slug> in the repository's workbench clone.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_assign_validates_and_names_the_branch`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`

### BLD-02

**Approve needs review, checks and CI** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The runner shall deploy an approved build only when it is in review, its sandbox checks passed and its CI is green; approving while CI still runs shall deploy it the moment CI passes, and not if CI fails.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-07](#cst-07)
- Sources: [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_requires_review_checks_and_green_ci`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_while_ci_runs_deploys_when_it_passes`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_all_takes_only_what_is_ready`
- Findings: [FND-07](#fnd-07)

### BLD-03

**Approve merges, deploys or rolls back** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner approves a build, the runner shall merge it with --no-ff into the default branch, push, deploy, and roll back on a failed deploy.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_approved_build_merges_deploys_or_rolls_back`

### BLD-04

**Change requests carry feedback** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall send a build back only with written feedback, and the builder shall continue on the same branch.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_revise_needs_feedback`

### BLD-05

**Builds check against today's main** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before the checks, the runner shall merge today's main into the build branch and set aside uncommitted leftovers, so that the review shows only the build's own change.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-28: an old branch showed 45 files`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_an_old_build_branch_gets_todays_main`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_uncommitted_builder_leftovers_are_set_aside`

### BLD-06

**A looping builder is stopped** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the builder makes 6 tool calls in a row with empty arguments, then the runner shall stop the build and say why.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-28: empty tool arguments loop`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_empty_tool_call_streak`

### BLD-07

**Sensitive files are flagged** · must · functional · **implemented**

The review shall flag changes to sensitive files (security settings, deploy and build code, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, SOUL text).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_review_flags_sensitive_files`

### BLD-08

**Two engines** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each build shall run on one of two engines, Hermes on the ChatGPT subscription or Claude Code on the Claude subscription, Claude Code by default when it is connected; an unknown engine shall be refused, and a build may move to the other engine except while that would lose unfinished work.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_engine_is_validated`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_switch_engine_allowed_and_refused`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_claude_stream_becomes_steps_and_result`
- Findings: [FND-06](#fnd-06)

### BLD-09

**A usage limit pauses a build** · must · functional · **implemented**

When an engine reaches its usage limit, the build shall wait until the reported reset and resume with its unfinished work.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/10-builder.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_claude_usage_limit_gives_the_reset_time`

### BLD-10

**The other engine reviews** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before a build reaches the owner, the other engine shall review its assignment, diff and checks and give a verdict (fine to deploy, improve first, do not deploy) with findings; if that reviewer is at its limit, the builder's own engine shall review, marked less independent.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-04](#dec-04)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_the_other_engine_reviews`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_review_verdicts_are_normalised`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_rate_limited_reviewer_hands_over_to_the_other_engine`

### BLD-11

**Code tools for both engines** · should · functional · **implemented**

Both engines shall get Serena's language-server tools and the code graph, read-only.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/builder/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_both_engines_get_serena`, `tests/test_builds.py::test_both_engines_get_the_code_graph_read_only`

### BLD-12

**Builds survive a runner restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the runner restarts during a build, the build shall resume; an interrupted deploy shall be marked failed, not repeated.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_builds_cut_off_by_a_runner_restart_are_resumed`

### BLD-13

**The Forgejo token goes only to the runner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

When the owner pastes a Forgejo token, the runner shall check it against Forgejo and store it encrypted, and the token shall never reach Hermes or any model.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_forgejo_connect_checks_then_stores_encrypted`, `tests/test_server.py::test_forgejo_token_goes_to_the_runner_only`

### BLD-14

**Honest token counts** · should · quality · **implemented**

Builds on both engines shall count all input tokens including cached ones, and show the cached share.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_claude_builds_count_cache_reads_like_chatgpt_builds`

### BLD-15

**Repositories and their graphs** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Code tab shall list every repository with its graph, code map and digest, reduce the graph to the 200 most-connected nodes for drawing, prefer the code map's digest for the overview, and show a node's source (inside the repository only) with what it calls and what uses it.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_repo_info_from_graph_and_code_map`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_graph_is_reduced_for_drawing`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_overview_prefers_the_code_map_digest`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_node_details_have_source_links_and_code_map`, `tests/test_app_routes.py::test_the_interactive_code_map_is_inside_the_repo_page`, `tests/test_repos.py::test_a_code_graph_the_update_cannot_read_is_built_anew_and_a_failure_is_logged_once_per_commit`
- Trace: `ops/repos.py`, `ops/graphify-sync.sh`
- Findings: [FND-50](#fnd-50)

### BLD-23

**Every repository's code map, shown clean** · should · functional · **implemented**

The repo page shall show the interactive code map of every repository that has one, switching the viewer to it when it shows another, without the viewer's minimap; and a code map shall raise none of the viewer's warnings of the stack's own making (the code graph's cache counted as changed files, the viewer's corrections to the graph).

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_every_repo_with_a_code_map_shows_it_clean_and_without_the_minimap`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `ops/graphify-sync.sh`, `ops/understand.sh`, `ops/ua_fix.mjs`, `images/opencode/Dockerfile`
- Findings: [FND-48](#fnd-48)

### BLD-16

**Removing a repository** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Removing a repository shall delete only the stack's copy (clone, graph, code map, workbench), never the Forgejo repository, and ai-stack itself shall not be removable.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_repos.py::test_remove_takes_the_repo_out_but_never_ai_stack`

### BLD-17

**Code maps explain every file** · should · functional · **implemented**

Where Understand Anything left placeholder explanations, the enrichment shall explain each file, function and layer from the code itself, in symbol excerpts for big files, and resume where it stopped after a usage limit.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_fallback_graph_gets_real_explanations_and_useful_layers`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_a_usage_limit_stops_and_a_second_run_resumes`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_other_fallback_wordings_count_as_placeholders_and_real_text_does_not`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_a_big_file_is_explained_in_parts_that_each_show_their_symbols`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_layer_answers_keyed_by_name_are_used`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_big_files_are_cut_to_symbol_excerpts`, `tests/test_ua_enrich.py::test_understand_runs_the_enrichment_and_ignores_graphify_output`
- Trace: `ops/ua_enrich.py`

### BLD-18

**Studied code is never run** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Analysing a third-party repository shall only read it, in a sandbox without internet access or keys.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/11-code.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/understand.sh`

### BLD-19

**Graphs stay current** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a repository's HEAD changes, its code graph shall be rebuilt within 2 minutes, without model calls.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14)
- Sources: `images/graphify/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/graphify-sync.sh`, `ops/systemd/graphify-sync.timer`

### BLD-20

**Supervised sessions land like builds** · should · process · **implemented**

When an owner-supervised coding session (Claude Code outside the builder) lands a change on main, ops/land.sh shall run the checks on the exact commit first and stop on any failure, take the land and deploy locks, restart the runner when the deploy asks for it, and record the deploy with its source and the owner's approval.

- Parents: [CST-07](#cst-07), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_deploy.py::test_a_deploy_is_recorded_with_its_source`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_the_landing_script_checks_before_it_merges`
- Trace: `ops/land.sh`, `ops/deploy.py`
- Rationale: Most changes so far were landed by such sessions (flock ~/.aistack-land.lock, ops/deploy.py) without the builder's review, CI first or a record in Mission Control (FND-04).
- Findings: [FND-04](#fnd-04)

### BLD-21

**A clash only in generated files is not a conflict** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a merge into or from a build branch clashes only in files drawn from other files (requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md), the runner shall draw them again from the merged sources and complete the merge; any other clash shall still stop it.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-29: build #9 and four session commits both changed requirements; only REQUIREMENTS.md clashed`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_clash_only_in_the_generated_requirements_is_drawn_again`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Rationale: Builds and sessions run side by side, and most of them update requirements, so the rendered file clashes nearly every time two land on the same day.

### BLD-22

**Builds run side by side in their own clones** · should · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall run up to BUILD_SLOTS builds at once (2 on the 16 GB PC), each in its own clone of the repo (/srv/ai/workbench/<repo>.<slot>) and only while it holds the lease on its files, with at most one ChatGPT build at a time, and a build cut off by a usage limit shall resume in the clone it started in.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_builds_run_together_only_when_their_files_do_not_overlap`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Rationale: A ChatGPT build's tokens are counted by its key's time window, so two at once would count each other's calls.

### BLD-24

**A repository without a check script runs its tests** · must · functional · **implemented**

The builder's checks on a repository without its own check script shall run its tests as pytest finds them (a tests folder, or test_*.py files up to three folders deep), else its Makefile's test target or its npm test, and say plainly when it has none.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `trial semver, 1 Nov 2026: build #13 checks said "no automatic checks in this repo" though it had 114 tests`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_a_repo_without_a_check_script_runs_its_tests`
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`
- Findings: [FND-94](#fnd-94)

### BLD-25

**The builder is measured on the owner's own projects** · should · quality · **proposed**

The builder's page shall show the changes merged a week into the owner's own repositories and how many were reverted, and one real change a week on such a repository shall be part of the probes.

- Parents: [SN-14](#sn-14), [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: The builder on your own projects`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

## CNX Connections

### CNX-01

**Nothing without a grant** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every mail tool shall refuse when the owner has not granted its scope, and tell the agent to ask him.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_nothing_without_a_grant`
- Trace: `images/mail/mail.py`

### CNX-02

**Grants by scope and time** · must · functional · **implemented**

The owner shall grant read, organize and trash separately for 1 hour, 8 hours or until revoked, revoke takes effect at once, and grants shall be refused from the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-connections.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_connection_grants_and_the_ai_phone`

### CNX-03

**Trash only, first batch asks, undo** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector shall move mail only to Trash, never expunge, never archive or trash protected mail (bank, payment, security, password reset, verification codes), wait for the owner's OK on the first Trash batch of each grant, and keep an undo record per batch.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_trash_asks_first_keeps_bank_mail_and_can_be_undone`

### CNX-04

**No sending** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector shall have no tool that sends, forwards, or changes filters or settings.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_mail_tools_cannot_send`

### CNX-05

**The connector is sealed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The mail connector's firewall shall allow only IMAPS to Gmail, and the app password shall never be logged or returned.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mail/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mail/entrypoint.sh`, `images/mail/mail.py`

### CNX-06

**A first-Trash approval survives a restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the mail connector restarts while a first-Trash approval waits, then the card shall not be left unanswerable; it shall be dropped and asked again, or kept answerable.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `same pattern as the AI phone's orphaned cards, fixed 2026-10-29`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_mail.py::test_a_card_left_by_a_restart_is_dropped_with_its_late_answer_and_a_waited_card_stays`
- Trace: `images/mail/mail.py`
- Findings: [FND-14](#fnd-14)

## CST Constraints

### CST-01

**One PC, one NAS** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The stack shall run on one Windows 10 Pro PC (i7-7700K, 16 GB RAM) inside WSL2 (Ubuntu-24.04, capped at 10 GB) with rootless Docker, plus a Synology DS923+ NAS (4 GB RAM) for git, CI, backups and Home Assistant.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `README.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `README.md`, `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Trace: `nas/forgejo/compose.yaml`
- Rationale: This is the hardware there is; 32 GB RAM is recommended but not bought.
- Refined by: [PLT-01](#plt-01), [SWM-74](#swm-74), [SWM-75](#swm-75)

### CST-02

**Subscription models only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall serve only subscription models (the ChatGPT subscription through LiteLLM), and Claude Code shall run only on the owner's Claude subscription; no paid per-token model shall be configured.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), [DEC-16](#dec-16), `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_every_key_holds_only_subscription_models`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_models_are_served_by_the_gateway`
- Trace: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `ops/keys.sh`, `config/litellm/chatgpt-models.yaml`
- Findings: [FND-05](#fnd-05)
- Refined by: [BLD-08](#bld-08), [GW-01](#gw-01), [GW-02](#gw-02), [MIS-05](#mis-05), [SWM-02](#swm-02), [SWM-29](#swm-29), [TRL-11](#trl-11), [TSK-14](#tsk-14), [TSK-18](#tsk-18)

### CST-03

**No admin rights needed** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every operation of the stack shall work without Windows administrator rights and without sudo in WSL; root in WSL is used only by the documented bootstrap and disk-heal steps.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`, `owner: find no-admin routes`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`, `ops/bootstrap-wsl-1-root.sh`
- Refined by: [PLT-07](#plt-07)

### CST-04

**Nothing public** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No service shall be reachable from the internet; PC services shall bind 127.0.0.1 and be reached only through tailscale serve, NAS services only on the home LAN, and no router port forward shall exist.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `.semgrep.yml`, `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Trace: `compose/apps.yaml`, `compose/core.yaml`, `nas/homeassistant/compose.yaml`
- Refined by: [HSE-09](#hse-09), [PLT-02](#plt-02), [SWM-27](#swm-27), [SWM-77](#swm-77), [TSK-10](#tsk-10), [TSK-19](#tsk-19)

### CST-05

**NAS hard rules** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No agent and no automated job shall delete NAS data or Docker volumes, change RAID/SHR or storage pools, touch unrelated shares, or weaken NAS security; NAS changes shall be applied only host-side by documented make targets or by the owner.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `nas/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `nas/AGENTS.md`, `ops/deploy-ha.sh`, `ops/deploy-testrepo.sh`
- Refined by: [HSE-09](#hse-09), [PLT-22](#plt-22), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-06

**Secrets encrypted, never read by agents** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Secrets shall exist only sops+age-encrypted in git and decrypted into a process environment per command; no agent shall read secrets/, a .env file or an *.enc.yaml file, and no secret shall be printed, logged or committed in plain text.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `secrets/AGENTS.md`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_deploy_never_prints_a_key`, `tests/test_server.py::test_forgejo_token_goes_to_the_runner_only`
- Trace: `.semgrep.yml`, `Makefile`
- Refined by: [BLD-13](#bld-13), [CNX-05](#cnx-05), [HSE-01](#hse-01), [SEC-10](#sec-10), [SEC-16](#sec-16), [SWM-68](#swm-68)

### CST-07

**No deploy without the owner** · must · constraint · **partial**

No change shall be deployed to the running stack without the owner's explicit approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/SECURITY.md`, `AGENTS.md`, [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_builds.py::test_approve_requires_review_checks_and_green_ci`, `tests/test_server.py::test_hermes_can_review_and_request_changes_but_never_approve`
- Gap: Builds need the owner's tap, but interactive Claude Code sessions land and deploy directly under a chat approval, outside the builder's review and before CI has run (FND-04).
- Findings: [FND-04](#fnd-04)
- Refined by: [AST-14](#ast-14), [BLD-02](#bld-02), [BLD-20](#bld-20), [SWM-42](#swm-42), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-08

**Pinned versions, monthly upgrades** · must · constraint · **partial**

Every image and package shall be pinned to an exact version or digest, and upgrades shall happen only through UPGRADE.md, one component at a time.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `AGENTS.md`, `UPGRADE.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `versions.env`, `versions.lock`, `UPGRADE.md`
- Gap: Every image is pinned and the health board compares the running images with their tags and the lock file (PLT-20), but it only notices drift and does not prevent it. On 2026-10-30 five containers ran other builds than their tags (FND-67); they were recreated on their tagged builds on 2026-10-31.
- Findings: [FND-67](#fnd-67)
- Refined by: [PLT-19](#plt-19), [PLT-20](#plt-20)

### CST-09

**English** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Code, comments, UI text, docs, reports, wikis, Telegram messages and commit messages shall be in English.

- Parents: [SN-12](#sn-12)
- Sources: [DEC-02](#dec-02), `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_desk.py::test_every_briefing_is_english_even_for_a_topic_once_marked_german`
- Trace: `AGENTS.md`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `images/desk/desk.py`
- Findings: [FND-21](#fnd-21)

### CST-10

**No bot-detection evasion, no ToS bypass** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No part of the stack shall evade bot detection or CAPTCHAs, or automate a provider's web interface against its terms (no ChatGPT-web bridge).

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-12](#dec-12), `images/browser/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/browser/AGENTS.md`, `images/pricewatch/AGENTS.md`
- Refined by: [RES-06](#res-06)

### CST-11

**No credentials or payment data typed by agents** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No agent shall type a password, a payment card, a bank or account number, or a one-time code, and no agent shall execute a financial trade or transfer; a purchase shall happen only as the owner-approved final tap of SHP requirements.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SHOPPING.md`, `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_secret_fields_and_card_numbers_are_never_typed`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_final_check_passes_only_the_approved_order`
- Refined by: [SHP-07](#shp-07), [SHP-09](#shp-09), [SWM-69](#swm-69)

### CST-12

**Not the main phone** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall drive only the old test phone (ai-phone), never the owner's main phone.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-17](#dec-17), `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/entrypoint.sh`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Refined by: [PHN-08](#phn-08)

### CST-13

**The benchmark stays frozen** · must · constraint · **implemented**

eval/bench-v1.yaml shall never change; a new benchmark is a new file with its own frozen hash.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `eval/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/check.sh`, `eval/FROZEN.sha256`

### CST-14

**Memory budget** · should · constraint · **violated**

The services in WSL together shall stay within the 10 GB WSL cap, each with a mem_limit, and the Windows host shall keep at least 1 GB free while the stack, one build and one Claude session run.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, `docs/DOGFOOD.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_full_swap_is_a_note_and_an_alert_when_ram_is_short_too`
- Trace: `compose/AGENTS.md`, `ops/windows/host-probe.ps1`, `ops/crew.py`
- Gap: The Windows host drops to about 0.45 GB free with Claude sessions and builds running, and wsl.exe then times out; nothing budgets memory across services (FND-18). Since 2026-10-30 the health board notes full swap and alerts when RAM is short too (This PC), so the limit is seen before reads fail.
- Findings: [FND-18](#fnd-18)
- Refined by: [PLT-01](#plt-01), [PLT-21](#plt-21), [SWM-74](#swm-74), [SWM-86](#swm-86), [TSK-08](#tsk-08)

### CST-15

**The Max plan's API credits, capped, for critical calls only** · must · constraint · **proposed**

The gateway may serve one strong Claude model on the API key of the Console organisation linked to the Max plan's monthly API credits, that organisation having no card and no auto-reload; only the calls of docs/NEXT-PHASE.md Part 6 may use it, the stack shall move them back to the ChatGPT subscription at 80 % of the month's credits, and no other per-token model shall be configured.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: some agents with low token throughput but high criticality use stronger models`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -
- Rationale: CST-02 allows subscription models only; the credits come with the Max subscription and stop when spent, so they add no cost, but they change CST-02's wording, which is the owner's decision (D8).

## CRW Crew

### CRW-01

**Crew jobs are agents** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each crew job shall appear as an agent with its schedule, last and next run, last result and health, taken from its systemd unit.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_systemd_state_becomes_crew_cards`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`

### CRW-02

**Crew schedules are editable in the app** · must · functional · **implemented**

The owner shall be able to set a crew job's schedule (a preset, an interval or an OnCalendar time), switch it off or on, and run it now from its Settings, applied as a systemd drop-in.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_text_times_and_intervals`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_crew_actions_are_validated`

### CRW-03

**The weekly self-review** · must · functional · **implemented**

Once a week the self-review shall collect the stack's signals (failed runs other than usage-limit pauses, failed jobs, error events, failed or rolled-back builds, red CI, open review items, newer versions) and turn them into ideas in the swarm's Ledger with one model call, and make no model call when there are no signals.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_scan_turns_signals_into_ideas_and_refines_a_known_one`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_signals_no_model_call`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_when_a_self_review_is_due`
- Trace: `ops/improve.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

### CRW-04

**The owner decides every idea** · must · functional · **implemented**

Build it shall turn an idea into an ordinary build assignment (review and tap-to-deploy still apply) and move the idea to active with its build linked, and Dismiss shall decline it with an optional reason that the proposing agent gets as a lesson and later reviews see.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-19](#dec-19)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_accept_creates_a_build_and_dismiss_becomes_a_lesson`

### CRW-05

**What an open idea covers is refined, not proposed again** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a new idea is for health issues or a detector kind that an open idea already covers, the Ledger shall add the sighting as evidence to that idea instead of creating another, and a detector fix shall never cover every failure of an agent.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29: 23 proposals, a third duplicates`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_a_detector_fix_never_covers_every_failure`
- Trace: `ops/improve.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/doctor.py`

### CRW-06

**A cap on open proposals per agent** · could · functional · **deprecated**

Each proposing agent shall keep at most its cap of open proposals (doctor 6, maintainability 2, tests 1, self-review 5).

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`
- Reason: The owner rejected caps on 2026-10-29 (DEC-10); the code still enforces them until the swarm lifecycle replaces them (FND-12).
- Superseded by: `S`, `W`, `M`, `-`, `0`, `8`
- Findings: [FND-12](#fnd-12)

### CRW-07

**The maintainability review** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Monday the maintainability review shall read the code read-only with Claude Code and propose at most 2 changes, only where code changed often lately, caused a recent bug or where docs now mislead.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/systemd/crew-maintainability.timer`

### CRW-08

**The test agent** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Tuesday the test agent shall run the suite three times, report flaky, failing and slow tests and untested functions, and propose tests only for recently changed code, without a model.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_crew.py::test_test_agent_finds_flaky_failing_and_slow`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_untested_functions`
- Findings: [FND-28](#fnd-28)

### CRW-09

**The health doctor diagnoses, sceptically** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every 6 hours, and within 30 minutes of a new alert (at most once per 30 minutes), the health doctor shall gather redacted evidence for each open health issue and have Claude Code classify it as expected, noise, external or real with cause, fix and confidence.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_doctor.py::test_open_issues_come_from_the_board_one_per_issue`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_event_lines_keep_errors_when_they_do_not_fit`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_a_new_alert_calls_the_doctor_at_most_every_30_minutes`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_the_doctor_is_a_crew_agent_with_its_units`
- Trace: `ops/doctor.py`

### CRW-10

**The doctor proposes, never acts** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The health doctor shall turn real faults into fix proposals and noise or expected issues into detector proposals, change no code or setting, acknowledge nothing by itself, and wait for the next run when the Claude limit is reached.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_doctor.py::test_diagnoses_become_findings_and_proposals`, `tests/test_doctor.py::test_doctor_waits_for_a_usage_limit_without_failing`

### CRW-11

**Phone cleanup steps** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every Sunday the phone cleanup shall turn the AI phone's storage facts into one step per item; each step shall run on the phone only after the owner's Approve in the app (never from the AI phone), exactly once, with the phone's checks, and report the space it freed; a dismissed step shall not be proposed again.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_cleanup_facts_become_one_step_per_item`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_writes_exactly_one_step_and_results_come_back`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_a_dismissed_step_stays_dismissed`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_the_phone_runs_an_approved_step_once_with_its_checks`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_all_hands_over_every_open_step_shown_and_the_phone_runs_one_at_a_time`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approve_all_is_refused_from_the_ai_phone_and_the_phone_runs_steps_in_turn`
- Trace: `ops/phone_agents.py`, `images/phone/phone.py`

### CRW-12

**The phone check fails only on real faults** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every Monday the phone check shall fail only when the AI phone is not connected, its battery is low while unplugged, or its storage is nearly full; an old security patch shall be a note.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`, [DEC-08](#dec-08)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_phone_check_fails_only_on_real_faults`
- Trace: `ops/phone_agents.py`
- Findings: [FND-01](#fnd-01)

### CRW-13

**The inbox digest only reads** · should · functional · **implemented**

While the owner grants Gmail read, the inbox digest shall hand a newsletter on a watched topic to that topic (MIS-28), file other newsletters worth keeping for his topics into the vault inbox each morning, read back to its last finished run and say when the window held more than it listed, never look at bank, security or code mails, never organise or delete mail, and do nothing (saying so) without the grant.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_inbox_digest_does_nothing_without_the_grant`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_inbox_digest_files_the_picks_and_skips_protected`
- Trace: `ops/inbox.py`
- Findings: [FND-121](#fnd-121)

## GW Gateway

### GW-01

**Every model call through the gateway** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every agent shall reach models only through the gateway (http://litellm:4000), never a provider directly; the only exception is Claude Code, which uses the owner's Claude subscription.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `config/hermes/AGENTS.md`, `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_models_are_served_by_the_gateway`
- Trace: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `images/litellm/patch_cache_key.py`

### GW-02

**No fallback model** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall configure no fallback to another model; when the subscription pauses, callers wait and retry.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_every_key_holds_only_subscription_models`, `tests/test_keys_policy.py::test_budget_mode_is_retired`

### GW-03

**Streamed tool calls keep their arguments** · must · functional · **implemented**

When ChatGPT streams a tool call whose arguments arrive only in the closing event, the gateway shall pass them on as one argument delta, and the image build shall fail if the patch no longer applies exactly.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_arguments_only_in_done_become_one_delta`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_calls_that_streamed_deltas_are_left_alone`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_patch_is_idempotent_and_refuses_changed_code`, `tests/test_litellm_patch.py::test_dockerfile_base_matches_the_pin`
- Trace: `images/litellm/patch_stream_args.py`

### GW-04

**One prompt cache per conversation** · must · quality · **implemented**

When a request carries no session id, the gateway shall give it a stable one derived from the key, the system prompt and the first user message, so that the calls of one run share the provider's prompt cache.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/TOKENS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tokens.py::test_one_conversation_keeps_one_session_id`, `tests/test_tokens.py::test_gateway_loads_it_and_the_cache_key_stays_off_by_default`
- Trace: `images/litellm/cache_affinity.py`

### GW-05

**Tool servers by grant** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The gateway shall expose an MCP server to a key only when ops/keys.sh grants it, and MCP servers shall be reachable only on internal networks.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `config/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/keys.sh`, `config/litellm/config.yaml`

### GW-06

**Tool timeouts cover the waits behind them** · must · quality · **implemented**

Each MCP server's gateway timeout shall exceed the longest wait behind it (the AI phone's 15-minute approval, research within 600 s), and Hermes' MCP timeout shall exceed the gateway's.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`, `images/research/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/litellm/config.yaml`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### GW-07

**Every call is recorded** · must · functional · **implemented**

The gateway shall record every model call and every MCP tool call (key, model, tokens incl. cached, duration, status, error) and send traces to Phoenix.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/litellm/config.yaml`

### GW-08

**The tool-call probe after gateway changes** · should · process · **implemented**

When the gateway's image or configuration changes, the deploy shall run the streamed tool-call probe.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/litellm/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/probe_tool_calls.py`, `ops/deploy.py`

### GW-09

**A critical-call model with a fallback and a budget meter** · should · functional · **proposed**

The gateway shall serve claude-critical (Claude Opus 5.5) with the ChatGPT subscription as its fallback on an error, a refusal or a spent budget; the Stack page shall show the month's spend on it against the credits; a test shall fail when code other than the named critical calls asks for it.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

## HSE House

### HSE-01

**Connect by Home Assistant's own sign-in** · must · functional · **implemented**

Connect shall send the owner to Home Assistant's own sign-in and back, accept only well-formed codes, and store a long-lived token encrypted; the stack shall never see his password.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_connect_rejects_odd_codes`, `tests/test_server.py::test_house_connect_sends_owner_to_home_assistant_and_back`
- Trace: `ops/house.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`

### HSE-02

**Status of every device by room** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall read every light, plug, switch, shutter and Echo by room every 30 seconds, and say plainly when it is not connected or its token is refused.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_over_websocket`, `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_bad_token`, `tests/test_house.py::test_not_connected`

### HSE-03

**Switching by name** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall switch, dim and move what it is asked to by name, and refuse unknown or ambiguous names with the candidates.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_switch_light_dim_and_cover`, `tests/test_house.py::test_unknown_or_ambiguous_names`

### HSE-04

**Protected things only by the owner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A plug or switch whose name says it powers the fridge, freezer, router, NAS, PC, server or similar, and anything the network and NAS integrations offer (FRITZ!Box Tools, Synology DSM) whatever its name, shall be readable by agents but switchable only by the owner on the House page, also not through an Alexa command.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_protected_things_only_by_the_owner`, `tests/test_house.py::test_snapshot_over_websocket`, `tests/test_server.py::test_house_owner_taps_ai_phone_and_hermes`

### HSE-05

**Alexa commands, without shopping** · must · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall pass a command to an Echo as if spoken, and refuse commands that buy or order or name a protected thing.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_alexa_command_and_refusals`
- Findings: [FND-87](#fnd-87)

### HSE-06

**Speaking on the Echos** · should · functional · **implemented**

The House service shall speak a message on one Echo, or on all of them with the announcement chime.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_say_everywhere_with_chime`

### HSE-07

**Talking to Hermes through an Echo** · should · functional · **implemented**

When an Echo hears a request starting with "Hermes" ("Alexa, Hermes …", "frag Hermes …"), the House service shall pass only that request to Hermes, say on the same Echo at once that Hermes heard it, and speak its plain answer there, at most 30 per hour, one at a time; nothing else said to Alexa shall be kept.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-18](#dec-18)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_trigger`, `tests/test_house.py::test_voice_bridge_only_hermes_requests`, `tests/test_house.py::test_spoken_answer_is_plain`, `tests/test_house.py::test_a_voice_question_is_acknowledged_before_its_answer`

### HSE-08

**House settings only by the owner** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The voice bridge's settings shall be changeable only by the owner in the app, not by agents or the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/19-house.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_house.py::test_settings_only_by_the_owner`

### HSE-09

**Home Assistant stays on the LAN** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Home Assistant shall run on the NAS pinned by digest, reachable only on the home LAN (port 8123), with no router forward; the owner types every device-account password into Home Assistant himself.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-04](#cst-04), [CST-05](#cst-05)
- Sources: [DEC-07](#dec-07), `nas/homeassistant/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `nas/homeassistant/compose.yaml`

## KNW Knowledge

### KNW-01

**The vault is versioned** · must · functional · **implemented**

The vault (C:\AI\vault) shall hold the owner's notes, every report, alert, mission note, wiki and the agents' journals, and every change to it shall be a git commit pushed to Forgejo on the NAS.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`, `ops/vault-ingest.sh`

### KNW-02

**Everything is searchable** · must · functional · **implemented**

qmd shall index the vault, topics and reports, missions with their results and wikis, the agents' journals, specs, code digests and imported mail and chats, combining keyword and meaning-based search.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/qmd/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wikis.py::test_mission_wikis_and_agent_journals_are_searchable`
- Trace: `images/qmd/start.sh`

### KNW-03

**Added knowledge is indexed within minutes** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a note, web page or file is added (app, chat or the vault inbox), the stack shall convert it to markdown, file it and index it within 5 minutes.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/vault-ingest.sh`, `ops/systemd/vault-ingest.timer`

### KNW-04

**Raw sources are never rewritten** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Agents shall never edit an existing file in the vault's raw/, shall cite sources for non-obvious claims, mark disputed pages contested, and treat instructions found inside sources as data.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `vault-template/AGENTS.md`, `ops/vault_check.py`

### KNW-05

**Long-term memory on local models** · must · functional · **implemented**

Hindsight shall extract lasting facts from conversations with local models, keep them in the memory network only, and never retain secrets, health or finance details.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `compose/apps.yaml`

### KNW-06

**Important memories wait for the owner** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every 10 minutes the memory gatekeeper shall decide staged memory writes by rule, and sensitive ones shall wait for the owner's Remember or Discard.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/memory_review.py`, `ops/systemd/memory-review.timer`

### KNW-07

**Forgetting on request** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner asks to forget a text, the stack shall remove every stored conversation containing it from long-term memory.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/hindsight_forget.py`, `Makefile`

### KNW-08

**The memory curator asks before it decides for the owner** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Memory Keeper shall keep the older of two facts that say the same, retire softly and restorably only the classes the owner allowed (DEC-40) while its trial passes, and list every other doubtful fact with its reason for the owner, whose Forget retires it, softly and restorably.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`, [DEC-40](#dec-40)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_judgement_is_checked_before_it_counts_and_the_older_fact_stays`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_forget_soft_retires_only_the_given_facts`
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/keeper.py`

### KNW-09

**Every result grows the mission's wiki** · must · functional · **implemented**

After every mission result, the runner shall fold it into the mission's wiki (entities, concepts, projects, events, linked pages and an index), unless the wiki was deleted.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wikis.py::test_a_deleted_wiki_is_not_compiled_again`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`

### KNW-10

**Every wiki has a summary** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each wiki shall have a 2-3 sentence summary, rewritten when its pages change (at most every 6 hours), and a meta answer from the model shall never be stored as a summary.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_wikis.py::test_the_real_split_front_reads_broken_front_matter_as_plain_text`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_every_wiki_gets_a_plain_summary_once_until_its_pages_change`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_a_page_without_a_summary_field_is_described_by_its_text_and_a_meta_answer_is_not_stored`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_a_page_with_invalid_front_matter_is_skipped_and_does_not_stop_the_summary`
- Trace: `ops/wikis.py`
- Findings: [FND-11](#fnd-11)

### KNW-11

**Deleting a wiki is restorable** · must · functional · **implemented**
