When the owner deletes a mission's wiki in the app, the runner shall remove it in one restorable vault commit and stop it growing back; the vault wiki and paths outside wikis shall not be deletable, and the delete shall be refused from the AI phone and from agents.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_every_action_a_button_sends_is_one_the_app_lets_through`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_delete_removes_a_mission_wiki_in_one_restorable_commit_and_stops_it_growing`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_the_vault_wiki_and_paths_outside_wikis_cannot_be_deleted`, `tests/test_wikis.py::test_wiki_delete_is_wired_for_the_app_only`
- Trace: `ops/wikis.py`

### KNW-12

**Browsing a wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall open on the list of every wiki, not on one picked for the owner; a wiki shall show its name, search, and its pages as one list per folder with each page's summary, with its graph and a tab with its summary, its agent and delete one tap away; an open page shall fill the screen with its links and backlinks (aliases resolved) and a way back to its wiki; and the app shall copy a page's original markdown only for pages that exist and are approved.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/09-wikis.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_wiki_links_backlinks_and_aliases`, `tests/test_server.py::test_wiki_copy_source_preserves_original_markdown`, `tests/test_server.py::test_wiki_copy_source_preserves_plain_and_empty_pages`, `tests/test_server.py::test_wiki_missing_or_unapproved_pages_do_not_expose_markdown`, `tests/test_server.py::test_wiki_copy_button_interactions`, `tests/test_app_layout.py::test_the_wiki_tab_opens_on_every_wiki_and_a_wiki_on_its_pages`

### KNW-13

**Search quality is measured before changes** · should · process · **implemented**

Before a change to qmd's collections or models, the retrieval evaluation (recall@8, nDCG@8, MRR) shall be run before and after and the results compared.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: `images/qmd/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `eval/retrieval.py`, `eval/qrels`

### KNW-14

**Mission wikis are curated** · could · functional · **implemented**

Every week a curation pass shall merge duplicate pages and mark contradictions in mission wikis, as the vault curator does for the vault wiki.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_merges_marks_and_lints_every_agent_wiki_and_changes_only_what_the_vault_restores`
- Trace: `ops/librarian.py`, `ops/wiki_rebuild.py`
- Rationale: Mission wikis were append-only (review item 5); the Librarian is that pass (MEM-08).
- Findings: [FND-44](#fnd-44)

### KNW-15

**Imports of mail and chat exports** · could · functional · **implemented**

make ingest-exports shall convert mail (mbox, eml) and chat exports (ChatGPT, Claude) offline into one markdown file per item and index them.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `converters/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `converters/bulk_convert.py`, `Makefile`
- Gap: Built; no export has been provided yet.

## MEM Memory

### MEM-01

**Every memory is in one inventory** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall keep one inventory of every memory store (vault notes, vault wiki, agent wikis, journals, mission notes, long-term memory, the Ledger, skills, run records and the search index), refreshed every hour without delaying the runner's state, with one snapshot a day for 120 days.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_every_memory_store_is_declared_with_its_kind_writers_readers_limit_and_curator`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_a_store_that_cannot_be_measured_says_why_and_the_runner_never_waits`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `ops/missions.py`

### MEM-02

**Each memory has one kind and one home** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each memory store shall be declared with its kind (episodic, semantic, procedural, shared work or search), what it holds, who writes it, who reads it, its limit and its curator.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_every_memory_store_is_declared_with_its_kind_writers_readers_limit_and_curator`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`

### MEM-03

**One writer per wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each wiki shall be a Ledger artifact owned by its one writer (the curator for the vault wiki, the desk for a briefing's topic wiki, the mission card for a mission's), and another agent's contribution shall reach it as a help on that artifact (SWM-109) that the writer's next compile folds in, naming the helper, and marks as folded. The Librarian's tidy (MEM-08) is the one other change to a wiki, and it adds no facts (DEC-32).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_one_writer_per_wiki_folds_in_the_help_other_agents_gave_it`
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/wikis.py`

### MEM-04

**Each agent reads the wikis its card names** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each agent's card shall name the wikis it reads (by default its own, the vault wiki and wikis on related subjects), a run's start shall search only those and its own wiki first of all, and a wiki built from the owner's personal mail (a mission that holds a Gmail grant) shall reach only its own mission. A topic's newsletters (MIS-28) are bulk mail handed through its report and do not make its wiki private (FND-121).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_run_reads_the_wikis_its_card_names_its_own_first_and_never_a_private_one`
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`, `ops/registry.py`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/missions.py`
- Findings: [FND-45](#fnd-45), [FND-121](#fnd-121)

### MEM-05

**One opening per run** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every run's opening shall be assembled in one place in a fixed order within one budget (card instructions, the owner's messages, its own state, its knowledge, the owner's facts, the team), with the swarm's pack fetched once by the runner and the agent told not to call swarm_context again, and a mission's working state shall be a section of its journal that each run replaces, not a second file.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_run_s_opening_has_one_order_one_budget_and_the_swarm_pack_once`, `tests/test_memory_coherent.py::test_a_mission_s_working_state_lives_in_its_journal`
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/missions.py`
- Findings: [FND-42](#fnd-42)

### MEM-06

**Personal facts in long-term memory, world knowledge in the wikis** · should · functional · **implemented**

Long-term memory (Hindsight) shall hold the owner's own facts (preferences, decisions, people), the wikis shall hold sourced knowledge of the world, and an agent's journal entry shall not be copied into long-term memory.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_journal.py::test_entries_stay_out_of_long_term_memory_and_the_opening_recalls_the_owner_s_facts`
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`
- Findings: [FND-42](#fnd-42)

### MEM-07

**Memory is observable** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Stack page shall show every memory store with its kind, what it holds, who writes and reads it, its size, its growth over 7 days, how full it is against its limit, who curates it and when that last ran, and its problems.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_inventory_measures_each_store_names_its_problems_and_reads_growth_from_daily_snapshots`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_app_draws_the_map_the_access_and_the_memory_section`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### MEM-08

**Every memory has a limit and a curator** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every memory store shall have a limit and a curator, or a stated rule that keeps it small. The Librarian (the memory curator's weekly pass) shall lint every agent wiki, merge duplicate pages keeping every link of both (a merge that would lose or add one is refused), mark contradictions only with sources already on the pages, write the hub pages of the subjects two or more wikis share in the vault wiki, and run qmd cleanup on the search index, changing on its own only what the vault's history restores (one commit per pass, of exactly the files it wrote) and leaving forgetting to the owner (KNW-08). A store over its limit for 8 days shall raise an alert on its curator's health item.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_inventory_measures_each_store_names_its_problems_and_reads_growth_from_daily_snapshots`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_merges_marks_and_lints_every_agent_wiki_and_changes_only_what_the_vault_restores`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_merge_that_would_lose_a_link_is_refused_and_a_page_changed_meanwhile_is_left`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_what_a_pass_wrote_before_a_step_failed_is_still_in_its_commit_and_nothing_else_is`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_pair_judged_two_subjects_is_not_asked_again_until_one_of_its_pages_changes`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_search_index_cleanup_is_measured_before_and_after`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_memory_over_its_limit_for_eight_days_alerts_its_curator`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `ops/librarian.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/vault_check.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-43](#fnd-43), [FND-44](#fnd-44)

### MEM-09

**The wikis' map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall open with a map of every wiki of the vault and of a mission that is not archived (one bubble per wiki, sized by its pages, its name below, above or beside it where it covers no other name or bubble) and a line between two wikis for the subjects both have (a page with the same title or alias), each subject named in full on a tap; a tap on a bubble shall open its wiki (FND-83); an archived mission's wiki shall stay readable in a fold below the list.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_wiki_map_has_every_wiki_and_the_subjects_they_share`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_an_archived_mission_s_wiki_is_listed_last_and_left_off_the_map`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_map_s_names_cover_no_other_name_or_bubble_and_stay_in_the_box`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_app_draws_the_map_the_access_and_the_memory_section`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_and_the_map_share_one_rule_for_a_subject`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `ops/librarian.py`
- Findings: [FND-61](#fnd-61), [FND-83](#fnd-83)

### MEM-10

**Who reads and writes which wiki** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Wiki tab shall show for each wiki which agents read it and wrote it in the last 7 days, counted in runs, from the run record (a run's start brief, its vault searches, its file reads and writes, and every page a result compiles into a wiki).

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_who_read_and_wrote_which_wiki_comes_from_the_run_record`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_app_draws_the_map_the_access_and_the_memory_section`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### MEM-11

**Prompts are versioned files on the agent cards** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every agent prompt of ops/ and the Hermes configure scripts shall live in prompts/<owner>.md, one file per agent named by its card's prompts field (or by a service, or shared.md for templates more than one card uses). Each prompt shall carry a version (the sha of its text) that every send records in the run's record, with the file's version in mission and crew manifests and in the card hash. A prompt shall change only after a replay passed, which runs its last cases with the last approved text and the new one on the agent's own model and needs valid answers as often and a judge that prefers the old answer no more often. The build gate (ops/check.sh) shall refuse a prompt text with neither its first-record baseline nor a passed replay.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-33](#dec-33)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_prompts.py::test_no_agent_prompt_is_left_in_ops_or_a_configure_script`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_every_prompt_loads_from_its_file_with_its_version_and_keeps_it_through_format`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_send_records_the_ref_and_a_live_call_keeps_its_case`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_build_gate_refuses_a_changed_prompt_until_its_replay_passed`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_replay_the_judge_prefers_the_old_text_on_fails_and_the_gate_stays_shut`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_an_agent_s_standing_brief_is_replayed_on_the_requests_its_cases_bring`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_a_filled_prompt_that_drives_tools_is_replayed_as_a_dry_run`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_deploy_hands_each_configure_script_its_soul_text_from_the_prompts_file`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_card_names_its_own_prompts_file_and_every_prompts_file_has_an_owner`
- Trace: `ops/prompts.py`, `prompts/AGENTS.md`, `ops/registry.py`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/check.sh`, `ops/missions.py`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`

### MEM-13

**The desk's and the scout's prompts are files too** · could · functional · **implemented**

The Topic Desk's prompts (images/desk/desk.py), the research scout's brief and the research privacy check (images/research/server.py) shall live in prompts/desk.md, prompts/scout.md and prompts/guard.md under the same replay gate as every other prompt. Their containers shall read prompts/ through a read-only mount, log each prompt's version, and the desk shall hand its calls back as replay cases; a research call whose prompt file cannot be read shall be refused.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-33](#dec-33)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_prompts.py::test_no_agent_prompt_is_left_in_ops_or_a_configure_script`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_every_image_has_a_tag_the_deploy_rebuilds_also_one_run_from_an_ops_script`, `tests/test_prompts.py::test_the_desk_and_the_scout_read_their_prompts_from_the_mount_and_the_desk_hands_back_its_cases`
- Trace: `images/desk/desk.py`, `images/research/server.py`, `ops/desk.sh`, `ops/deploy.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `prompts/desk.md`, `prompts/scout.md`, `prompts/guard.md`
- Findings: [FND-46](#fnd-46)

### MEM-12

**A changed fact is superseded, not overwritten** · could · functional · **implemented**

When a compile changes a fact on an agent wiki's page, the old fact shall be kept under "Superseded" with the date it stopped holding, and a line once under Superseded shall never be dropped; a page whose Contradictions section remains shall stay contested; and the Librarian shall mark an agent wiki's page not confirmed for 90 days as stale (a later compile that confirms it clears the mark). Long-term facts are never overwritten by an agent; the owner retires them (KNW-08).

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_compile_keeps_superseded_lines_and_a_contested_page_and_skips_merged_stubs`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_the_librarian_merges_marks_and_lints_every_agent_wiki_and_changes_only_what_the_vault_restores`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/librarian.py`

### MEM-14

**A compile sees what it rewrites** · must · functional · **implemented**

A wiki compile shall show the model each page it may rewrite whole up to 12,000 characters (past that its start, with the cut marked), and every list line of the old page that the model was not shown shall stay on the page under its own section.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_compile_sees_a_long_page_whole_and_keeps_the_lines_it_was_not_shown`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`
- Findings: [FND-103](#fnd-103), [FND-110](#fnd-110)

### MEM-15

**Hand-offs by reference past a budget** · should · functional · **implemented**

A task step shall get each earlier result it builds on whole while they fit a budget together, and past it each result's marked start, its size and where the whole result is (a file in an OpenCode step's work folder, a vault file for a Hermes step; a Claude or build step takes them whole apart from its brief), and a prompt too long for one command-line argument shall be refused with its size.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_result_too_long_for_a_prompt_goes_by_reference_through_every_adapter`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`
- Rationale: FND-99 moved the results out of the builder's brief, but every step still gets them copied whole; a Hermes step takes its prompt as one argument, which Linux refuses past 128 KiB. A size contract test pushes a 150,000-character result through every adapter.
- Findings: [FND-104](#fnd-104)

### MEM-16

**A cut a model reads shows itself** · should · quality · **implemented**

Every cut of text going to a model (cap reason prompt) shall carry the textcap marker with how much is missing, and ops/truncation.py shall refuse a prompt cap without it.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`, `docs/TRUNCATION-AUDIT.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_truncation.py::test_every_cap_names_its_reason_and_an_unexplained_one_fails_the_check`
- Trace: `ops/truncation.py`, `ops/textcap.py`
- Rationale: 92 of 96 prompt caps sliced without a mark; FND-100 and FND-103 showed that a model takes an unmarked cut for the whole text. All 98 prompt caps are marked since the audit of 2 November.
- Findings: [FND-105](#fnd-105)

### MEM-17

**A contradiction is worked to an end** · should · functional · **implemented**

A contradiction the Librarian marks shall become a Ledger item owned by its wiki's writer, the writer's next compile shall be told of it, a mark open for 14 days shall start a refinement task (TSK-34) that researches it, and a settled claim shall move under Superseded with the item closed.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-32](#dec-32), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_a_contradiction_on_a_wiki_becomes_a_dispute_a_task_researches_and_its_settling_closes`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_a_dispute_its_research_could_not_settle_goes_to_the_owner`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_compile_is_told_of_the_disputes_on_the_pages_it_rewrites`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Findings: [FND-106](#fnd-106)

### MEM-18

**Long-term memory holds each fact once** · should · functional · **implemented**

Long-term memory shall keep one fact for one statement of the owner (a repeat confirms it, it is not added again), in English, with no facts on agents' runs, and a fact on a passing state shall carry the date it held; its retain mission shall ask for this at the source and the Memory Keeper (MEM-21) shall fix what still gets in.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-04](#sn-04)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_judgement_is_checked_before_it_counts_and_the_older_fact_stays`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_each_change_is_logged_with_what_was_there_and_undone`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`
- Rationale: Measured 2 Nov: the same voice preference up to five times, facts in Spanish and German, about 35 facts on agent runs, and 55 forget suggestions waiting since 30 October (KNW-08 retires only on the owner's tap; decision D2 in docs/MEMORY.md). The owner decided D2 on 2 Nov (DEC-40).
- Findings: [FND-107](#fnd-107)

### MEM-19

**The inventory has every store** · should · functional · **implemented**

The memory inventory (MEM-01) shall also declare a task's hand-offs (the steps' results), Hermes' own memory (USER.md), the code graphs and the chats, each with its kind, writer, reader, limit and curator.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_memory.py::test_every_memory_store_is_declared_with_its_kind_writers_readers_limit_and_curator`, `tests/test_memory.py::test_the_inventory_measures_each_store_names_its_problems_and_reads_growth_from_daily_snapshots`
- Trace: `ops/memory.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-108](#fnd-108)

### MEM-20

**A memory trial** · should · process · **implemented**

The stack shall have a trial with an answer key for the Memory Keeper's judgement (eval/trials/memory.yaml) that plants faults among true facts and scores the changes it would make itself (precision, a true fact touched counts against it) and the faults it finds (recall), and contradictions of the owner's facts shall count only as questions; its last three runs on the current prompt shall gate the Keeper's automatic changes.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/MEMORY.md`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_trial_scores_the_automatic_changes_against_the_answer_key`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_nothing_is_changed_while_the_trial_has_not_passed_three_times_on_the_current_prompt`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `eval/trials/memory.yaml`

### MEM-21

**One Memory Keeper, by rules** · must · functional · **implemented**

One agent, the Memory Keeper, shall look after every memory store every night (its judgement parts on Wednesdays) by these rules: it deletes nothing (a fact is soft-retired, rewritten with its old text kept, or moved under Superseded); it writes every change with what was there, why and how to undo it, and the owner can undo each from its page; it corrects world knowledge only through a research task's sourced findings; it makes no automatic change to the owner's facts beyond retiring duplicates, test noise and agents' runs, translating into English and dating passing states, and asks him when two of his facts disagree; it makes those changes only while its trial passes (MEM-20) and at most 40 a pass; and it says once a week what it changed.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-09](#sn-09), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-31](#dec-31), [DEC-40](#dec-40), `docs/MEMORY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_keeper.py::test_each_change_is_logged_with_what_was_there_and_undone`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_nothing_is_changed_while_the_trial_has_not_passed_three_times_on_the_current_prompt`, `tests/test_keeper.py::test_the_crew_saves_the_keeper_s_report_and_undoes_a_change_on_the_owner_s_tap`
- Trace: `ops/keeper.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/systemd/crew-memory.timer`, `prompts/memory.md`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/guide/08-knowledge.md`

## MIS Missions

### MIS-01

**A mission is a file in the vault** · must · interface · **implemented**

Each mission shall be vault/missions/<slug>/MISSION.md with its type (briefing or task), status (active, paused, archived), schedule, triggers, depth, one-off flag and delivery channels, versioned with the vault.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_task_writes_mission_and_queues_first_run`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-02

**Schedules** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall accept the schedules daily HH:MM, weekdays HH:MM, weekly <days> HH:MM, every Nh (1 to 24) and manual, refuse any other text, and compute each mission's next run from it.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_valid_schedule`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_due_times_and_next`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_schedule_text`

### MIS-03

**A missed run catches up once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the PC was off at a scheduled time, the runner shall run the mission once when it is back (within 20 hours), never for a time before the mission was created, and not after a resume.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_check_schedules_catches_up_once_and_not_before_created`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_update_validates_and_resume_skips_catch_up`

### MIS-04

**One run at a time** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall run missions one at a time from a queue, drop a duplicate queued run of the same mission but always queue event-triggered runs, and let a run that parks on a wait free the slot until it times out.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_enqueue_dedup_but_events_always_queue`, `tests/test_queue.py::test_a_parked_run_frees_the_slot`, `tests/test_queue.py::test_park_times_out`
- Findings: [FND-29](#fnd-29)

### MIS-05

**Usage-limit pause** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a run fails because the subscription's usage limit is reached, the runner shall mark it waiting and retry it every 30 minutes, up to 16 times (8 hours), and never mark it failed for that reason.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), `ops/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_paused`
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-06

**Creating a mission** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a mission is created by chat or form, the runner shall validate it, write its MISSION.md, give it a unique ASCII slug and queue its first run.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_task_writes_mission_and_queues_first_run`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_slugify_is_unique_and_ascii`

### MIS-07

**No duplicate missions from retries** · must · functional · **implemented**

If a mission with a similar title (word overlap at least 0.4) was created in the last 30 minutes, then the runner shall refuse the new one as a duplicate.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-27: Hermes retried a slow create and made a duplicate mission`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_create_refuses_a_duplicate_within_30_minutes`

### MIS-08

**Changing, pausing and archiving** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall let the owner or Hermes run, pause, resume, archive and update a mission (goal, questions, sources, schedule, delivery, depth), validating every field and recording the change in the mission's activity.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_update_validates_and_resume_skips_catch_up`

### MIS-09

**Event triggers fire once** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall check each mission's triggers (SEC filing, price move, news) every 10 minutes, take the first check as a baseline, and run the mission once for each new event.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/05-triggers.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_triggers.py::test_clean_triggers`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_clean_triggers_rejects`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_check_events_baseline_then_fire`
- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`

### MIS-10

**Triggers proven end to end** · should · quality · **implemented**

Each trigger kind shall have fired at least once end to end, or been replayed from a recorded event, with its alert delivered.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`
- Verified by: demonstration: `ops/conductor.py`, `eval/results/20261101-020317-trigger-proof.json`

### MIS-11

**Briefings by the Topic Desk** · must · functional · **implemented**

A briefing run shall have the Topic Desk collect the brief's news, filings and prices over the window its schedule gives (36 hours for a daily briefing, the time between runs plus 12 hours for a weekly one), search Google News in each query's own language, write a structured report with sources, file it under topics/<slug>/reports/YYYY/MM/, skip filing boilerplate, and commit it to the vault.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/desk/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_desk.py::test_release_text_skips_filing_boilerplate`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_section_parsing`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_briefing_reads_the_news_of_its_schedule_s_window`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_google_news_searches_in_the_query_s_language_over_the_brief_s_window`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_a_weekly_brief_keeps_a_week_of_news_and_a_daily_one_36_hours`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_the_price_note_names_proxies_only_when_a_row_is_one`
- Trace: `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/desk.sh`, `ops/missions.py`
- Findings: [FND-77](#fnd-77), [FND-80](#fnd-80)

### MIS-12

**German report clean-up** · could · functional · **deprecated**

Where a brief's language is German, the desk shall normalise dates, number formats and units to German conventions.

- Parents: [SN-12](#sn-12)
- Sources: [DEC-02](#dec-02)
- Verified by: inspection: `images/desk/desk.py`
- Reason: DEC-02 made every report English; the last German topic was archived and the clean-up removed on 2026-10-30 (FND-21).
- Findings: [FND-21](#fnd-21)

### MIS-13

**Long-term tasks keep notes** · must · functional · **implemented**

A task run shall run as Hermes' mission profile on its own key, write its log under missions/<slug>/log/ and keep its working state between runs in its journal (MEM-05).

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/missions.py`, `config/hermes/configure-mission.sh`

### MIS-14

**One-off missions** · should · functional · **implemented**

When a mission is created as one-off, the runner shall run it once, deliver its result, and archive it.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-15

**Delivery channels** · must · functional · **implemented**

The runner shall deliver a mission's results on each of its channels (app push, Telegram with buttons into the app, designed HTML e-mail) after every finished run, except that a briefing the desk chose no news for shall stay in the app, and the next full delivery by Telegram and e-mail shall name the earlier days that found nothing; every e-mail sent or failed shall be recorded in the notification log and the run's timeline.

- Parents: [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/07-delivery.md`

- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/missions.py`

### MIS-16

**Cost per run** · must · functional · **partial**

The runner shall attribute calls, tokens and spend to each run from the gateway's records of the run's key within the run's time window (UTC).

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: `docs/MISSION-CONTROL.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runner.py::test_spend_window_query_window_is_utc`
- Gap: The attribution assumes one run per key at a time, but vk-mission is also used by background jobs during task runs (FND-03).
- Findings: [FND-03](#fnd-03), [FND-25](#fnd-25)
- Refined by: [SWM-81](#swm-81)

### MIS-17

**Cleanup analysis never deletes** · should · functional · **implemented**

A cleanup analysis mission (AI phone or PC) shall report what takes the space, rank unused apps and old data, label PC clutter safe, caution or never, list blocked apps only, show every step in the run's log, and delete nothing.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_phone_report_ranks_unused_apps_and_skips_blocked`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_pc_report_labels_and_summary`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_target_is_guessed_from_the_words`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_run_steps_reach_the_timeline`
- Trace: `ops/cleanup.py`, `ops/windows/disk-scan.ps1`

### MIS-18

**Each agent reads its memory at the start** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a run starts, the runner shall put the agent's opening (MEM-05) in front of the run's first prompt, once, within 12,000 characters, its own journal part (working state, standing notes, its last 5 entries) giving way first from its oldest entries.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`

### MIS-19

**Each agent writes its memory at the end** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a run ends, the runner shall condense its log into one journal entry (what it did, found, what failed and why, open questions), with gpt-sub-fast or deterministically when the model is out, and keep it in the journal only (MEM-06).

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/04-missions.md`

### MIS-20

**The owner can edit an agent's memory** · should · functional · **implemented**

The owner shall be able to add, edit and forget journal lines on the agent's Memory tab, every change being a commit of only that agent's files, and the weekly curator shall fold entries older than the last 8 into standing notes and move them to ARCHIVE.md.

A job hunt shall search the Bundesagentur für Arbeit's job board for current openings by job title or skill, place and radius, and read an opening's full ad by its reference number; the research scout shall have these as tools on its isolated web container, and a job-hunt briefing shall carry such searches as a source and report each opening once.

### MIS-22

**A briefing is rated by what the owner did with it** · should · functional · **proposed**

Each briefing shall offer Acted on it, Useful and Noise in the mail, the app and Telegram; its page shall show the share acted on or useful; an item kind rated noise three times is dropped and a briefing rated noise three weeks running asks whether to stop.

### MIS-23

**Results shows what you asked for, first** · must · functional · **implemented**

A mission's Results shall begin with the files the owner asked it to make. Each file shall show its name, words, checkboxes and size, whether it is final or a draft, how it was delivered, and Open, Download and Copy. A file that was asked for and not saved shall be shown as asked for, not delivered, with the reason and a way to run again. Drafts its runs left in the Ledger shall follow, then the run reports. The mission header, Home's result card and the chat card shall show the files too. Files shall live outside the vault's git, so that deleting one frees its space.

- Parents: [MIS-15](#mis-15), [SN-01](#sn-01), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-03: Why does it not show the actual result of the mission in results? Results should always also show the results right? ... the mission files should be shown in the results section too or a dedicated files section`

- Refined by: [MIS-24](#mis-24), [MIS-25](#mis-25), [MIS-26](#mis-26)

### MIS-24

**The mission saves the files it was asked for** · must · functional · **implemented**

The files the owner asks for shall be recorded with the mission when it is made, and can be changed in its Settings or by Hermes. The mission's agent shall be told them and shall save each one with file_save, either as text or from a Ledger report unchanged. The tool is on its own server, given only to the mission agent. Saving a file again shall keep the earlier version.

- Refined by: [MIS-27](#mis-27)

### MIS-25

**Files arrive as real attachments; a missing one makes the run partial** · must · functional · **implemented**

A run's delivery shall attach the files it saved: to the e-mail as attachments under their own names, and on Telegram as documents after the result's message. Each file shall record its own delivery. A run that ends without a file that was asked for shall be marked partial, and its notification and e-mail shall name the missing file.

- Trace: `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/notify.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/files.py`

### MIS-26

**Mission files are cleaned up by rules the owner sees** · must · functional · **implemented**

Deleting a file shall move it, with its versions, to a trash it can be restored from for 30 days. Every day a cleanup shall: - keep only the newest 5 versions of each file; - move to the trash the unpinned files of missions archived and untouched for 180 days; - empty what has been in the trash for 30 days. A Files page shall show every mission's files, the space they take against a limit, the trash and these rules, and offer Clean up now, which first shows what it would do. Past 80 % of the limit the owner shall get a notice.

- Parents: [MIS-23](#mis-23), [PLT-21](#plt-21)
- Sources: `docs/DELIVERABLES.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_files.py::test_delete_goes_to_the_trash_and_restores`, `tests/test_files.py::test_the_cleanup_follows_its_rules_and_a_dry_run_changes_nothing`
- Trace: `ops/files.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### MIS-27

**Hermes promises only what the stack can deliver** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner asks for a file the stack cannot make (Word, PDF, images, archives), Hermes shall say so in the chat and offer Markdown before any mission is made. mission_create shall refuse such a file. The tools' descriptions shall say what can be made and how it is delivered.

- Parents: [MIS-24](#mis-24), [AST-02](#ast-02)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-03: Check this mission i asked it to send me a md attachment ... Why does it not follow my instructions?`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_files.py::test_hermes_says_at_once_what_the_stack_cannot_deliver`, `tests/test_files.py::test_what_the_stack_cannot_make_is_refused_with_the_reason`, `tests/test_files.py::test_the_app_and_the_runner_agree_on_what_a_file_can_be`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/guide.js`

### MIS-28

**A newsletter on a watched topic reaches that topic** · should · functional · **implemented**

While the owner grants Gmail read, a newsletter that names an active briefing's topic (the watchlist and searches of its TOPIC.md) and passes one check against its key questions shall go to that topic's folder and be read by its next report as untrusted data, with the mail named as a source line; through the report it reaches the topic's wiki. A mail without a List-Unsubscribe header, a protected mail and the stack's own briefings shall never go there, no link in a mail shall be opened, and a mail's text shall never decide what the desk fetches. The topic's page shall list each mail with what came of it: waiting for the next report, cited in a report, or read and not used.

- Parents: [SN-10](#sn-10), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-04: does everything in my uranium newsletter reach the wiki and make the AI smarter?`, [FND-121](#fnd-121)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_newsletter_on_a_watched_topic_goes_to_that_topic_as_untrusted_data`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_only_a_list_s_mail_goes_to_a_topic_never_a_person_s_or_the_stack_s_own_or_a_protected_one`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_mail_the_check_leaves_out_stays_out_and_says_why`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_usage_limit_leaves_the_mails_for_the_next_run`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_the_listing_reaches_back_to_the_last_finished_run_and_says_when_it_held_more`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_topic_s_names_come_from_its_brief_and_short_capitals_match_exactly`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_long_mail_is_narrowed_around_the_names_and_every_gap_is_marked`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_the_next_report_reads_a_handed_mail_as_data_and_names_it_as_a_source`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_a_filed_report_settles_its_mails_so_they_are_read_once`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_the_topic_s_page_lists_each_mail_and_what_came_of_it`, `tests/test_topic_mail.py::test_the_e_mail_of_a_report_drops_the_link_only_the_app_can_open`
- Trace: `ops/inbox.py`, `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/desk.sh`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/provenance.py`, `prompts/inbox.md`, `prompts/desk.md`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-121](#fnd-121)

## OBS Observability

### OBS-01

**One record for every agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent run (Hermes profiles, Claude Code, the AI phone, Gmail, missions, builds, Claude tasks, crew jobs) shall report its events into one runtime record, open on its first event and stay closed after its end.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05), `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_run_opens_on_first_event_and_stays_closed_after_its_end`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_requests_that_come_together_each_read_their_own_runs`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_chat_sessions_are_tied_to_their_chat`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_hermes_runs_without_a_ref_are_tied_to_the_live_mission`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `ops/runtime.py`, `config/hermes/plugins/aistack-runtime/__init__.py`, `config/hermes/plugins/aistack-runtime/plugin.yaml`, `config/hermes/install-runtime-plugin.sh`, `config/hermes/claude-hooks/claude-runtime-settings.json`
- Findings: [FND-88](#fnd-88)

### OBS-02

**One honest end state** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall end in exactly one of done, failed, stopped, stalled or limit; an unknown end state shall become done and a malformed event shall be skipped.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_unknown_end_states_become_done_and_bad_events_are_skipped`

### OBS-03

**Silent runs are closed as stalled** · must · functional · **implemented**

The watchdog shall close a run that is silent for 20 minutes as stalled (3 hours for a declared wait), keep a parent alive while its children work, and let a late real end replace the watchdog's verdict.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_watchdog_closes_silent_runs_honestly`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_declared_wait_is_not_a_stall`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_children_show_under_their_parent_and_keep_it_alive`

### OBS-04

**Observers never block** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Hermes runtime plugin, the Claude hooks and the phone and mail emitters shall never block, veto or fail the action they observe, and shall spool events while Mission Control is unreachable.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `config/hermes/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_plugin_records_a_turn_and_spools_without_mission_control`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_claude_hook_turns_hooks_and_transcript_into_events`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_forwarder_sends_spools_and_keeps_what_failed`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_the_mail_tools_survive_a_log_they_cannot_write`

### OBS-05

**What it does and what it touches** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every run's view shall show its steps in order, and what it read, changed, was refused and saw only partly, with the tool that did it, across both engines.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_files_read_and_changed_across_engines`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_touches_refusals_connections_and_retention`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_pipeline_steps_reads_writes_and_model_calls_go_to_the_current_run`
- Refined by: [SWM-78](#swm-78)

### OBS-06

**Statistics and stages** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every run shall show per-tool calls, errors and durations, model latency (p50, p95) and cache share, and its stages in one schema.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_stats_pair_calls_with_results`, `tests/test_observe.py::test_stages_use_one_schema`, `tests/test_telemetry.py::test_timeline_merges_sources_and_warns`

### OBS-07

**Problems by fixed rules** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each finished run shall be checked by deterministic rules (unknown tools, empty arguments to a tool that needs arguments, repeated calls, tool errors, results cut off and not read on before the answer, no clean end, usage-limit stalls) without a second model; the list of tools whose arguments are all optional shall match every tool in the repo, and the deploy's smoke-test refusals shall not count as problems.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_problems_what_it_touches_and_skills`, `tests/test_observe.py::test_the_deploy_smoke_test_refusals_are_expected_not_problems`, `tests/test_health_signals.py::test_the_no_argument_list_matches_every_tool_that_takes_none`, `tests/test_health_signals.py::test_a_call_with_no_arguments_is_a_problem_only_when_the_tool_needs_some`, `tests/test_health_signals.py::test_a_cut_off_result_the_agent_read_on_is_no_partial_read`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/obs_core.py`, `ops/telemetry.py`
- Findings: [FND-62](#fnd-62)

### OBS-08

**Drift since the last run** · should · functional · **implemented**

A run's first event shall carry a manifest (agent, profile, model, tools), and the view shall name what changed against the previous run of the same agent, without stopping the run.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_drift_against_the_previous_run_of_the_same_agent`, `tests/test_observe.py::test_manifest_diff_names_what_changed`
- Refined by: [SWM-25](#swm-25)

### OBS-09

**The agent log** · should · interface · **implemented**

Every agent's log shall use one set of kinds (START, READ, WRITE, TOOL, WEB, MAIL, PHONE, THINK, SAYS, WAIT, PROPOSE, ERROR, END) drawn by one renderer, live and afterwards.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/web/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_journal.py::test_the_log_kinds_are_the_designed_ones`

### OBS-10

**The health board** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Agents tab shall keep one health item per agent (missions, crew jobs, engines), raising alerts for failing streaks (naming the last run's reason), missed schedules, long usage-limit blocks, stalls and lost tools and notes for single failures and anomalies, rolling sessions up into the agent that started them and clearing each kind of issue with the agent's next finished run that does not have it.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_health_flags_streaks_overdue_slow_and_limits`, `tests/test_health.py::test_sessions_and_scouts_roll_up_and_a_good_run_clears`, `tests/test_health.py::test_engines_get_one_item_and_only_alerts_are_sent`, `tests/test_health.py::test_a_kind_of_issue_clears_with_the_next_run_without_it_whatever_notes_it_has`, `tests/test_health_signals.py::test_a_failing_job_s_alert_says_why`
- Findings: [FND-62](#fnd-62), [FND-64](#fnd-64), [FND-65](#fnd-65)

### OBS-11

**Told once** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall send each new alert once by push and Telegram, again after a day if it is still open, and never send notes. An alert that leaves the board for less than 6 hours shall count as the same alert when it comes back (a deploy hides the run record for a moment), and Hermes shall send no restart notices of its own.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_observe.py::test_notify_sends_each_issue_once_and_a_flicker_is_not_a_new_issue`, `tests/test_deploy.py::test_the_runner_restarts_for_any_module_it_may_import_also_one_it_reaches_indirectly`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/obs_core.py`, `config/hermes/configure.sh`, `ops/deploy.py`
- Findings: [FND-47](#fnd-47), [FND-49](#fnd-49)

### OBS-12

**Acknowledge hides until it happens again** · should · functional · **implemented**

When the owner acknowledges an issue, the board shall hide it until it happens again and record the acknowledgement in the activity feed.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_health.py::test_acknowledge_hides_until_it_happens_again`

### OBS-13

**Agents can read the health board** · should · interface · **implemented**

The open health issues, each with the run that shows it, shall be readable for agents through the API and the health_issues tool.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/18-crew.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_health.py::test_the_health_doctor_can_read_open_issues`

### OBS-14

**From a model call to its trace** · could · functional · **implemented**

Every model call shown in a log shall open its Phoenix trace.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_phoenix_picks_the_litellm_span_of_a_model_call`
- Refined by: [SWM-79](#swm-79)

### OBS-15

**Results per agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each agent's page shall show, under Results, what it delivered first (each result, newest first, opening it; for a mission run as a task, a link to the task's runs) and then what it took (runs, success, time, model calls, tokens, cost, wiki pages; for proposing agents the proposals and their fate).

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05), `chat 2026-11-01: Presenter ... show in that useless Payoff tab?`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/crew.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### OBS-16

**One notification policy** · should · quality · **implemented**

Every notification the stack sends (push, Telegram) shall go through one path that applies the same once-per-issue rule, the owner's preferences per kind of message and channel, and quiet hours in which only approvals go out and Telegram messages wait to be sent as one when the quiet hours end; each decision, and each report e-mail sent or failed, shall be logged and shown in the app.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `docs/REVIEW-2026-10-27.md`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_notify.py::test_preferences_decide_per_category_and_approvals_always_go_out`, `tests/test_notify.py::test_quiet_hours_hold_telegram_and_send_it_as_one_message_afterwards`, `tests/test_notify.py::test_the_same_key_goes_out_once_a_day_and_every_decision_is_logged`, `tests/test_notify.py::test_a_push_from_the_activity_feed_follows_the_preferences`, `tests/test_notify.py::test_nothing_but_the_notification_path_talks_to_telegram`, `tests/test_quiet_runs.py::test_every_email_outcome_is_recorded`
- Trace: `ops/notify.py`, `ops/notify.sh`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`
- Findings: [FND-17](#fnd-17), [FND-70](#fnd-70), [FND-78](#fnd-78)
- Refined by: [SWM-59](#swm-59)

### OBS-17

**How much each subscription carries** · should · quality · **implemented**

The Stack page shall show, for ChatGPT and Claude, the tokens and calls of the last 5 hours (ChatGPT's usage window) and 24 hours, the agents that used most, and whether the subscription is at its usage limit now and until when, so that the owner sees a limit coming before work waits for it.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-01: Do we have data we are missing out to report in mission control in some way that user would care about?`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_task_page.py::test_each_subscription_s_load_in_its_window_and_who_used_it`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Rationale: Both subscriptions are the stack's only capacity (CST subscription only); the app said "waiting for ChatGPT" only after work had stopped.

### OBS-18

**A research run shows under the run that asked for it** · must · functional · **implemented**

A scout run that answers a research call shall be recorded under the run whose call asked that question (matched by the question), not under whichever run used the scout at the same time, so that a task's agents, sources, tokens and cost hold only its own work.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [APP-36](#app-36)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: the task page showed geizhals.de as the China briefing's top source; the owner: Yes, fix it`

- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `ops/conductor.py`
- Findings: [FND-111](#fnd-111), [FND-114](#fnd-114)

## PHN AI phone

### PHN-01

**What asks the owner waits for the answer** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Before anything on the AI phone that needs the owner's OK (a job, PHN-26; money, a setting, deleting), the AI phone service shall wait up to 15 minutes for the answer and treat no answer as no; looking at the screen, going back and going home need no approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`, `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_action_waits_for_the_owner`
- Trace: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Findings: [FND-13](#fnd-13)

### PHN-02

**A time window the owner opens** · must · functional · **implemented**

Where the owner opened a time window (minutes, or until he stops it), the AI phone service shall run ordinary actions without asking until it ends, still logging each one; spending money, settings and deletions shall ask each time, and typing or a web address only when it holds private data (DEC-38, PHN-19).

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_in_free_mode_only_an_address_or_text_with_private_data_asks`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_allow_for_a_few_minutes`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_allowance_any_length_or_until_stopped`
- Findings: [FND-02](#fnd-02), [FND-13](#fnd-13)

### PHN-03

**Money and destructive steps always ask** · must · constraint · **implemented**

An action that would buy, pay, order or subscribe, uninstall an app, delete files or trim caches, or change a phone setting shall need its own approval every time, even inside a time window, and that approval shall open no window.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_money_buttons_always_ask_even_in_a_time_window`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_uninstall_always_asks_even_inside_the_time_window`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_delete_always_asks_and_never_blocked_or_everything`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_start_in_ai_mode_asks_once_for_all_that_is_missing`

### PHN-04

**Blocked apps are never touched** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall check the block list (banking, investment, payment, wallet, password, authenticator, Play Store, Settings) before and after every action and before any screen read, and go to the home screen when a blocked app is in front.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_block_list_covers_money_passwords_and_settings`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_blocked_app_and_stop_refuse_everything`, `tests/test_phone.py::test_url_block_list_and_done_summary`

### PHN-05

**Stop ends it at once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner presses Stop, the AI phone service shall disconnect, refuse every action and approval, and end any time window until he switches it on again.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_blocked_app_and_stop_refuse_everything`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_stop_refuses_approvals`

### PHN-06

**A secure lock is never entered** · must · constraint · **implemented**

If the AI phone has a PIN, pattern or password lock, then the AI phone service shall stop and never enter it; a swipe lock may be dismissed.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_a_pin_locked_phone_is_never_unlocked`

### PHN-07

**The AI phone cannot approve itself** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Mission Control shall refuse approvals, time windows, grants, purchases and cleanup approvals that come from the AI phone's own address; the AI phone may only press Stop.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/17-ai-phone.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_cannot_approve_its_own_actions`, `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_approvals_are_refused_from_the_ai_phone`

### PHN-08

**The phone service reaches one phone only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service's firewall shall allow traffic only to the AI phone's tailnet address, no internet and no home network.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-12](#cst-12)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/entrypoint.sh`

### PHN-09

**An offline phone is not scanned** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the AI phone does not answer, then the AI phone service shall not scan its ports and shall retry with a back-off of up to 10 minutes.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `incident 2026-10-29: 11,000 hanging connections exhausted rootless Docker's network`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_an_offline_phone_is_not_port_scanned`

### PHN-10

**Reconnects after a restart** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the AI phone service restarts, it shall reconnect to a paired phone within a minute without the owner's action.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone_agents.py::test_a_paired_phone_reconnects_after_a_restart`

### PHN-11

**No approval card is left unanswerable** · must · functional · **implemented**

If an approval card is left behind by a restart, then the AI phone service shall drop it, log why and ask again where the work was mid-way (Starts in AI mode), and Mission Control shall tell the owner when an answer was not taken.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29: Allow gave no feedback`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_cards_left_by_a_restart_are_dropped_and_start_in_ai_mode_asks_again`, `tests/test_server.py::test_the_ai_phone_cannot_approve_its_own_actions`

### PHN-12

**The approval card answers at once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner taps Allow or Decline, the card shall change at once to what happens next and disappear when the phone is done.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-29`
- Verified by: inspection: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

### PHN-13

**The screen sleeps** · must · functional · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall let the screen sleep after 30 seconds when idle and not keep it on while charging, and wake it before each action (while the agents work it stays on, PHN-17).

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-08](#dec-08)
- Verified by: inspection: `images/phone/phone.py`
- Findings: [FND-01](#fnd-01)

### PHN-14

**Starts in AI mode after a restart** · should · functional · **implemented**

The AI phone shall come back to AI mode after a reboot (wireless debugging on through KeepADB, Tailscale as always-on VPN, both exempt from battery saving), applied only after the owner's one Allow.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-13](#dec-13)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_phone.py::test_start_in_ai_mode_asks_once_for_all_that_is_missing`

### PHN-15

**Deletes and uninstalls stay inside safe limits** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The AI phone service shall never uninstall blocked or system apps and shall delete only at allowed paths, optionally only files older than a given age, showing files, size and dates in the approval.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `images/phone/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_uninstall_refuses_blocked_and_system_apps`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_old_personal_data_is_offered_for_deletion_by_age`, `tests/test_cleanup.py::test_delete_always_asks_and_never_blocked_or_everything`

### PHN-16

**Screenshots go to the owner** · should · constraint · **implemented**

Screenshots shall go to the owner's approval card and log; a model shall get one only when it asks with phone_look(picture=true).

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04)

The Phone page shall show the screen's state and let the owner keep it on to watch (up to an hour) or turn it off.

## PLT Platform

### PLT-01

**Every service has limits and rotated logs** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every compose service shall have a mem_limit and the shared json-file logging block (10 MB, 3 files).

- Parents: [CST-01](#cst-01), [CST-14](#cst-14)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`, `ops/ci-compose-check.sh`
- Trace: `compose/core.yaml`, `compose/apps.yaml`

### PLT-02

**Least network per service** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each compose service shall join only the networks it needs; internal networks shall be internal, and only services that call a provider, a registry proxy or the web reader networks shall reach the internet.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/apps.yaml`, `compose/core.yaml`

### PLT-03

**No Docker socket in containers** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No container shall mount the Docker socket, and no service shall run privileged.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `compose/AGENTS.md`, `AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `.semgrep.yml`, `ops/semgrep_selftest.py`, `tests/semgrep`

### PLT-04

**Services come back by themselves** · must · quality · **implemented**

When a service crashes, the PC reboots or WSL restarts, the stack shall restart its services and timers without the owner's action.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `images/mission-control/guide/12-stack-page.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `compose/apps.yaml`, `ops/systemd/missions.service`, `ops/windows/host-setup.ps1`

### PLT-05

**WSL never idles out** · must · quality · **implemented**

The WSL VM shall not shut down while idle (idle timeouts off, a keep-alive task at logon).

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/windows/host-setup.ps1`

### PLT-06

**Hermes sees its tools after a boot** · must · functional · **implemented**

After a boot, the stack-ready job shall wait until the gateway lists every expected MCP tool for vk-main and restart Hermes once, before any job or mission may start (PLT-28).

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: `ops/stack_ready.py`

- Trace: `ops/stack_ready.py`

### PLT-07

**Self-healing WSL disk** · must · functional · **implemented**

A Windows task shall check the WSL disk at logon and every 15 minutes and repair file-system errors without administrator rights.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [CST-03](#cst-03)
- Sources: `ops/windows/AGENTS.md`

### PLT-08

**Deploy rebuilds what changed** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a change is deployed, ops/deploy.py shall rebuild the changed images, recreate their services, reconfigure the Hermes profiles, install changed units (enabling a timer it brings for the first time), restart the runner when one of its modules changed and the swarm service when the agent cards changed, and then run the health checks and the smoke test.

- Trace: `ops/deploy.py`

### PLT-09

**A failed deploy rolls back** · must · functional · **implemented**

If the health checks or the smoke test fail after a deploy, then the stack shall revert the merge, push the revert, and deploy the previous version.

### PLT-10

**One deploy at a time** · must · functional · **implemented**

ops/deploy.py shall take an exclusive lock, so that two deploys never overlap, and a deploy that cannot get it within 40 minutes shall give up without changing the stack.

The smoke test shall check every wiring boundary (gateway, key model grants and refusals, traces, Forgejo reach, gates, no Windows drive auto-mount) using only the local smoke model, and exit non-zero on any failure.

versions.lock shall record, per container, the image and digest that runs (marking a container that runs another build than its tag names), and the health board shall note, every 10 minutes, a container on another build than its tag and a running image that the lock file lacks.

The stack shall prune event logs, run logs, build logs and diffs, chat uploads and shares older than 90 days (shares after 14 days), once a day, keeping run summaries for good.

### PLT-26

**What nobody uses is found and offered for retirement** · should · functional · **partial**

The app shall count which screens are opened (route, day, count, nothing else), and from 30 days of data a monthly report shall list agents with no run, screens with no visit and services with no call, each offered to the owner for retirement with what it costs and what goes with it.

- Sources: `chat 2026-11-02: Retire what nobody uses`, `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`

- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Gap: The monthly report of unused agents, screens and services and its retirement decisions; the first is due from 2 Dec, when 30 days are counted.
