### Registry and identity

#### SWM-01

**Every agent described in one place** · must · functional · **partial**

Every agent shall be described once, as an agent card in config/agents.yaml, and its crew unit and timer, its gateway key with MCP grants, its Hermes profile settings, its Board contact and wake rules and its row on the Agents page shall be generated from that card.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a sample agents.yaml generates every derived file; the checks fail when a unit, key grant or profile in the repo differs from what the file generates.
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/registry.py`
- Gap: The cards exist and the checks fail on drift from keys and units; the units, Hermes profiles and the Agents page are not generated from them yet.
- Rationale: Today an agent is wired in up to five places (crew.py, keys.sh, Hermes config, systemd units, the app), which is how the shared vk-mission key (FND-03) happened unnoticed.

#### SWM-22

**A card states the agent's job** · must · interface · **implemented**

An agent card shall name the agent's id, purpose, responsibilities, owned artifact kinds with a definition of done for each, harness, model, key, schedule, roles, rooms, contact policy, wake policy, budgets, tools and autonomy, and the checks shall refuse a card that misses one or names an unknown harness, model, tool, role or room.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MAST failure taxonomy (Cemri et al., arXiv 2503.13657)`, `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_card_missing_a_field_or_naming_unknown_values_is_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a card missing each field in turn, and a card naming an unknown value of each kind, are refused with the field named.
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/registry.py`
- Rationale: Specification failures (unclear role, unclear task, no termination condition) are the largest of MAST's three failure categories; the card makes each agent's job explicit. The fields map onto an A2A AgentCard (name, description, skills) plus the stack's own.

#### SWM-23

**One identity per agent** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each agent shall have its own gateway key and its own swarm token, no key or token shall be listed by two cards, and the Swarm service shall take the actor of every event and the sender of every message from the token of the call, never from its arguments.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-07](#sn-07), [SEC-07](#sec-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI03 identity and privilege abuse`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_identity_comes_only_from_the_stored_credential`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_no_agent_config_or_environment_holds_a_swarm_token`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/registry.py`, `ops/swarm_setup.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0 (docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md): the gateway's per-user BYOK credentials carry each agent's own token to an MCP server; a key needs a LiteLLM user for it, so phase 1 gives every agent one.
- Findings: [FND-03](#fnd-03)
- Refined by: [SWM-89](#swm-89)

#### SWM-24

**Least privilege from the card** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each agent's gateway key shall grant exactly the MCP servers and swarm tools its card lists, and the checks shall refuse a key grant that no card lists.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SEC-07](#sec-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI02 tool misuse`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_shared_key_and_extra_grants_are_refused`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the key grants generated from a sample card equal its tool list; an extra grant in ops/keys.sh fails the checks.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`

#### SWM-89

**Swarm tokens stay in the gateway** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Each agent's swarm token shall be stored only as the agent's per-user credential in the gateway, never in an agent's environment, configuration or prompt, and the Swarm service shall take a caller's identity only from that stored credential and refuse a call that carries a client-supplied credential header.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SWM-23](#swm-23)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_identity_comes_only_from_the_stored_credential`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_no_agent_config_or_environment_holds_a_swarm_token`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/swarm_setup.py`, `ops/update-keys.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0 found that a credential header sent by the client replaces the stored one, so an agent that knew another agent's token could act as that agent; agents therefore never see tokens.
- Findings: [FND-32](#fnd-32)

#### SWM-25

**Each run names the card it ran with** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent run shall record the content hash of the card it ran with in its run manifest, so that the run view names what changed in the card since the agent's previous run.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [OBS-08](#obs-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_card_hash_changes_with_any_field`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_a_run_names_its_card_and_the_next_run_shows_a_change`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: two runs with different cards show the changed fields in the second run's drift line.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`

#### SWM-26

**Pause any agent, a role or the whole swarm** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner pauses an agent, a role or the whole swarm in Mission Control, the Swarm service shall refuse the paused agents' swarm tool calls and the runner shall start none of their runs within 60 seconds, until the owner resumes them.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI10 rogue agents`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_paused_agents_are_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_owner_pauses_and_resumes_agents_from_the_app`, `tests/test_runner.py::test_a_paused_agents_runs_are_skipped`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: after a pause, a swarm tool call from the agent is refused and a due timer run is skipped with the reason paused; demonstration on the phone.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/missions.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### SWM-27

**Agent cards in the A2A format** · could · interface · **implemented**

The Registry shall serve each card as an A2A v1.0 AgentCard document, on the Docker network only.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`, [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_each_card_is_an_a2a_agent_card_on_the_internal_network`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`

### Harnesses and models

#### SWM-02

**Different harnesses and models** · must · functional · **planned**

An agent shall run on any of the harnesses Hermes profile, Claude Code, OpenCode, Python job or script without a model, with any model the gateway serves or the Claude subscription, when only its card changes.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: -
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Acceptance: Test: changing a sample card's harness changes only the generated unit and adapter; the adapter conformance suite (SWM-28) passes for each harness.

#### SWM-28

**One adapter contract, tested for every harness** · must · interface · **partial**

Each harness adapter shall give its agent the swarm tools, run the start and finish steps of the run contract and start the agent when it is woken, and one conformance test suite shall run against every adapter.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_wake_waits_out_the_usage_limit_and_a_hermes_agent_gets_one_turn`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_woken_crew_agent_answers_its_requests_with_its_model`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_finish_is_checked_first_and_a_run_without_one_is_a_health_note`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/crew.py`
- Gap: The Hermes adapter (one turn through the running Hermes, ending with swarm_finish), the Claude Code crew (answers from its model, start and finish recorded by crew.py) and script agents (declined at once) are covered; OpenCode has no adapter yet.

#### SWM-29

**Subscription and local models only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A card shall name only a model the gateway serves under CST-02, the owner's Claude subscription or a local Ollama model, and the checks shall refuse any other.

- Parents: [CST-02](#cst-02), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), [DEC-16](#dec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_registry.py::test_paid_models_are_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a card naming a paid API model is refused.
- Trace: `ops/registry.py`

### Ledger

#### SWM-03

**One artifact model with a lasting identity** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent-produced artifact (idea, finding, report, wiki page, purchase, build, agent change, requirement) shall be one Ledger record with a kind, an identity key unique per kind, versions, an evidence log, links, watchers and references to outside records.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-10](#dec-10), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_history_is_append_only_and_rebuilds_the_state`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_kinds_are_data_not_code`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an artifact of each kind is created, versioned, linked and watched through the same tools.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Builds and purchases keep their own stores, where the owner's approvals and money rules live (SWM-69); the Ledger links them to their ideas by ref (build:<id>).
- Findings: [FND-24](#fnd-24)

#### SWM-30

**History is append-only** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every Ledger change shall be stored as an append-only event with actor, run id, time, sequence number, reason and evidence, the current state of an artifact shall be derived from its events, and no event shall be changed or deleted.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `event sourcing`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_history_is_append_only_and_rebuilds_the_state`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: replaying an artifact's events rebuilds its stored state; the database refuses an update or delete of an event (trigger).
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Audit, replay for the improvement loop (SWM-82) and restart safety (SWM-19) all need the full history, not the latest row.

#### SWM-31

**Retried writes take effect once** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every Ledger write shall carry an idempotency key, and a write repeated with the same key shall return the first result and change nothing.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `Hermes Kanban idempotency keys`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_retried_writes_take_effect_once`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the same upsert sent twice leaves one version and one event.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-32

**No lost updates between agents** · must · quality · **implemented**

A Ledger update shall name the version it was based on, and the Swarm service shall refuse an update based on an older version with the current version, so that the agent can merge and retry.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `optimistic concurrency control`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_a_stale_update_is_refused_with_the_current_version`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: two agents update the same artifact from version 3; the second is refused and receives version 4.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-33

**Look up before writing** · must · functional · **implemented**

When an agent creates an artifact whose key already exists, the Ledger shall apply it as an update of the existing artifact; when the TF-IDF cosine similarity of a new artifact's text to an open artifact of the same kind reaches the calibrated threshold (0.20 on the owner's triage of 2026-10-29), the Ledger shall refuse the create unless the agent links it as different with a reason.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_same_key_updates_and_a_similar_new_one_is_refused`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_report_names_what_the_guard_would_have_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a reworded idea with the same key updates the existing one; a reworded idea with a new key above the threshold is refused with the similar artifact named; the calibration set catches all 17 known pairs.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: 7 of 26 ideas on 2026-10-29 were reworded duplicates that title matching missed. Phase 0 measured TF-IDF (AUC 0.992, 7 of 7 nearest neighbours right) as good as embeddings (0.993, 7 of 7) on that triage, at no memory cost; the threshold catches every known duplicate and is recalibrated on the Ledger's own merges.
- Findings: [FND-26](#fnd-26)

#### SWM-09

**Agents see each other's artifacts** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent shall be able to search, read, link and watch every other agent's artifacts, within the information-flow rules of SWM-67.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_private_artifacts_never_reach_an_agent_with_a_way_out`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_the_builders_prompt_lists_the_finding_the_doctor_linked_to_its_idea`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/builds.py`
- Findings: [FND-22](#fnd-22)

#### SWM-34

**Watchers hear of every change** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a watched artifact changes, the Swarm service shall put the change into each watcher's next context pack, and into the owner's Chatroom feed when he watches it.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_watchers_see_changes_once`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an agent watching an artifact finds its new version in its next context pack, once.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-35

**One store per artifact** · must · constraint · **implemented**

When a kind moves into the Ledger, its existing records shall be migrated with their history in one step, and in the same phase the old writer shall be removed, so that no artifact is written in two stores.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_accept_creates_a_build_and_dismiss_becomes_a_lesson`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_imports_ideas_and_the_owners_decisions`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_imports_ideas_and_the_owners_decisions`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_migration_import_is_for_the_system_only`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: after phase 1, nothing writes the proposals table or the crew finding files; a test migrates a copy of missions.sqlite and finds every proposal with its decisions.
- Trace: `ops/crew.py`, `ops/doctor.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-24](#fnd-24), [FND-12](#fnd-12)

#### SWM-36

**Nothing is lost from the record** · must · quality · **implemented**

Ledger events and Board messages shall be kept for at least 365 days and included in the nightly backup, and none shall be deleted before then.

- Parents: [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_snapshot_is_a_consistent_copy`, `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_restore_drill_opens_the_swarm_snapshot_and_counts_its_record`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: the swarm database lies under /srv/ai and is in the backup set; the restore rehearsal restores it.
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/backup.sh`, `ops/restore.sh`, `ops/restore_check.py`

### Lifecycle

#### SWM-04

**The generic lifecycle** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every artifact shall move through draft, open, ready, active, checking and done, with the side states blocked, snoozed, declined, merged and gone, and every state other than merged shall be able to return to open with a reason.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_every_transition_follows_the_table`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_merging_moves_the_evidence_to_the_target`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: every allowed transition succeeds and every other is refused (SWM-37).
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Merged keeps its pointer to the target; reopening means reopening the target. The owner may also mark an artifact gone: "already done by hand" is gone, not declined (2026-10-29).

#### SWM-37

**Transitions are data, refusals are explained** · must · quality · **implemented**

The allowed transitions shall be one table of state, next state and actor class in the Swarm service, and every refused transition shall return the rule that refused it.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_every_transition_follows_the_table`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: one test per row of the table, and one refused move per actor class with its reason.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-06

**Who may move which state** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Agents shall be able to create, refine, link, merge, block, reopen and mark gone; only the owner, or an autonomy rule he enabled (SWM-42), shall move an artifact to active, decline or snooze it; only the Checker shall move it to done.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-19](#dec-19)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_agents_cannot_approve_decline_snooze_or_finish`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_every_transition_follows_the_table`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an agent's move to active, declined, snoozed or done is refused; the owner's and the Checker's succeed.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-38

**A definition of ready per kind** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall refuse an agent's move of an artifact to ready unless it meets its kind's definition of ready; for an idea that is a problem statement, at least two evidence entries or one health alert, a plan naming files and a test, a success check (SWM-44) and a confidence of medium or high.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_definition_of_ready_for_an_idea`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an idea missing each part in turn cannot become ready; a complete one can.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: The definition gates an agent's claim; the owner's Build it is his own judgement (DEC-22).

#### SWM-05

**Agents refine and reopen over many runs** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before writing, an agent shall look up the artifact by key and similarity and update, merge or reopen it with new evidence and a new plan version instead of creating a copy.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_scan_turns_signals_into_ideas_and_refines_a_known_one`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_same_key_updates_and_a_similar_new_one_is_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: three doctor runs on the same issue leave one artifact with three evidence entries and plan version 3.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-39

**A declined artifact returns only with news** · must · functional · **implemented**

If an agent reopens a declined artifact, then the Swarm service shall require evidence dated after the decline and show the artifact to the owner as reopened with that evidence.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_a_declined_artifact_returns_only_with_newer_evidence`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a reopen of a declined idea without newer evidence is refused; with newer evidence it shows on Home with the reason.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: A near-copy of a declined idea is compared too: it becomes a sighting on the declined idea, which stays declined, so what the owner said no to is not proposed again in new words (2026-10-29: the test agent's #30 restated #11).

#### SWM-08

**Ranking instead of caps** · must · functional · **implemented**

Open and ready artifacts shall be ranked by priority = impact x confidence x (1 + log2(1 + recurrences in 30 days)) / effort, with the owner's pins on top, and the backlog shall not be capped.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_ranking_follows_the_formula_and_pins_come_first`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the ranking of a sample backlog matches the formula; a pinned artifact ranks first; creating the 50th open idea succeeds.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Rationale: A recurrence is a reopen, a health alert as evidence, or a duplicate merged into the artifact: on 2026-10-29 five ideas were merged into one, which the old flat list could not show.
- Findings: [FND-12](#fnd-12)

#### SWM-40

**Limit work in progress, not the backlog** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall refuse to move an artifact to active while its kind's work-in-progress limit is reached (start value for builds 2), and the refused artifact shall stay ready at its rank.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), `Kanban WIP limits`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_only_two_ideas_are_built_at_once`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: a third build cannot become active while two are; it becomes active when one leaves.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-41

**Kinds extend the lifecycle, never fork it** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A kind shall add only steps inside a state and fields of its own, and adding a kind shall be a Registry change that needs no change to the lifecycle code.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_kinds_are_data_not_code`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a new kind declared only in configuration can be created, moved and checked.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-42

**Autonomy rules are the owner's, and off by default** · must · constraint · **implemented**

An autonomy rule that lets an artifact move to active without the owner's tap shall exist only when the owner enabled it for one kind with its conditions, recorded as a decision, and no rule shall cover money, passwords, phone settings, deletion, NAS changes or deploys outside the builder's tap-to-deploy path.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-07](#cst-07)
- Sources: [DEC-19](#dec-19), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_an_enabled_rule_starts_what_it_covers_and_withdrawing_it_holds_the_rest`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: with no rule enabled every move to active needs the owner; a rule for kind idea never applies to a purchase; a rule naming a forbidden action is refused.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/checker.py`
- Rationale: DEC-19 says everything that ships needs the owner's approval; whether small upkeep fixes may ship by themselves is an open question to him, so the default is off.

#### SWM-43

**Autonomy can be withdrawn at once** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the owner withdraws an autonomy rule, the Swarm service shall move the artifacts that became active under it and have not shipped to blocked, waiting for the owner.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-19](#dec-19)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_an_enabled_rule_starts_what_it_covers_and_withdrawing_it_holds_the_rest`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: withdrawing a rule blocks its unshipped active artifacts and leaves shipped ones in checking.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

### Verification

#### SWM-07

**Success checks decide done** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every actionable artifact shall carry a machine-checkable success check, and after it ships the Checker (code, no model) shall move it to done when the check holds for its window or back to open when it fails.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_the_checker_proves_reopens_and_catches_a_regression`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: a deployed fix whose issue stays away for its window becomes done; one whose issue returns is reopened with the new evidence.
- Trace: `ops/checker.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-44

**A typed catalogue of checks** · must · interface · **implemented**

A success check shall be one of health_issue_absent, log_count, test_passes, metric, ci_green, smoke_ok or owner_confirms, with typed parameters, and owner_confirms shall be accepted only when the artifact states why no other type can check it.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_a_check_must_be_complete_and_name_what_exists`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_each_check_type_passes_fails_or_waits`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_the_facts_read_as_empty_where_the_stack_is_not`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_the_owner_confirms_or_reopens_a_shipped_idea`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_a_log_count_can_read_a_host_units_journal`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_a_log_count_waits_its_days_not_one_day`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: each type passes and fails on fixtures; owner_confirms without a reason is refused.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/checker.py`

#### SWM-45

**A check must be runnable before it counts** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before an artifact becomes active, the runner shall check that its success check names a test, container, metric or issue that exists, and refuse Build it or an autonomy rule's start otherwise.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_a_check_must_be_complete_and_name_what_exists`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_an_enabled_rule_starts_what_it_covers_and_withdrawing_it_holds_the_rest`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: an idea whose check names a missing test node cannot become ready.
- Trace: `ops/checker.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Rationale: The swarm service cannot see the host's tests and containers; the runner can, so it checks there.

#### SWM-46

**No agent certifies its own work** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The agent that owns or carried out an artifact shall not decide that it is done; done shall follow only from the Checker's run of the success check.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MAST failure taxonomy (Cemri et al., arXiv 2503.13657) (task verification failures)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_agents_cannot_approve_decline_snooze_or_finish`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_every_transition_follows_the_table`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: a transition to done from any agent token, including the owner agent's, is refused.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Missing or incorrect verification is one of MAST's three failure categories; independent, ground-truth verification is the remedy.

#### SWM-47

**Regressions reopen and climb** · must · functional · **implemented**

When the success check of an artifact that is done fails within 30 days, the Checker shall reopen it, add the failure as evidence and count a recurrence.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-10](#dec-10), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_the_checker_proves_reopens_and_catches_a_regression`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Acceptance: Test: a done artifact whose issue returns on day 12 is open again with recurrence 1 and a higher rank.
- Trace: `ops/checker.py`

### Board

#### SWM-10

**Agents talk directly, to groups and to all** · must · functional · **implemented**

An agent shall be able to message chosen agents directly, multicast to every agent with a role, post to a room (room:all reaches every agent) and discuss an artifact in its thread.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_each_address_reaches_exactly_its_agents`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: one message per address type reaches exactly the agents it names.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`
- Findings: [FND-22](#fnd-22)

#### SWM-48

**Messages in the A2A shape** · must · interface · **implemented**

A Board message shall carry messageId, sender, recipients, contextId (its thread), replyTo, parts (text, data or an artifact reference), act, priority, expiry, hop count and a schema version, in the A2A v1.0 Message and Part structure.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_each_address_reaches_exactly_its_agents`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: every stored message validates against the schema; a message without contextId or act is refused.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-49

**Five acts with fixed meanings** · must · functional · **implemented**

A message's act shall be inform, request, propose, handoff or ack; a request shall become a tracked request (SWM-12), a propose shall become a Ledger operation only when the artifact's owner agent accepts it, and a handoff shall make the receiver the artifact's owner.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `FIPA ACL speech acts`, [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_handoff_gives_the_artifact_to_the_receiver`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_proposal_is_applied_only_when_the_artifacts_owner_accepts`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-12

**Requests are never lost** · must · functional · **implemented**

A request between agents shall be tracked in the A2A task states submitted, working, input-required, completed, failed, rejected or canceled until it ends, and shall appear in the sender's and the receiver's context pack until then.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_request_is_tracked_until_it_ends_and_expires_with_a_reason`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: an open request is in both context packs on every run until it is completed; it survives a restart.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: A2A v1.0 has no expired state; an expired request is canceled with the reason expired (SWM-50).

#### SWM-50

**Every request and thread ends** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every request shall carry an expiry (default 24 hours, at most 7 days), and when it expires the Board shall cancel it with the reason expired and tell the sender in its next context pack.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MAST failure taxonomy (Cemri et al., arXiv 2503.13657) (termination failures)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_request_is_tracked_until_it_ends_and_expires_with_a_reason`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a request with a 1-hour expiry is canceled after an hour and appears as expired in the sender's pack.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/checker.py`

#### SWM-51

**Contact policies are enforced** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Board shall deliver a message only to recipients its sender's card may message, and shall refuse any other with the policy that refused it.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MCP Agent Mail contact policies`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_contact_policy_and_private_content`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a scout message to the builder is refused when the scout's card does not list it.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-52

**Delivery once, in order** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Board shall deliver each message to each recipient at least once, keep only one copy per messageId, order each thread by sequence number and record when each recipient read it.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_owner_comes_first_and_agent_claims_are_marked`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a message sent twice with the same messageId is stored once; a thread reads back in send order; a read receipt is stored when the recipient's context pack includes it.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-14

**Code guards against loops and floods** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Board shall stop a reply chain between agents after 4 hops without an owner message or an artifact change, refuse more than 20 messages an hour per agent and per room and more than 50 a day per thread, refuse a message near-identical to one in the same thread within 24 hours, and accept in room:all only informs that reference an artifact.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI08 cascading failures`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_code_guards_loops_floods_repeats_and_room_all`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: one test per guard: the 5th hop, the 21st message, the duplicate and an unreferenced post to room:all are refused with the guard named.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: The numbers are start values for the owner to confirm (design decision 5).

#### SWM-13

**Agents are woken through the runner, within budgets** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a message arrives for a scheduled agent, the Swarm service shall ask the runner, through its inbox, to wake the agent according to the card's wake policy (never, digest, on_request, always) and daily wake budget, and the runner shall start the run only if the agent's previous run has ended.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-03](#sn-03), [SEC-11](#sec-11)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_request_wakes_an_on_request_agent_within_its_budget`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_wake_waits_out_the_usage_limit_and_a_hermes_agent_gets_one_turn`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-23](#fnd-23)

#### SWM-53

**Wakes wait out the usage limit** · must · functional · **implemented**

While the subscription's usage limit is reached, the runner shall hold wakes and start them when the limit lifts, and shall drop none.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-01](#dec-01), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_wake_waits_out_the_usage_limit_and_a_hermes_agent_gets_one_turn`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_the_usage_limit_is_read_from_the_runners_real_state`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-31](#fnd-31)

#### SWM-54

**The owner's messages come first** · must · functional · **implemented**

An agent's context pack shall list unread messages from the owner before every other item.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MCP Agent Mail overseer messages`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_app_door_only_reads_and_the_owner_posts_through_the_runner`, `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_owner_comes_first_and_agent_claims_are_marked`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: in a pack with 30 items the owner's message is first.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-55

**Decisions live in the Ledger, not in chat** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every owner message that approves, declines or sets a rule shall be linked to the Ledger event it caused, and an agent message claiming a decision that has no Ledger event shall change nothing.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_owner_comes_first_and_agent_claims_are_marked`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: the owner's decline in a thread creates the declined event and links it; an agent saying approved does not move the artifact.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: No message changes an artifact's state: approvals, declines and rules are the owner's taps, each a Ledger event.

#### SWM-56

**Rooms** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Board shall start with the rooms all, health, code, research, house, shopping and ops; an agent may propose a room and it shall exist only after the owner confirms it.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_each_address_reaches_exactly_its_agents`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a post to an unconfirmed room is refused; after the owner confirms it, the post is accepted.
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: The rooms are listed in config/agents.yaml: a new room is a reviewed change the owner lands.

### Chatroom

#### SWM-11

**The chatroom** · must · functional · **implemented**

Mission Control shall show every room, direct-message thread, request and artifact thread live, and let the owner post; only his messages shall approve, decline or set rules.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_app_door_only_reads_and_the_owner_posts_through_the_runner`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Demonstration: the owner reads a DM between two agents and posts in a room on his phone; test: an approval posted by an agent token has no effect.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-57

**Live and phone-first** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Chatroom page shall show a new message within 5 seconds of its arrival, work at a 375-pixel-wide viewport, and filter by agent, room and act with full-text search.

- Parents: [SN-06](#sn-06), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_app_door_only_reads_and_the_owner_posts_through_the_runner`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a message posted through the API appears in the event stream within 5 seconds; demonstration in the mobile viewport.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`

#### SWM-58

**The owner always sees who said it** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Chatroom shall show each message's sender and the origin of its content (owner, agent or external source), and shall mark agent text that claims an approval, an urgency or the owner's wish as an agent's claim.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI09 human-agent trust exploitation`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_owner_comes_first_and_agent_claims_are_marked`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a message whose text says 'the owner approved this' renders with the agent-claim mark and its origin.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-59

**Notifications through the one policy** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Board shall notify the owner of mentions and urgent requests only through the stack's single notification path (OBS-16), once per item, and shall send no Telegram message of its own.

- Parents: [SN-18](#sn-18), [OBS-16](#obs-16)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_app_door_only_reads_and_the_owner_posts_through_the_runner`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_mention_notifies_the_owner_once_through_the_shared_path`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-17](#fnd-17)

#### SWM-90

**Who works on what, and every idea's timeline** · should · functional · **implemented**

Mission Control shall show, from the Ledger, which agents wrote to which artifacts and which agents messaged each other in the last 30 days, the artifacts two or more agents work on, and for each artifact its events, evidence and thread merged by time.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_graph_shows_who_works_on_what_and_who_talks`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: the graph names the shared artifact and the talking pair; demonstration on the Swarm page.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`
- Rationale: The owner asked to see which agents interact on the same artifacts and with each other (2026-10-29).
- Findings: [FND-30](#fnd-30)

### Run contract

#### SWM-16

**One run contract for every harness** · must · functional · **partial**

Every agent run shall start with its context pack (owner messages, open requests, own and watched artifacts, lessons, its journal) and end with validated Ledger operations, messages and a run summary, through the same MCP tools on every harness.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_a_pack_asked_again_soon_is_not_built_twice`, `tests/test_improve.py::test_no_caps_and_a_known_problem_is_refined_not_copied`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_context_pack_has_its_order_budget_and_lessons`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_wake_waits_out_the_usage_limit_and_a_hermes_agent_gets_one_turn`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_woken_crew_agent_answers_its_requests_with_its_model`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_finish_is_checked_first_and_a_run_without_one_is_a_health_note`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/crew.py`
- Gap: The Hermes adapter (one turn through the running Hermes, ending with swarm_finish), the Claude Code crew (answers from its model, start and finish recorded by crew.py) and script agents (declined at once) are covered; OpenCode has no adapter yet.
- Findings: [FND-19](#fnd-19), [FND-63](#fnd-63)

#### SWM-60

**The context pack has a budget and an order** · must · quality · **implemented**

The context pack shall hold at most 12,000 characters, in the order owner messages, open requests, own artifacts by rank, watched changes, help received (SWM-109), lessons, journal, and shall record what it cut; the team roster and its rule (SWM-108) come after it, outside the budget.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-03](#sn-03)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [MIS-18](#mis-18)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_context_pack_has_its_order_budget_and_lessons`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an oversized pack is cut from the end, stays within 12,000 characters and lists the cut items.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: The journal block alone is capped at 6,000 characters (MIS-18); the pack keeps the run's prompt within the subscription's limits and the agent's attention.

#### SWM-61

**Lessons from the owner's decisions** · must · functional · **implemented**

The context pack shall include the owner's decisions and reasons on the agent's own artifacts from the last 90 days.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-10](#dec-10)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_improve.py::test_accept_creates_a_build_and_dismiss_becomes_a_lesson`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_imports_ideas_and_the_owners_decisions`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_the_context_pack_has_its_order_budget_and_lessons`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a decline reason given to the doctor's idea is in the doctor's next context pack.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/doctor.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-62

**A small tool surface** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The swarm shall offer agents at most 12 MCP tools, whose descriptions together hold at most 6,000 characters.

- Parents: [SN-03](#sn-03), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MCP Agent Mail's 45 tools as the counter-example`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_board_tools_keep_the_surface_small`, `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_tool_surface_is_small`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the tool list served by the Swarm MCP server has at most 12 entries and 6,000 description characters.
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`

#### SWM-63

**Stateless, short tool calls** · must · interface · **implemented**

The swarm MCP tools shall keep no state in the MCP session and each shall return within 30 seconds; work that waits longer shall be a tracked request (SWM-12), not a blocking call.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MCP specification 2026-07-28 (stateless core, Tasks extension)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_a_request_is_tracked_until_it_ends_and_expires_with_a_reason`, `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_board_tools_keep_the_surface_small`, `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_tool_surface_is_small`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: every tool answers a fresh connection without prior calls; a test with a slow backend gets a request id within 30 seconds.
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`
- Rationale: MCP 2026-07-28 removed protocol sessions and moved long-running work to the Tasks extension; the mail tool's 960-second timeout shows what blocking calls cost.

#### SWM-64

**A run without a finish is visible** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall validate a run's finish operations and refuse invalid ones with a reason the agent can act on, and a run that ends without a finish shall raise a health note on its agent.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [OBS-07](#obs-07), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_a_finish_is_checked_first_and_a_run_without_one_is_a_health_note`, `tests/test_crew.py::test_a_crew_job_without_a_swarm_card_opens_no_swarm_run`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/crew.py`
- Findings: [FND-113](#fnd-113)

#### SWM-21

**Multi-step Hermes work on Hermes Kanban** · could · functional · **deprecated**

Multi-step work shared by Hermes profiles shall run as Hermes Kanban task graphs keyed by the artifact's key, with Kanban's events mirrored into the Ledger.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_an_idea_split_into_three_tasks_offers_two_at_once_and_the_third_after_both`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0: tasks are created idempotently from outside Hermes (the artifact key as idempotency key) and task_events can be followed by id; the bridge reads them through the Hermes container (the host cannot read its volume) and keeps the actor itself (Kanban records outside comments as 'default').
- Reason: Superseded by SWM-91 (DEC-25): the task graph lives in the Ledger, which already holds every artifact, its history and the owner's decisions; the builders are not Hermes profiles.

### Memory

#### SWM-65

**The journal is the notebook** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The swarm shall use each agent's journal (MIS-18, MIS-19) as the agent's memory between runs and shall add no second per-agent memory store.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [MIS-18](#mis-18)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: inspection: `ops/journal.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: the context pack's memory block comes from ops/journal.py; no swarm table or Hindsight bank holds agent notes.
- Trace: `ops/journal.py`
- Rationale: The Ledger holds artifacts, events and lessons only; agent notes stay in the journal (vault agents/<id>/MEMORY.md).
- Findings: [FND-19](#fnd-19)

#### SWM-66

**Memory cannot be poisoned through the swarm** · must · constraint · **partial**

Journal entries, lessons and evidence derived from external content shall keep that origin, and a standing note shall come only from the owner, the agent's own card or the memory curator, never from another agent's message.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI06 memory and context poisoning`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_external_content_stays_marked_as_untrusted`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a standing note proposed in an agent message is refused; an evidence entry quoting a web page keeps its external origin in the next pack.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Gap: External origin is kept and marked; standing notes still live only in the journal, which agents cannot write through the swarm yet.

### Safety and security

#### SWM-15

**Messages are untrusted data** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Board shall carry the origin of each message's content, redact secrets, and present agent messages to agents as data, and an approval claimed in an agent message shall have no effect.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI01 agent goal hijack, ASI07 insecure inter-agent communication`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_contact_policy_and_private_content`, `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_the_owner_comes_first_and_agent_claims_are_marked`
- Phase: 3 Board and Chatroom
- Acceptance: Test: a message containing an instruction and an approval claim reaches the recipient wrapped as data and moves nothing.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-22](#fnd-22)

#### SWM-67

**Information-flow labels keep the trust boundaries** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Every message, evidence entry and artifact shall carry a sensitivity label (public, internal, private) and an origin, content derived from labelled inputs shall carry the highest of their labels, and the Swarm service shall refuse to deliver private content to an agent whose card holds a way out (web fetch, browser, AI phone, mail sending) and deliver external content to an agent that holds private data only labelled as untrusted.

- Parents: [SN-04](#sn-04), [SN-05](#sn-05), [SEC-04](#sec-04), [SEC-05](#sec-05)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI01`, `lethal trifecta (S. Willison)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_contact_policy_and_private_content`, `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_private_artifacts_never_reach_an_agent_with_a_way_out`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: Hermes main's message quoting a vault note is refused for the scout; the scout's research note reaches Hermes main wrapped as untrusted; a summary of a private artifact is private.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Without labels the Board would join every agent's private data, untrusted input and ways out into one system, undoing the separation SEC-03 and SEC-04 build.
- Findings: [FND-22](#fnd-22)

#### SWM-68

**No secrets in the swarm** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall run the stack's redaction on every write and refuse a message or artifact that still contains a key, a token, a password or a payment card number.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-06](#cst-06)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [SEC-16](#sec-16)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_secrets_are_redacted_and_card_numbers_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a message carrying a sample API key is refused; a log excerpt with a token is stored redacted.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Findings: [FND-93](#fnd-93)

#### SWM-69

**Standing approvals stay where they are** · must · constraint · **implemented**

No role, card, rule or message in the swarm shall change who approves money, purchases, passwords, phone settings, deletions, NAS changes or deploys; those approvals shall stay with the services that ask the owner today.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [CST-05](#cst-05), [CST-07](#cst-07), [CST-11](#cst-11)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-03](#dec-03)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_tool_surface_is_small`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: the swarm tools offer no action that bypasses the phone, mail, shopping or builder approvals; test: an autonomy rule naming one is refused (SWM-42).
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`
- Rationale: The swarm's tools offer no approval: money, purchases, phone, mail, deletions and deploys keep their own owner approvals in the phone, mail, shopping and builder services.

#### SWM-70

**No agent rewrites itself** · must · constraint · **implemented**

An agent shall not change its own card, prompt, tools or model; such a change shall exist only as an agent-change artifact that passes the gate of SWM-18.

- Parents: [SN-05](#sn-05), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI10 rogue agents`, [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_no_agent_writes_or_moves_an_agent_change_about_itself`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_build_that_changes_an_agent_is_blocked_without_a_passed_agent_change`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: a Ledger operation from an agent that writes, edits or moves an agent change about itself is refused; the builder's review blocks a build touching config/agents.yaml (or ops/keys.sh, a Hermes profile, the builder's own code) without a linked agent change whose experiment passed, and only the owner's written override lifts the block.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/builds.py`

#### SWM-71

**A failing agent stops itself** · must · functional · **implemented**

If an agent's runs fail 3 times in a row, or its messages trip the Board's guards 3 times within 24 hours, then the Swarm service shall pause the agent (SWM-26) and raise one health alert.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI08 cascading failures`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_a_failing_proposer_pauses_itself_once`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_guard_trips_pause_an_agent_and_expired_requests_and_refused_wakes_are_signals`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Trace: `ops/checker.py`

### Reliability and resources

#### SWM-19

**Waiting state survives restarts** · must · quality · **partial**

Everything that waits (an approval, a request, a blocked artifact, a wake) shall be stored with its resume point, so that a restart of any agent or service resumes it.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_writes_wait_in_the_outbox_while_the_service_is_down`, `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_snapshot_is_a_consistent_copy`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the Swarm service restarted while a request is open resumes the request and delivers its answer.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Gap: The Ledger and the runner's outbox survive restarts; the mail connector drops a first-Trash card left by a restart and its agent asks again (CNX-06, FND-14), so that wait is redone rather than resumed.
- Findings: [FND-14](#fnd-14)

#### SWM-72

**Agents work when the swarm is down** · must · quality · **partial**

If the Swarm service is unreachable, then an agent run shall do its own job, queue its finish operations locally and the runner shall replay them in order within 5 minutes of the service's return, and no run shall fail only because the swarm is down.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications 2026 ASI08 cascading failures`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_sync_survives_a_restarting_service`, `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_writes_wait_in_the_outbox_while_the_service_is_down`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: with the service stopped a crew run completes and its operations appear in the Ledger after the service starts.
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Gap: The runner's writes wait in the outbox; agents that call through the gateway (later phases) have no outbox yet.

#### SWM-73

**One writer, one database, backed up** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall be the only writer of the swarm database (SQLite in WAL mode under /srv/ai), and the restore rehearsal shall restore it.

- Parents: [SN-09](#sn-09), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-39](#dec-39), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_snapshot_is_a_consistent_copy`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: no other process opens the database for writing; the rehearsal's checklist includes it.
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`
- Rationale: Design decision 2 (SQLite or a database on the stack's Postgres) was decided by the owner on 2 Nov 2026 (DEC-39).

#### SWM-74

**The swarm fits the memory budget** · must · constraint · **partial**

The Swarm service shall run with a mem_limit of 256 MB and load no embedding model, so that the swarm adds at most 256 MB to the stack's resident memory.

- Parents: [CST-14](#cst-14), [CST-01](#cst-01)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_tool_surface_is_small`
- Phase: 0 Proof of the risky parts
- Acceptance: Analysis: phase 0 measures the service's p95 resident memory under a replayed week of runs and writes it into the memory budget table.
- Trace: `compose/apps.yaml`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Gap: mem_limit 256 MB and no embedding model. Measured on 2026-11-01 at 22:43, three days after the switch-over, it used 60.5 MB with 504 artifacts, 1,357 events and 7 messages; the p95 over a full week is still to be read.
- Rationale: Phase 0 measured 48 MB peak for a prototype at target size (2,000 artifacts, 100,000 events, 20,000 messages); embeddings would have cost 1.2 GB peak for no better duplicate detection.
- Findings: [FND-18](#fnd-18), [FND-26](#fnd-26)

#### SWM-75

**Tool calls answer within half a second** · should · quality · **implemented**

With 2,000 artifacts and 100,000 events, ledger_find, ledger_get and board_inbox shall answer within 500 ms at the 95th percentile.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-01](#cst-01)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: analysis: `docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md`, `ops/swarm-probes/proto.py`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a benchmark on a generated database of that size stays within 500 ms p95.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Rationale: Phase 0's prototype at that size: p95 5.1 ms (find), 3.2 ms (get), 3.7 ms (inbox).

#### SWM-76

**Schema changes are versioned and rehearsed** · must · process · **implemented**

Every change to the swarm database's schema shall be a numbered, forward-only migration, tested on a copy of the live database before it deploys.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-09](#sn-09)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_ledger.py::test_schema_migrations_are_numbered_and_forward_only`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: migrations apply in order to an empty and to a sample database; the deploy runs them on a copy first.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`

#### SWM-77

**No new way in** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Swarm service shall publish no port: agents reach it through the gateway on an internal network, and the host runner through a Unix socket in /srv/ai/swarm.

- Parents: [CST-04](#cst-04), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_service.py::test_the_runner_acts_only_for_host_agents_and_relays_the_owner`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: the compose service has no ports entry; the runner's door refuses a caller without its token.
- Trace: `compose/apps.yaml`

### Observability

#### SWM-78

**Every change and message is on the timelines** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every Ledger event shall appear as an activity event on the owner's timelines, and every write made for a run shall appear in that run's view (OBS-05).

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [OBS-05](#obs-05)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05), [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_ledger_changes_reach_the_activity_feed_once`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: an upsert and a message from a run show in that run's view and on the activity feed.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `ops/systemd/swarm-sync.timer`

#### SWM-79

**Every run is a trace, whatever the harness** · must · interface · **implemented**

Every agent run shall be a trace in Phoenix with an invoke_agent span, a chat span per model call and an execute_tool span per tool call, carrying the agent, the run id and what it belongs to: gateway runs through LiteLLM's tracing, Claude Code runs exported from the runtime record when they end.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [OBS-14](#obs-14)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05), `OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions (agent and tool spans)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_otlp.py::test_a_claude_run_becomes_one_trace_with_model_and_tool_spans`, `tests/test_otlp.py::test_only_claude_runs_are_traced_when_they_end`, `tests/test_otlp.py::test_the_protobuf_is_an_otlp_export_request`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a run on each harness yields one trace in Phoenix with the attributes set.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/otlp.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`
- Rationale: The GenAI conventions are still in development status; the span names follow them and the attribute names are pinned in one place so a convention change is one edit.
- Findings: [FND-25](#fnd-25)

#### SWM-80

**The swarm's own failure signals** · must · functional · **implemented**

The health board shall raise alerts for guard trips, expired requests, refused wakes, refused transitions above 10 a day per agent, Ledger write errors and Checker failures.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-18](#sn-18)
- Sources: [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_recoverable.py::test_a_fault_the_checker_finds_is_an_alert_not_its_failed_run`, `tests/test_checker.py::test_refused_writes_become_a_health_signal_and_scorecards_add_up`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_guard_trips_pause_an_agent_and_expired_requests_and_refused_wakes_are_signals`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Trace: `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/checker.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`

#### SWM-17

**Scorecards per agent** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every agent shall have a daily scorecard from the Ledger's history (accepted share, proven share, reopen rate, duplicate attempts, decline reasons, request handling, cost, cost per proven artifact) on the Agents page.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-05](#dec-05)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_checker.py::test_refused_writes_become_a_health_signal_and_scorecards_add_up`, `tests/test_swarm_board.py::test_tokens_per_agent_include_the_claude_runs_under_a_crew_job`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_request_handling_is_on_the_scorecard`
- Phase: 2 Checker, scorecards and autonomy rules
- Trace: `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Findings: [FND-25](#fnd-25)

#### SWM-81

**Cost per run on every harness** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every run shall record its model calls with tokens and its duration in the runtime record, from the gateway's records or, for Claude Code, from its transcript's usage, so that cost is known per run on every harness.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-03](#sn-03), [MIS-16](#mis-16)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_runtime.py::test_the_claude_hook_turns_hooks_and_transcript_into_events`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Test: a Claude Code run's recorded usage equals the usage in its JSON result.
- Trace: `config/hermes/claude-hooks/runtime_hook.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`
- Rationale: On the subscriptions the money cost is $0; the cost that matters is the usage limit, so tokens per model call are the measure.
- Findings: [FND-25](#fnd-25)

### Improvement loop

#### SWM-18

**Agent changes are evaluated before they ship** · should · process · **implemented**

A change to an agent's prompt, tools, model or harness shall run on a replay set of its past runs and ship only when no score drops and its target score rises, then be watched for 14 days with a one-step revert.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [QA-07](#qa-07), [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_an_agent_change_leaves_ready_only_after_a_passed_experiment_and_a_drop_sends_it_back`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_an_experiment_replays_both_arms_on_the_same_examples_and_ships_only_through_build_it`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_ready_change_without_an_experiment_starts_one_and_an_agent_without_a_model_cannot_be_replayed`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_shipped_agent_change_is_watched_and_a_drop_reopens_it_with_the_revert`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_deployed_build_is_reverted_in_one_step_through_the_merge_queue`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: an agent-change artifact without an experiment result cannot leave ready; one whose scores drop returns to open with the results.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/evals.py`, `ops/checker.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `ops/improve.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`
- Findings: [FND-36](#fnd-36), [FND-37](#fnd-37)

#### SWM-82

**Replay sets from real runs** · should · functional · **partial**

Every run shall store its inputs (context pack, offered tools, card version) with its outcome label (proven, declined with reason, duplicate, failed) in a Phoenix dataset of its agent, on every harness.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `Phoenix datasets and experiments`, [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_finished_run_adds_one_replay_example_with_its_label_and_a_later_decline_updates_it`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_the_replay_set_is_mirrored_to_phoenix_only_when_it_changed`, `tests/test_truncation.py::test_run_inputs_stay_valid_json_however_long_they_are`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: a finished run adds one dataset example with its label; a later decline updates the label.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/evals.py`, `ops/crew.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Gap: OpenCode has no swarm adapter (SWM-28), so its runs are not recorded; every other harness's runs are (Hermes wakes, Claude Code and script crew runs).
- Findings: [FND-25](#fnd-25), [FND-39](#fnd-39)

#### SWM-83

**The Coach turns scorecards into agent changes** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every month the Coach shall read every scorecard and open agent-change artifacts with evidence, a target score and an experiment plan, and its own changes shall pass the same gate.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_scorecard_with_40_percent_declined_ideas_yields_one_agent_change_naming_its_target`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: a fixture scorecard with 40% declined ideas yields one agent-change artifact naming the target score.
- Trace: `ops/coach.py`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/systemd/crew-coach.timer`
- Findings: [FND-34](#fnd-34)

#### SWM-84

**Failures are classified** · could · functional · **implemented**

Every failed or declined run shall be classified into the MAST categories (specification, inter-agent misalignment, verification) and their failure modes, and the scorecard shall show the counts.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `MAST failure taxonomy (Cemri et al., arXiv 2503.13657)`, [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_labelled_fixture_runs_are_classified_with_at_least_80_percent_agreement`, `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_finished_run_adds_one_replay_example_with_its_label_and_a_later_decline_updates_it`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: labelled fixture runs are classified with at least 80% agreement with their labels.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`

### Rollout

#### SWM-85

**Phases with gates** · must · process · **planned**

The swarm shall be built in the phases 0 to 5, each shipped, tested and deployed on its own, and a phase shall start only when the previous phase's gate is met and recorded in the design doc.

- Parents: [SN-15](#sn-15), [SN-13](#sn-13)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-21](#dec-21)
- Verified by: -
- Phase: 0 Proof of the risky parts
- Acceptance: Inspection: each phase's gate result is in the design doc before the next phase's first commit.

#### SWM-86

**Prove the risky parts first** · must · process · **implemented**

Phase 0 shall prove that the gateway carries each agent's swarm token to an MCP server (per-user upstream credentials), that a Hermes Kanban task can be created and followed from outside Hermes, and the swarm's memory need, or choose and record the fallback for each.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-14](#cst-14)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15), `design: AI Stack Agent Swarm (claude.ai artifact 18fPjMRbQnxSVikesY8w6M)`
- Verified by: demonstration: `docs/SWARM-PHASE0.md`, `ops/swarm-probes/identity.sh`, `ops/swarm-probes/kanban.sh`, `ops/swarm-probes/proto.py`
- Phase: 0 Proof of the risky parts
- Acceptance: Demonstration: three recorded probes with their results in the design doc.
- Trace: `ops/swarm-probes/AGENTS.md`

#### SWM-87

**Shadow run before switch-over** · must · process · **deprecated**

Before an old path is removed, the new path shall run beside it for 7 days, and the switch shall happen only when every artifact the old path created in that week exists in the Ledger.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: [DEC-15](#dec-15)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_client.py::test_the_migration_report_names_what_the_guard_would_have_refused`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Analysis: a comparison report of the shadow week, attached to the phase gate.
- Trace: `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Reason: The owner chose to switch over at once (DEC-22). The replay of 2026-10-29 had shown the duplicate guard catching all six of his merges.

#### SWM-88

**Acceptance tests come with the code** · must · process · **implemented**

When a swarm requirement is implemented, its acceptance test shall be added in the same commit and its status set to implemented with that test in verify.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-15](#sn-15)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14), [DEC-21](#dec-21)
- Verified by: inspection: `AGENTS.md`, `requirements/AGENTS.md`
- Phase: 1 Ledger, registry and run contract
- Acceptance: Inspection: the checks refuse an implemented requirement without a test (QA-09); the commit names the ids (QA-11).
- Trace: `ops/req.py`

#### SWM-20

**Requirements live in the Ledger** · should · functional · **implemented**

Requirements shall become Ledger artifacts of kind requirement, so that a violated requirement is an open artifact agents can work on, and an agent's proposal shall name the requirements it serves.

- Parents: [SN-16](#sn-16), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-14](#dec-14), [DEC-15](#dec-15), [DEC-26](#dec-26)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase5.py::test_a_requirement_file_change_creates_or_updates_its_artifact_and_a_violated_one_is_open`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Acceptance: Test: a requirement file change creates or updates its Ledger artifact; a violated one is open.
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Findings: [FND-35](#fnd-35)

### Parallel work

#### SWM-91

**Big work is split into a task graph** · must · functional · **implemented**

When an accepted idea needs changes in more than one area, the planner agent shall split it into tasks with dependencies (a Ledger artifact of kind task per piece, linked depends_on), and tasks without open dependencies shall be offered to builders at the same time.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-24](#dec-24), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_an_idea_split_into_three_tasks_offers_two_at_once_and_the_third_after_both`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_the_planner_splits_only_across_areas_and_falls_back_to_one_task`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_an_idea_with_tasks_is_shipped_only_when_every_task_is_merged`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `ops/planner.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`
- Findings: [FND-36](#fnd-36)

#### SWM-92

**File leases keep parallel builders apart** · must · functional · **implemented**

While a build works on a task, the Ledger shall hold a lease on the files and code-graph modules the task names; a second task whose files overlap shall wait, and a lease shall end when its build ends or after 4 hours.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-24](#dec-24), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_leases_keep_tasks_on_the_same_file_apart_and_a_stale_one_expires`, `tests/test_swarm_phase4.py::test_builds_run_together_only_when_their_files_do_not_overlap`
- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `ops/builds.py`

#### SWM-93

**A merge queue lands parallel work one at a time** · must · functional · **implemented**

Approved builds shall land through one queue: each is merged onto the current main, checked on the combined result, and deployed; a failure takes that build out of the queue and sends it back, and the rest continue.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: [DEC-24](#dec-24), [DEC-25](#dec-25)

- Phase: 4 Parallel work, wider review and the harness adapters
- Trace: `ops/builds.py`

#### SWM-94

**Every build starts from the code graph** · should · functional · **implemented**

Before a builder changes code, the runner shall give it the code graph's view of the files it plans to touch: callers and callees, the tests that cover them, and the requirements traced to them; the review shall show the same blast radius.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [DEC-24](#dec-24), [DEC-25](#dec-25)
