### Verification

#### SWM-95

**Independent reviewers with different lenses** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each build shall be reviewed by at least two reviewers that do not share its engine or its context: one checks the change against the requirements it names, one looks for security and data-flow problems; any block shall stop the deploy unless the owner overrides it with a written reason, and each verdict and override shall be evidence on the idea.

#### SWM-96

**Every build names and proves its requirements** · should · functional · **implemented**

A build shall name the requirement ids it implements or changes, and its checks shall fail when a named requirement has no test that the build's change runs.

### Observability

#### SWM-106

**A map of the team** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Swarm page shall show every agent grouped by its job, with what it is for, how it runs and wakes, what it can reach, whether it reads private data, takes in outside content or can send data out, and its last 7 days of runs; each agent shall have one page (its housekeeping page's Team tab, or its own) with its last 30 days, its open health issues, its runs, with whom it works, what it said and did, the tasks it did steps for, its memory and the ideas it worked on; and a mission's page shall show its own last 30 days.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-28](#dec-28)

### Board

#### SWM-107

**The team room** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Chatroom shall be one timeline of what the agents do together, drawn from their work so that no agent has to post: tool calls between agents with their answers, work on another agent's artifact marked as help, Board messages, work on the shared artifacts, the owner's decisions and messages, and runs no one asked for; filtered by type, agent (two picked: only what passes between them), topic, time and text, with a burst of asks between the same two agents folded into one line; its filters come from the address on every visit and go back into it, and the run timeline under them shows the same agents and window.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-30](#dec-30)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_team_room.py::test_lines_mark_help_in_another_agent_s_lane_and_keep_every_text_whole`, `tests/test_team_room.py::test_the_ledger_feed_has_events_and_messages_but_not_the_requirements_mirror`, `tests/test_team_room.py::test_the_chatroom_is_the_team_room_with_its_filters`, `tests/test_swarm_views.py::test_a_tapped_line_opens_activity_on_what_passed_between_the_two_and_the_filter_does_not_stick`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Trace: `images/mission-control/teamroom.py`, `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`
- Rationale: The Board had 6 messages in total and only one step into another agent's lane had ever happened, while a mission asked the scout about 100 times a day through its tools: the teamwork is in the work, so the room is drawn from it. Broadcast group chats (AutoGen) cost every agent every message; shared work (MetaGPT's message pool, blackboard and stigmergy designs) coordinates with less.
- Findings: [FND-89](#fnd-89)

### Run contract

#### SWM-108

**Every agent knows the team** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every agent's start-of-run pack shall hold one line per agent of the swarm (its purpose, what it owns, how much of its work is open, when it last wrote to the Ledger, which one is itself) and the lane rule: each agent is responsible for its own lane first and for the team's goals, and gets involved in another's lane only with a clear, valuable contribution its owner would not see or has missed. swarm_context with an agent's id shall show that agent up close (its card and open work) and what it did lately (runs with summaries, its settled work with the reasons, the evidence it gave to others), as far as the asking agent may see it (SWM-67), without a new tool (the surface stays at 12). Reading is free; nothing makes an agent contribute.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [DEC-30](#dec-30)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_team_roster.py::test_every_run_starts_knowing_the_team_and_the_lane_rule`, `tests/test_team_roster.py::test_team_who_shows_an_agent_s_card_and_open_work_but_not_what_the_asker_may_not_see`, `tests/test_team_roster.py::test_team_work_shows_runs_settled_work_and_help_given_to_others`, `tests/test_team_roster.py::test_host_agents_read_the_team_in_their_prompt_and_mcp_agents_have_the_tools`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`
- Rationale: Before this, an agent's start pack held its own work, messages and lessons but nobody else: it could not know who else works here or what they do (the swarm_context team flag was never used). Discovery by agent cards follows A2A's Agent Card; the pull tools keep the cost to the agents that need it.

### Ledger

#### SWM-109

**Lane discipline** · should · functional · **implemented**

An agent's own write to another agent's artifact (evidence, a link from it, an update) shall be a help: it shall say why the artifact's owner would not see this (20 characters at least), shall be refused when it repeats what the artifact already holds, and shall fit the agent's daily budget (3 unless its card says otherwise). The helped agent (in its next run) or the owner (in the Chatroom) shall judge it useful or not, the owner's word over the agent's; without a verdict, the work moving on counts as useful and the work dropped as not useful. Each agent's scorecard shall count its helps, and the Coach shall propose an agent change towards quiet when at least half of 3 or more judged helps were not useful. Nothing makes an agent help; writes by the system, the checker, the owner and the stack's code for host agents are no helps.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: [DEC-30](#dec-30)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_lane_discipline.py::test_a_step_into_another_lane_names_why_its_owner_would_not_see_it`, `tests/test_lane_discipline.py::test_each_agent_steps_in_only_a_few_times_a_day`, `tests/test_lane_discipline.py::test_the_helped_agent_or_the_owner_judges_and_the_work_s_fate_does_otherwise`, `tests/test_lane_discipline.py::test_the_coach_turns_unhelpful_steps_into_an_agent_change_for_quiet`, `tests/test_lane_discipline.py::test_the_room_shows_why_and_lets_the_owner_judge`
- Phase: 5 Improvement loop: replay sets, the evaluation gate, the Coach
- Trace: `images/swarm/ledger.py`, `images/swarm/swarm.py`, `ops/coach.py`, `ops/swarm_client.py`, `images/mission-control/teamroom.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/swarm.js`
- Rationale: The owner's rule: each agent is responsible for its own lane first and for the team's goals, and gets involved in another's only with a clear, valuable contribution its owner would not see or has missed. Broadcast coordination costs every agent every message and the MAST study finds a third of multi-agent failures between agents; so a step in is rare, reasoned, new and judged, and an agent whose steps do not help learns to stay out.

### Observability

The map, the Activity feed and the timeline shall be drawn from one list of interactions, so the number on a line is exactly what a tap on that line opens, for every line and every window.

#### SWM-111

**Every run has one cause** · should · functional · **implemented**

Every run shall have exactly one cause (you, an agent, its timer, a rule, an alert, a test, or unknown), crew jobs recording theirs when they start; what is unknown or from before it was recorded shall be counted on screen, never hidden, and a lumped starter such as Hermes shall be settled to the real agent.

#### SWM-112

**What you asked and decided is on the map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The list shall hold what the owner did: each chat and spoken turn with its answer, Run now, a typed task, a question to Claude, and decisions at the app's door and on the shared work, each reaching the agent concerned; an arrow from the owner shall open those rows. Nothing the owner said or was told shall be shown to the AI phone. Tests shall stay apart from work and one piece of work shall count once, a chip showing the tests when asked.

#### SWM-113

**Tool use, hand-overs and messages to you are on the map** · should · functional · **implemented**

The list shall hold what an agent does through another (Main on the AI phone, Inbox on Gmail), hand-overs through a file or an output (a plan's next step, a mail filed for the curator or a topic, an idea to the builder), and every message to the owner with its named sender, all senders naming themselves; a link worked out from timing shall say it is matched.

#### SWM-114

A run that fell silent while the stack itself was down shall end as stopped at its last activity, with that reason, and raise no alert; a bar shall end at the run's last activity when it was silent to its end.

Every tool server of the gateway shall say what a call to it means for the map, or the build fails; a record the map reads that can't be read shall be named on screen instead of showing nothing.

## TSK Task engine

| Phase | | Requirements |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | Headroom | - |
| 1 | Task kernel | [TSK-01](#tsk-01), [TSK-02](#tsk-02), [TSK-03](#tsk-03), [TSK-04](#tsk-04), [TSK-20](#tsk-20) |
| 2 | Plan graph and durable steps | [TSK-05](#tsk-05), [TSK-06](#tsk-06), [TSK-07](#tsk-07), [TSK-08](#tsk-08), [TSK-09](#tsk-09) |
| 3 | Event fabric | [TSK-10](#tsk-10), [TSK-11](#tsk-11), [TSK-12](#tsk-12) |
| 4 | Self-staffing | [TSK-13](#tsk-13), [TSK-14](#tsk-14), [TSK-15](#tsk-15), [TSK-16](#tsk-16), [TSK-17](#tsk-17) |
| 5 | Reach | [TSK-18](#tsk-18), [TSK-19](#tsk-19) |

| 7 | Artifacts, map and telemetry | [TSK-23](#tsk-23), [TSK-24](#tsk-24), [TSK-25](#tsk-25), [TSK-26](#tsk-26), [TSK-27](#tsk-27) |
| 8 | Customisation and UX | [TSK-28](#tsk-28), [TSK-29](#tsk-29), [TSK-30](#tsk-30), [TSK-31](#tsk-31), [TSK-32](#tsk-32) |
| 9 | More reach | [TSK-33](#tsk-33) |

### Task kernel

#### TSK-01

**One task for every kind of work** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall represent every piece of work as a task with its goal, definition of done, budget, deadline, autonomy level and rhythm (once, on a schedule, on an event, or after other tasks finish), and chat, missions, builds and Claude tasks shall each create one.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: the user should just give a task and the AI Stack figures out the rest`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_every_door_s_work_becomes_a_task_with_every_field`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a task created from each door carries every field; the task list holds every run of the last 7 days.
- Trace: `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/missions.py`

#### TSK-02

**Give a task in one sentence** · must · interface · **implemented**

Mission Control shall start a task from one sentence in a single composer, and its deliberate mode shall show the task's fields filled with their presets before the task starts.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-06](#sn-06)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: give more deliberate tasks with details preset, but just giving a task is the usual way`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_owner_s_presets_are_kept_and_show_me_the_plan_first_waits`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a sentence becomes a task with every field set, and a preset changed in deliberate mode is kept. Checked on screen at 375 px.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/guide/20-tasks.md`

#### TSK-03

**The stack writes the specification** · must · functional · **implemented**

Before a task's first step, the intake agent shall write its specification (the goal, a checkable definition of done, the plan, and the agents it proposes with the reason for each), ask at most two questions and only when the goal cannot be planned without them, and wait for the owner only where the task's autonomy level asks for it.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `Blitzy: requirements into a specification and an action plan, checked against the result`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_planning_a_restart_cut_off_is_planned_again_or_failed_when_old`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_at_most_two_questions_and_answers_plan_it_again`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_coding_task_without_a_repository_asks_which_one`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_failed_intake_says_why_and_can_be_planned_again`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_prompt_names_every_agent_and_repository_and_the_app_cannot_be_used_from_the_ai_phone`, `eval/tasks-v1.yaml`, `eval/results/20261031-140852-tasks-gate.json`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Replay: 20 recorded one-sentence tasks (coding, research, analysis, watching) each get a specification with a checkable definition of done; no task gets more than two questions.
- Trace: `ops/tasks.py`, `prompts/intake.md`, `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/keys.sh`
- Findings: [FND-117](#fnd-117)

#### TSK-04

**One board for every task** · should · interface · **implemented**

The app shall list the tasks under Waiting on you, Running, Scheduled and Done, each with its plan, the live tree of its agents, its use of the subscriptions and the reason each agent was chosen; a scheduled task shall show when its last run ended, what it produced, and when the next run comes.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_after_another_task_waits_until_that_one_is_done`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_weekly_briefing_task_gets_its_content_not_the_plan_email_when_asked_and_shows_its_runs`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel
- Acceptance: Test: a running two-agent task appears under Running with both agents in its tree; checked on screen at 375 px.
- Trace: `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-77](#fnd-77)

### Plan graph and durable steps

#### TSK-05

**A plan graph for every task** · must · functional · **implemented**

The Conductor shall turn a specification into a graph of steps with dependencies and parallel branches, stored with the task in the runner's task database, and a step shall start only when every step it depends on has finished; a plan with a cycle, or with a step no agent can do, shall be refused.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `Anthropic dynamic workflows (beta, 2026-10-09): the lead model writes the orchestration`, [DEC-35](#dec-35)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_branches_run_together_the_join_after_both_and_a_cycle_is_refused`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_one_off_task_with_several_agents_goes_to_the_conductor_as_a_plan_graph`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_owner_can_cancel_and_every_planned_task_goes_to_the_conductor`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: a plan with two parallel branches and a joining step runs the branches together and the join after both; a plan with a cycle is refused.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

#### TSK-06

**Steps survive restarts** · must · quality · **implemented**

Each step shall be recorded with its inputs, outputs and resume point before and after it runs, so that a restart resumes the task at its first unfinished step and no finished step runs twice.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: [SWM-19](#swm-19), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_restart_runs_the_interrupted_step_again_and_no_finished_step_twice`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_that_fails_twice_fails_its_task_and_a_limit_is_waited_out`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_cut_off_by_the_runner_stopping_is_interrupted_not_failed`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_task_cut_off_by_the_runner_stopping_is_queued_again`, `eval/results/20261031-152501-conductor-gate.json`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: the runner is killed during step 3 of a five-step task; after the restart step 3 runs again, steps 1 and 2 ran once, and the task finishes.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`

#### TSK-07

**Any step on any harness** · must · functional · **partial**

The Conductor shall run each step on the harness and model its plan names, through the one adapter contract every harness implements.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: [SWM-02](#swm-02), [SWM-28](#swm-28)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_on_opencode_reads_only_and_records_its_tool_and_model_calls`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_each_step_s_prompt_carries_the_results_it_waits_for`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_long_requirements_list_reaches_the_builder_apart_from_its_brief`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: one task whose steps run on Hermes, Claude Code and OpenCode finishes, each step through its adapter.
- Trace: `config/opencode-step.json`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/shared.md`
- Gap: Hermes, Claude Code, Python and OpenCode have adapters (OpenCode since 2026-11-01, one `opencode run` per step, read only, run for real on a repository question); the phase 2 gate ran one task across Hermes, Claude Code and Python, and one task across Hermes, Claude Code and OpenCode has not run yet.
- Findings: [FND-99](#fnd-99)

#### TSK-08

**Steps start only with headroom** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Conductor shall start a step only when the PC has the memory its harness needs available (0.25 GB for a model call, 0.5 GB for a Hermes turn, 0.75 GB for Claude Code, by Windows' available memory) and the subscription the step uses is below its usage limit, and shall otherwise queue the step and show why it waits.

- Parents: [SN-13](#sn-13), [CST-14](#cst-14)
- Sources: [AST-12](#ast-12), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_starts_only_with_headroom_and_says_what_it_waits_for`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: with available memory stubbed below a step's need, the ready step stays queued with the reason shown, and starts when memory returns.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/windows/host-probe.ps1`
- Rationale: The first rule asked 1.5 GB free for every step and held the phase 2 gate at 0.7 GB while the owner played a game; a step asks for what its harness starts (owner, 31 Oct 2026: "You can make due with less RAM").

#### TSK-09

**One trace per task** · must · quality · **implemented**

Every agent run, model call and tool call of a task shall be part of one trace for that task, in the shape of the OpenTelemetry GenAI conventions (invoke_agent with chat and execute_tool spans under it), shown in Phoenix and in the task's run view.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07)
- Sources: `OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions (development status, 2026)`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_one_trace_the_task_s_run_holds_each_step_s_run`, `tests/test_runtime.py::test_a_hermes_run_without_a_ref_joins_the_live_step_of_its_agent`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_claude_step_waits_for_its_claude_task_also_across_a_restart`, `tests/test_otlp.py::test_a_task_run_is_one_trace_from_the_run_to_every_model_and_tool_call`
- Phase: 2 Plan graph and durable steps
- Acceptance: Test: a task with steps on two harnesses yields one trace whose spans name every agent run, model call and tool call.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/runtime.py`, `images/mission-control/runrecord.py`, `images/mission-control/otlp.py`
- Rationale: The task is one run in the runtime record, each step a run inside it and the agents' own runs and model calls inside the steps (#/live/task:<id>, Live trace and the task's page); a Hermes run, which can't carry its parent in, joins the live step of its agent; a Claude step's Claude task runs inside its step (since 2026-11-01); a minute after the run ends the tree goes to Phoenix as one trace (otlp.tree_spans, TSK-26).
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### Event fabric

#### TSK-10

**One event bus** · must · functional · **implemented**

The stack shall collect events from news searches and feeds, Forgejo and GitHub activity (read on a schedule, since nothing may reach the PC from outside), incoming mail, Home Assistant state changes, the calendar, watched files, prices, filings, schedules and finished tasks into one event log, each with its source, time and content, and shall show for each source when it last looked, what it found, or why it is off.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: trigger on different kinds of events, like a particular news event breaking`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_reach.py::test_a_calendar_rule_fires_once_its_time_before_the_event_and_the_address_stays_private`, `tests/test_events.py::test_the_web_sources_baseline_first_then_fire_their_rules`, `tests/test_events.py::test_the_local_sources_each_fire`, `tests/test_events.py::test_mail_and_forgejo_are_read_only_while_a_rule_asks_and_a_baseline_is_not_new`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: a sample event from each source lands in the event log with its source, time and content.
- Trace: `ops/ics.py`, `ops/events.py`, `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/tasks.py`

#### TSK-11

**Rules that start tasks** · must · functional · **implemented**

When a trigger rule's condition matches an event, the stack shall start a task from the rule's template at most once per cooldown, after a model confirms the event is the one the rule describes where the rule asks for it, and shall record each firing with the event that caused it.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_events.py::test_a_rule_waits_out_its_cooldown_runs_its_check_and_records_every_firing`, `tests/test_events.py::test_rules_are_checked_when_saved_and_schedules_come_due`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: two matching events within the cooldown start one task; a rule with the model check skips an event the check rejects; every firing names its event.
- Trace: `ops/events.py`, `prompts/intake.md`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`

#### TSK-12

**Breaking news** · should · functional · **implemented**

When at least three independent sources report the same new story on a watched subject within 30 minutes, the event bus shall raise one breaking-news event for that story, and later reports of the story shall attach to that event.

- Parents: [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: like a particular news event breaking`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_alert_s_answer_is_in_its_message`, `tests/test_events.py::test_breaking_news_is_three_publishers_on_one_new_story_within_30_minutes`
- Phase: 3 Event fabric
- Acceptance: Test: three sources on one story within 30 minutes raise one event, a fourth attaches to it, and two sources raise none.
- Trace: `ops/events.py`
- Findings: [FND-118](#fnd-118)

### Self-staffing

#### TSK-13

**What each agent can do, measured** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every agent card, harness and model shall carry its skills, tools and limits and its measured success, time and usage per task type, updated after each task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_judged_tasks_credit_each_agent_and_measure_it`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a finished task updates the measured success, time and usage of each agent, harness and model it used, for its task type.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `config/agents.yaml`

#### TSK-14

**Routing by measured results** · should · functional · **partial**

The Conductor shall choose each step's agent, model and harness from the measured results for the step's task type, and shall fall back to the next choice when a subscription reaches its usage limit.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `Vals Router Index (2026-10-20): no router beat the best single model on long agent tasks`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_step_goes_to_the_agent_that_did_that_kind_best_or_away_from_a_limit`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_the_conductor_routes_a_step_before_its_first_attempt_and_says_why`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Replay: routing by measured results scores at least as well as the fixed assignment on the replay sets; with one subscription stubbed at its limit, steps go to the next choice.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `ops/conductor.py`
- Gap: The Conductor routes a step before its first attempt to an agent sharing the planned one's role that met that kind of task at least 15 points more often (from 3 judged tasks on), or away from a subscription at its limit, and writes why on the step; whether routing beats the fixed assignment can only be measured once enough tasks are judged.

#### TSK-15

**Agents made for the task** · must · functional · **implemented**

When no agent card fits a step, the Conductor shall write a temporary agent (its prompt, tools, model, harness and budget) within the grants the task's autonomy level allows, run it under the existing keys, grants and sandbox, and end it with its task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `owner 2026-10-31: even invent new agents for this task specifically`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, [DEC-34](#dec-34)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_temporary_agent_stays_within_its_grants_and_ends_with_its_task`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_step_no_agent_fits_gets_a_temporary_agent_that_ends_with_the_task`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_an_agent_the_intake_names_that_does_not_exist_gets_a_temporary_agent`, `docs/SECURITY.md`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a step no card fits gets a temporary agent whose grants are inside the task's autonomy level; a grant outside it is refused; the agent is gone when its task ends. Security review before the phase ships.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

#### TSK-16

**Good temporary agents become permanent** · should · functional · **implemented**

A temporary agent that served at least three tasks that met their definition of done shall become a Coach proposal, replay-tested like any agent change, and every agent design shall stay in an archive with its scores.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `Automated Design of Agentic Systems (Hu, Lu, Clune; ICLR 2025)`, `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_design_is_archived_reused_by_name_and_proposed_after_three_met_tasks`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: the third successful task of a temporary agent creates one Coach proposal; the archive keeps the design with its scores.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`

#### TSK-17

**Each task judged against its definition of done** · must · quality · **implemented**

The Checker shall judge each finished task against its definition of done, reading beside the answer what the stack measured itself (each build's checks, the judge's check steps, the pages its agents opened), and the stack shall keep a daily history of success, time, tokens, usage headroom, queue length and the owner's corrections per agent, model and task type.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-08](#sn-08)
- Sources: [MIS-16](#mis-16), `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_staffing.py::test_the_judge_reads_what_the_stack_measured_not_only_the_summary`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_each_run_of_a_recurring_task_is_judged_on_its_own_and_a_quiet_run_is_not`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_a_finished_task_is_judged_point_by_point_and_a_build_by_what_happened`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_judged_tasks_credit_each_agent_and_measure_it`
- Phase: 4 Self-staffing
- Acceptance: Test: a finished task gets a verdict against its definition of done, and the day's history row of each agent, model and task type it used changes.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `prompts/shared.md`
- Findings: [FND-97](#fnd-97), [FND-92](#fnd-92), [FND-102](#fnd-102)

### Reach

#### TSK-18

**More harnesses at no cost** · could · functional · **partial**

The stack shall offer Codex CLI on the ChatGPT subscription as a harness, and shall add a further harness only if running it costs nothing.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_on_opencode_reads_only_and_records_its_tool_and_model_calls`
- Phase: 5 Reach
- Acceptance: Test: the adapter conformance suite (SWM-28) passes for Codex CLI.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `config/opencode-step.json`
- Gap: OpenCode runs steps on the ChatGPT subscription through the gateway, at no cost (TSK-07). Codex CLI is not a harness yet.

#### TSK-19

**Agents outside the stack through A2A** · could · interface · **implemented**

The swarm shall accept A2A tasks from agents outside the stack on the tailnet, each outside agent under its own key and grants.

- Parents: [SN-17](#sn-17), [CST-04](#cst-04)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`, `A2A protocol v1.0 (Linux Foundation, 2026)`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_reach.py::test_the_owner_s_calendar_and_a2a_switches_are_refused_from_the_ai_phone`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_outside_agents_are_off_until_switched_on_and_then_need_the_tailnet_and_their_token`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_an_outside_agent_s_message_is_a_task_that_waits_for_the_owner_and_it_sees_only_its_own`, `tests/test_reach.py::test_the_owner_adds_and_revokes_an_outside_agent_and_only_a_hash_is_kept`
- Phase: 5 Reach
- Acceptance: Test: an outside agent's A2A request with its token becomes a task that waits for the owner, and one without a token or from outside the tailnet is refused.
- Trace: `ops/a2a.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Rationale: The swarm already speaks A2A on the Docker network. The owner asked for it on 31 Oct 2026; it stays off until the owner switches it on, and an outside request waits for the owner's OK by default.

### Task kernel

#### TSK-20

**What the Conductor may do without asking** · must · constraint · **implemented**

The Conductor shall plan, staff and run tasks and create temporary agents within read-only and sandboxed grants without the owner's approval, and shall ask before it messages other people, spends money, deploys code, deletes data or acts on the AI phone.

- Parents: [SN-02](#sn-02), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-ROADMAP.md`, [DEC-34](#dec-34)
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_owner_s_presets_are_kept_and_show_me_the_plan_first_waits`
- Phase: 1 Task kernel

### Plan graph and durable steps

#### TSK-21

**Every task through the Conductor** · must · functional · **partial**

Every occurrence of every task, one-off, scheduled, event-triggered or after another task, shall run as a plan graph under the Conductor, a single-agent task as a one-step graph, with builds, Claude tasks, mission agents, the Topic Desk and delivery as step kinds that keep their own approvals.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_briefing_that_runs_as_its_mission_moves_under_the_conductor_and_its_mission_keeps_the_topic`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_worker_starts_an_event_s_and_a_schedule_s_run_while_it_holds_its_lock_and_heals_a_half_start`, `tests/test_triggers.py::test_a_task_s_trigger_starts_the_task_s_next_run_not_a_mission_run`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_task_on_an_event_waits_for_its_trigger_and_is_watched_with_the_missions_triggers`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_event_triggered_briefing_writes_the_desk_s_alert_and_delivers_it`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_price_watch_runs_its_shopping_agent_s_check_and_a_reached_target_starts_its_next_run`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_an_event_starts_a_run_whose_steps_know_the_event_and_more_events_wait_their_turn`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_weekly_briefing_task_gets_its_content_not_the_plan_email_when_asked_and_shows_its_runs`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_owner_can_cancel_and_every_planned_task_goes_to_the_conductor`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_step_waits_for_its_build_and_the_owner_s_tap_and_a_task_runs_again_on_request`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_a_sentence_gets_a_specification_and_reaches_its_door`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: a scheduled briefing task, an event-triggered task and a one-step chat task each run as a plan graph with their steps in the task's trace.
- Trace: `ops/missions.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `ops/shopping.py`, `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`
- Gap: Every task the intake plans runs under the Conductor, once, on a schedule, on an event (each firing an occurrence, its event in every step's prompt) or after another task, with coding as a build step, code questions as Claude steps (DEC-36), a briefing as the desk's plan or, on an event, the desk's alert (TSK-22), and a price watch as checks of its shopping agent, whose purchases stay its own runs (DEC-37). Work started at a door directly (a chat, a build, a Claude question, a mission made on its page) is followed there, not run as a graph.
- Findings: [FND-84](#fnd-84)

#### TSK-22

**A recurring task runs its plan each time, fanned out** · must · functional · **partial**

A recurring task shall keep its plan as a template that each occurrence copies with its own window, and a step that lists items shall run once per item in parallel as headroom allows, the next step getting every result; a briefing's plan shall be collect, research per topic group, write, check, deliver.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_delivered_briefing_grows_its_topic_wiki_and_a_deleted_one_stays_deleted`, `tests/test_staffing.py::test_each_run_of_a_recurring_task_is_judged_on_its_own_and_a_quiet_run_is_not`, `tests/test_desk.py::test_the_collected_events_survive_the_hand_over_and_each_finding_cites_its_links`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_briefing_runs_collect_scouts_write_and_deliver_and_each_desk_step_hands_on_its_work`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_scheduled_task_runs_at_once_then_each_time_from_its_template_with_its_own_trace`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_with_items_fans_out_in_parallel_and_the_next_waits_for_every_copy`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: one occurrence of a weekly briefing runs a collect step, three research steps in parallel and a write, check and deliver step, and the next occurrence copies the template again.
- Trace: `ops/staffing.py`, `images/desk/desk.py`, `ops/desk.sh`, `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Gap: A recurring task's occurrences copy its plan, each with its own steps, trace, result and verdict against the definition of done (a run that found nothing new is kept and not judged); a step with items fans out; a briefing runs collect, a scout per key question in parallel, write and deliver. The check step is the writer's citation check inside the write step, not a step of its own.
- Findings: [FND-84](#fnd-84), [FND-85](#fnd-85)

### Observability of the swarm

#### TSK-23

**What every step used and made is recorded** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every page, search result list, job ad, filing, price series, vault note and memory a step reads, and every finding, draft, report and delivery it makes, shall be recorded as an artifact with W3C PROV's used, generated and derived links, and the report's citations shall mark which sources it cited.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-20](#sn-20)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md`

An agent's budget (ops/keys.sh) and its prompt files (prompts/<owner>.md, replay-gated by ops/prompts.py) are still changed through a build, not in the app.

### Plan graph and durable steps

#### TSK-34

**Improve a wiki or one page as a task** · should · functional · **implemented**

On every wiki and on every wiki page the owner shall be able to say what is wrong or what to find out, and the stack shall make it a task whose research steps see the wiki or page as it stands and whose last step folds the findings into that wiki, the named page first, correcting what the findings contradict while keeping the old fact as superseded.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-10](#sn-10)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-01: Ideally i would want to fire refinement tasks for each level i am looking at`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_tasks.py::test_improving_a_wiki_or_a_page_is_a_task_that_ends_writing_into_that_wiki`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_wiki_refinement_s_research_sees_the_page_and_its_writer_folds_the_findings_in_page_first`, `tests/test_librarian.py::test_a_refinement_writes_the_pages_it_is_given_in_the_wiki_s_own_folders_and_leaves_the_vault_index`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: a request on a page becomes a task whose research prompts carry the page and whose wiki step writes the page asked about first; a wiki or page that is not there is refused; the vault wiki keeps its own folders and index.
- Trace: `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/mission_extras.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-83](#fnd-83)

#### TSK-35

**A step that cannot do its work is not done** · must · functional · **implemented**

The intake shall see, for each agent it may plan with, what the agent can reach (the AI phone, the house, e-mail, web research, the vault), and a step whose agent answers that it cannot do its work (its answer begins "BLOCKED:") shall fail with that reason, without a second attempt, so that its task fails and the owner's message says why, instead of the task being reported done.

- Parents: [SN-07](#sn-07), [SN-12](#sn-12)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-01: It doesnt actually open the task when i click that. Rly its done? I didnt see ai phone do anything.`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_step_that_says_it_is_blocked_fails_its_task_at_once_and_says_why`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_plan_sees_what_each_agent_can_reach`
- Phase: 6 Every task a plan graph
- Acceptance: Test: a step that begins BLOCKED fails at once with its reason and its task fails with the reason in the message; the agents the intake sees name the AI phone only for the agent that holds it.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `prompts/shared.md`
- Findings: [FND-97](#fnd-97)

## TRL Trials

### TRL-01

**A trial goes through the front door** · must · functional · **implemented**

A trial shall be a task in the owner's words with what is forbidden and a budget (eval/trials), given through the runner's inbox like the app gives it, into a fresh repository holding only its specification, watched to its end or its budget, an intake question answered for the unattended run and counted.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_is_checked_before_it_runs`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_task_given_straight_to_the_builder_is_followed_through_its_build`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_runner_s_answer_is_read_in_both_of_its_shapes`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `eval/trials/semver.yaml`, `Makefile`

### TRL-02

**An answer key no agent reaches, proved before it scores** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A trial's answer key shall live where no container is given it (/srv/ai/private/trials) and shall score its own reference solution fully before a run starts; a key that cannot is refused.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-16](#sn-16)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_answer_key_must_score_its_own_reference_fully_and_scores_by_area`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_s_build_ends_at_review_and_is_never_merged_and_the_judge_scores_it_too`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`

### TRL-03

**Scored by a program, in a sandbox** · must · functional · **implemented**

The delivered code shall be scored by the answer key's own program in a container with no network, read-only, as nobody, with limited memory, per area of the specification; the model judge's verdict is kept beside it.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_answer_key_must_score_its_own_reference_fully_and_scores_by_area`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `ops/staffing.py`
- Findings: [FND-92](#fnd-92), [FND-94](#fnd-94)

### TRL-04

**The clean room is audited** · must · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall be audited for forbidden reads (the pages its agents read) and actions (the commands and files of its builds and steps, not the prompts that name what is forbidden) and the delivered code, and its code's similarity to the upstream implementation measured with normalised token fingerprints and flagged above the trial's threshold.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_clean_room_audit_catches_forbidden_reads_and_actions_but_not_the_trial_s_own_names`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_similarity_tells_copied_code_from_independent_code`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`

### TRL-05

**A trial's build is scored, never merged** · must · constraint · **implemented**

A trial's build step shall end when its build reaches review, giving the branch and head the key scores; the run then archives the code, closes the build and removes the clone, so nothing of a trial is merged, deployed or left waiting for the owner.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_or_claude_task_a_step_started_is_not_a_task_of_its_own`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_trial_s_build_step_ends_at_review_and_is_scored_not_merged`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_trial_s_build_ends_at_review_and_is_never_merged_and_the_judge_scores_it_too`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-91](#fnd-91), [FND-99](#fnd-99)

### TRL-06

**Every run reported and kept** · should · functional · **implemented**

Each run shall be reported in the vault (score by area, every failed case, the clean room, the plan, builds, time, tokens, questions) and kept as a result for the scoreboard, and the owner told.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_report_says_the_score_the_clean_room_and_how_it_went`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-90](#fnd-90)

### TRL-07

**The roles software work needs** · should · functional · **implemented**

The swarm shall staff an Analyst, a Tester, a Tracer, a Breaker and a Presenter beside the coder, the reviewers and the judge, each with what it may and may not read enforced by its key, network and workspace.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [SN-17](#sn-17)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_role_s_build_stays_in_its_lane`, `tests/test_registry.py::test_the_repo_cards_pass_the_registry_check`
- Trace: `config/agents.yaml`, `ops/conductor.py`, `prompts/intake.md`

### TRL-08

**The Orchestrator loops and re-plans** · should · functional · **implemented**

The Conductor shall run bounded loops (code, test, fix until it passes or the budget ends) and, after each wave, let the Orchestrator change the plan with the change shown to the owner.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_between_waves_the_orchestrator_keeps_or_changes_the_rest_of_the_plan`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_recurring_or_fixed_plan_gets_no_checkpoint`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_the_checkpoint_reads_a_long_result_s_start_and_knows_it_is_cut`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_check_sends_failures_back_to_the_builder_until_they_pass`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_check_out_of_fix_rounds_reports_what_still_fails_and_the_plan_goes_on`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_only_a_check_loops_and_only_back_and_a_fold_keeps_the_loop_on_its_build`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_keeps_a_check_s_fix_loop_and_a_budget_preset`
- Acceptance: Test: a re-plan after a wave is recorded and shown on the task.
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/builds.py`, `prompts/shared.md`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`
- Rationale: The checkpoint is the intake agent on its own key (one model call); it never touches a finished step, adds at most three steps a look (each after finished or planned steps, and before steps not yet started that must wait for it) and looks at most three times a run, so a run's cost stays bounded. It reads each result's first 6,000 characters, marked as cut where it is longer.
- Findings: [FND-94](#fnd-94), [FND-100](#fnd-100)

### TRL-09

**A scoreboard across versions** · should · functional · **implemented**

The app shall show every trial's runs side by side, with score, cost, time, interventions and the coordination numbers, per version of the agents and the code.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_run_s_tokens_count_each_model_call_once`, `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_scoreboard_holds_every_run_side_by_side_with_its_version`
- Acceptance: Test: two runs of one trial on different versions show side by side with the change between them named.
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `images/mission-control/server.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`, `images/mission-control/web/static/app.js`
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### TRL-10

**A builder's work is one build** · must · functional · **implemented**

Consecutive builder steps of a plan (a builder step whose only predecessor is a builder step nothing else waits for) shall become one build whose brief lists them as stages, so a stage that plans or checks without committing does not fail the task.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_consecutive_builder_steps_become_one_build_with_stages`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`
- Rationale: The first trial run (semver, 1 Nov 2026) failed in 3 minutes because its plan's first builder step read and planned, committed nothing, and a build without a commit fails; its retry did the same.
- Findings: [FND-90](#fnd-90)

### TRL-11

**A task stops at its budget** · should · functional · **implemented**

A task may carry a budget of hours and tokens (its run's model calls and its builds' tokens); once it is spent, no further step shall start, and the task shall fail saying which budget ran out.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19), [CST-02](#cst-02)
- Sources: `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_build_inside_the_trace_counts_once_against_the_budget`, `tests/test_conductor.py::test_a_spent_budget_stops_the_next_step`, `tests/test_tasks.py::test_the_intake_keeps_a_check_s_fix_loop_and_a_budget_preset`
- Trace: `ops/conductor.py`, `ops/tasks.py`, `ops/trials.py`
- Findings: [FND-101](#fnd-101)

### TRL-12

**Requirements traced and gaps found, scored** · should · functional · **implemented**

A trace trial (T2) shall give the swarm a specification's requirements and an implementation with every requirement ID taken out of its code, ask for each requirement's file and line and for every requirement the code does not meet, and score the answer against where the IDs sat (the function), the deviations planted in the code and the code's own defects the key has confirmed, a gap the key has reviewed and rejected counting as a false alarm (TRL-13 for one it does not know); on an open-source broker (mqtt-trace) and on the stack's own code against its own requirements (stack-trace).

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `chat 2026-11-01: take the existing implementation and the requirements ... map where in the code requirements are fullfilled ... detect which requirements are unfullfilled`, `docs/SWARM-TRIALS.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_the_trace_trial_gets_the_implementation_beside_its_specification`
- Trace: `eval/trials/mqtt-trace.yaml`, `eval/trials/stack-trace.yaml`, `ops/trials.py`
- Rationale: The MQTT spec pilot (spec/README.md) gives the key and the numbers to beat; the key and its make_key.py live under /srv/ai/private/trials/mqtt-trace, out of every agent's reach.

### TRL-13

**A gap beyond the key is for review, and a run can be scored again** · should · functional · **implemented**

A trial's scorer shall list a gap its key does not know for review, unscored, with the agents' reason; the report and the scoreboard shall name those gaps; and a recorded run shall be scored again with the key as it is now, its tokens counted again from the run record, the record keeping what it said before.

- Parents: [SN-19](#sn-19)
- Sources: `mqtt-trace run 2026-11-02 02:16: 25 gaps beyond the key, all counted as false alarms; of four read in the code, three were real defects of mochi-mqtt (topics.go 615, packets.go 427, clients.go 537) and one a MAY`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_trials.py::test_a_gap_beyond_the_key_is_for_review_and_a_run_can_be_scored_again`
- Trace: `ops/trials.py`, `images/mission-control/web/static/task.js`
- Rationale: The key of a trace trial is one reading of the code, not all of it; a trace that finds a real defect the key does not know must not lose points for it, and the owner reviews such gaps into the key (real, or not).

### TRL-14

**The trials run as a weekly rotation with a trend** · must · functional · **partial**

One trial shall run each night at 01:00 in a weekly rotation (research accuracy, stack-trace, memory, coding, review catch, offer picking, phone routine and the voice test), never two at once and skipped while a task runs, and

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-16](#sn-16)

- Trace: `ops/rotation.py`, `ops/systemd/rotation.timer`, `ops/systemd/rotation.service`
- Gap: From 2 Nov 2026 rotation.timer starts one trial a night at 01:00 (research accuracy twice, stack-trace, semver, mqtt-trace, Mustache; Sunday rests) when the stack is idle, else skips the night with why. #/trials shows each run beside the one before (score, time, tokens, code version) but draws no trend over the weeks yet; the memory trial runs on the Keeper's own path, and review catch, offer picking, the phone routine and the voice test do not exist yet.

### TRL-15

**A drop in score freezes what it examines** · must · functional · **proposed**

A trial run that scores below its last good run by more than its noise band (the spread of its first three runs) shall open a finding naming the commits since that run and hold the licences of the classes it examines at ask until it scores again.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-02](#sn-02)
- Sources: `docs/NEXT-PHASE.md`
- Verified by: -

### TRL-16

**New exams for research, offers, reviews and the phone** · must · functional · **partial**

The trials shall include research accuracy (25 questions with known answers, claims and citations checked), offer picking (30 recorded offer sets with the right pick and traps), review catch (10 seeded bugs the reviews must find) and a phone routine with checks, each with its key out of every agent's reach.

- Parents: [SN-08](#sn-08), [SN-11](#sn-11)

Offer picking, review catch and the phone routine are not written yet.

### TRL-17

**Probes look for what the trials do not** · should · process · **proposed**

Each probe of docs/NEXT-PHASE.md shall run as a task (source probe:<name>) with its question and measure written before it runs, and its result and the change it led to shall go into docs/PROBES.md.

## VOI Voice

### VOI-01

**Speech services on the NAS, for the NAS only** · must · constraint · **implemented**

Speech to text and text to speech for Home Assistant's voice pipeline shall run on the NAS, listen on the NAS itself only (127.0.0.1), store no audio, keep their logs (which carry transcripts) to 1 MB each, and be deployed host-side with images pinned by digest.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_voice.py::test_the_speech_services_listen_on_the_nas_only_and_keep_small_logs`
- Trace: `nas/voice/compose.yaml`, `ops/deploy-voice.sh`

### VOI-02

**The voice is measured, not assumed** · should · functional · **implemented**

A repeatable measurement shall report, for the speech services on the NAS, the word error rate and the time per sentence of speech to text on spoken home sentences with noise, and the time to the first audio of each candidate voice; it shall store no audio.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_voice.py::test_the_bench_speaks_wyoming_and_scores_words`
- Trace: `ops/voice_bench.py`

### VOI-03

**A voice gateway Home Assistant can talk to** · must · functional · **implemented**

The app shall offer Home Assistant's own Ollama integration a gateway on a port of its own (127.0.0.1:11435 on the PC) with only the model list and the chat, refuse every call without its key (and all calls while no key is set), offer one model per room (hermes:kitchen), and stream Hermes' answer back as Ollama does.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11), [SN-05](#sn-05)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_the_gateway_needs_its_key_and_offers_one_model_per_room`, `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_a_spoken_turn_joins_the_voice_chat_and_streams_back_as_ollama_does`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `compose/apps.yaml`

### VOI-04

**One running Voice chat** · must · functional · **implemented**

Every spoken turn shall join one chat called Voice, with its room, in the same Hermes session from every room and the app, framed by the voice prompt (main.md VOICE); Home Assistant's own history and prompt shall not be used, and "new topic" shall start a fresh Hermes session while the chat keeps what was said.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_a_spoken_turn_joins_the_voice_chat_and_streams_back_as_ollama_does`, `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_let_me_check_while_a_tool_runs_and_new_topic_starts_a_fresh_session`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`, `prompts/main.md`

### VOI-05

**No silence while Hermes looks something up** · should · functional · **implemented**

When Hermes reaches for a tool before saying anything, the gateway shall say "Let me check" at once, and it shall record on each spoken answer its room, the time to its first word and whether it bridged.

- Parents: [SN-11](#sn-11)
- Sources: `docs/HOME-VOICE.md`
- Verified by: test: `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_let_me_check_while_a_tool_runs_and_new_topic_starts_a_fresh_session`, `tests/test_voice_gateway.py::test_a_spoken_turn_joins_the_voice_chat_and_streams_back_as_ollama_does`
- Trace: `images/mission-control/server.py`

### VOI-06

**Home Assistant set up by a repeatable script** · should · functional · **implemented**

One script shall connect Home Assistant to the speech services, to the voice gateway (with its key) and to one Hermes agent per room, and create the English pipelines without a server-side wake word or local intents; run again, it shall keep what exists and add only what is missing.

The agents shall read or touch the Home Assistant app on the AI phone, whose sign-in reaches the house, the FRITZ!Box and the NAS, only inside a phone task the owner approved that names it; free mode alone shall not open it.

## Decisions

| Decision | Date | What | Supersedes |
|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="dec-01"></a>**DEC-01** Subscription only | 2026-10-27 | No paid model in the gateway, no fallback to one; all agent keys get subscription models only. Runs that hit the usage limit wait and retry. | the earlier $5 Anthropic + $5 OpenRouter development budget with per-key daily caps |
| <a id="dec-02"></a>**DEC-02** Everything in English | 2026-10-27 | Chat, app, reports, wikis and Telegram in English, reversing the earlier choice of German briefings. | - |
| <a id="dec-03"></a>**DEC-03** Builder in a sandbox with tap-to-deploy | 2026-10-27 | The builder codes in a sandbox, cannot push or deploy, and every change ships only after the owner's tap, with smoke test and automatic rollback (not full PC rights). | - |
| <a id="dec-04"></a>**DEC-04** Build order for hands in the world | 2026-10-28 | AI review and Semgrep, then Serena, then the share target and the old phone as a sealed AI phone, then browser hands and shopping missions that never buy on their own, then (later) sessions on the main phone. | - |
| <a id="dec-05"></a>**DEC-05** Observability first | 2026-10-28 | Every agent feature ships with its timeline, files read and changed, payoff, statistics and issue signals, clickable in Mission Control; a feature without it is unfinished. | - |
| <a id="dec-06"></a>**DEC-06** The guide is part of the Stack page | 2026-10-28 | The in-app guide lives on the Stack page, not as its own tab. | - |
| <a id="dec-07"></a>**DEC-07** Home Assistant on the NAS | 2026-10-29 | Home Assistant runs as a new container on the NAS (LAN only); nothing changes about existing NAS data or shares. One Home Assistant Voice speaker may be proposed for the owner to buy. | - |
| <a id="dec-08"></a>**DEC-08** The AI phone screen sleeps | 2026-10-29 | The AI phone's screen is no longer kept on (it drained the battery faster than it charged); it sleeps after 30 s and every action wakes it first. | 2026-10-28: the screen stays awake while connected (30 s wake loop, Stay awake) |
| <a id="dec-09"></a>**DEC-09** Needs you is only the owner's decisions | 2026-10-29 | Health alerts and the stack's own improvement ideas leave "Needs you" and show as "Stack upkeep". | - |
| <a id="dec-10"></a>**DEC-10** No caps, a lifecycle instead | 2026-10-29 | Capping how many ideas an agent may keep open is not the right approach; agents should reopen and refine ideas over many runs within a generic artifact lifecycle. | - |
| <a id="dec-11"></a>**DEC-11** Off-site key copy in OneDrive | 2026-10-25 | The age key is copied to OneDrive (own folder), not to a USB stick; Windows 11 upgrade declined. | - |
| <a id="dec-12"></a>**DEC-12** No ChatGPT-web bridge | 2026-10-27 | No browser automation of the ChatGPT web app as an API (terms of service, bot-detection evasion); not to be proposed again. | - |
| <a id="dec-13"></a>**DEC-13** KeepADB on the AI phone | 2026-10-29 | KeepADB 1.8.38 (F-Droid, checksum verified) may be installed so the AI phone starts in AI mode after a restart; each phone setting it needs runs only on the owner's Allow. | - |
| <a id="dec-14"></a>**DEC-14** Requirements engineering for the repo | 2026-10-29 | The stack's requirements are engineered retroactively, prepared for the agent swarm, kept current with every change, and contradictions between the parts are looked for and reported. | - |
| <a id="dec-15"></a>**DEC-15** Agent swarm design | 2026-10-29 | Agents on different harnesses and models get a shared artifact ledger with a generic lifecycle, a communication board (direct, multicast to all, rooms) and a chatroom, reusing existing technology. | - |
| <a id="dec-16"></a>**DEC-16** Claude Code as second engine | 2026-10-28 | Claude Code on the owner's Claude subscription becomes the builder's second engine, the default for code work when connected, and the reviewer of ChatGPT builds; code it reads is sent to Anthropic. | - |
| <a id="dec-17"></a>**DEC-17** The old phone is the AI phone | 2026-10-28 | The old Samsung S22 (ai-phone) is the AI phone; the main phone (S25) is not driven until a separate design for sessions on it. | - |
| <a id="dec-18"></a>**DEC-18** Voice through Alexa and a Voice speaker | 2026-10-29 | Talking to Hermes through the Echos ("Alexa, Hermes …") is wanted; a Home Assistant Voice speaker may follow. | 2026-10-28: the owner does not want voice |
| <a id="dec-19"></a>**DEC-19** Approve everything that ships | 2026-10-27 | The stack's own improvement proposals and builds ship only after the owner's approval. (Asked on 2026-10-29 whether small upkeep fixes may ship by themselves; no answer yet.) | - |
| <a id="dec-20"></a>**DEC-20** The owner does not run scripts | 2026-10-24 | Setup and recovery prefer routes that need no scripts or clicks from the owner; what only he can do (passwords, sign-ins, purchases, approvals) is asked for in one step with exact instructions. | - |
| <a id="dec-21"></a>**DEC-21** Swarm requirements before swarm code | 2026-10-29 | Before the agent swarm is built, its requirements are engineered to best practice and the current state of the art (A2A, MCP, the OWASP agentic top 10, multi-agent failure research), each with an acceptance criterion and a rollout phase. | - |
| <a id="dec-22"></a>**DEC-22** Switch over to the Ledger now | 2026-10-29 | No shadow week; the swarm's Ledger becomes the only store of the stack's ideas at once, the caps go, and the timelines, Claude Code traces and the session landing path are built in the same go ("Why wait for 5th? Implement all now and without gaps"). | the shadow week of SWM-87 |
| <a id="dec-23"></a>**DEC-23** The AI phone asks before typing or opening anything | 2026-10-29 | Typing text, opening a web address and opening an app on the AI phone always ask the owner, even inside a time window, so private data cannot leave through the phone unseen (answering FND-02 with the recommended option, "do all of this"). | - |
| <a id="dec-24"></a>**DEC-24** The swarm grows into parallel work, wider review and a live board | 2026-10-29 | After the phase 3 review (review 2026-10-29: full-scale swarm gaps (compared with Blitzy-style parallel code agents)), the owner asked for a full-scale, production-grade swarm. Phase 4 adds parallel work (a task graph, file leases, a merge queue, code-graph context) and independent reviewers; the Swarm page adds who is working right now and requests to the owner; phase 5 adds flow metrics. Governance stays: only the owner's taps decide, and every deploy needs the owner's approval. | - |
| <a id="dec-25"></a>**DEC-25** How phase 4 makes parallel work safe | 2026-10-30 | The owner asked to do phase 4 and close all gaps. The task graph lives in the Ledger (kind task, depends_on links), not in Hermes Kanban (SWM-21 deprecated): the Ledger already holds every artifact with its history and the owner's decisions, and the builders are not Hermes profiles. Two build slots on the 16 GB PC, one ChatGPT build at a time. Approved builds land through one merge queue, checked again on the combined code when main moved. The owner's assistant (main) reads and writes the Board but takes requests only from the owner (wake never), so no agent can steer the agent that holds the phone, mail and house tools. A reviewer's block stops the deploy; the owner may override it with a written reason (only his taps decide, DEC-24), and the override is evidence on the idea. | - |
| <a id="dec-26"></a>**DEC-26** How phase 5 lets the agents improve without rewriting themselves | 2026-10-30 | The owner asked to start phase 5. Agents are tuned only through agent changes: standing instructions on their card (or a model, tools or harness change), written by the Coach or the owner, never by the agent itself. A change is replayed on the agent's own past runs, with and without it, on the agent's own model, and is scored by code, not by a model judge: TF-IDF similarity to what the owner accepted, declined or merged (the Ledger's calibrated 0.20), and valid operations. It ships only when no score drops and its target rises; the owner still taps Build it and approves the build. After it ships it is watched for 14 days on real runs and one tap reverts it. Runs from before runs were recorded count as history examples (the evidence the agent cited, the artifact it wrote, its fate). Failures are classified into MAST by rules on the reason their label carries; what no rule matches stays unclassified. Requirements are mirrored into the Ledger (kind requirement), so ideas name the ones they serve. Phoenix keeps the replay sets and experiments as datasets for inspection; the Ledger keeps the record. Experiments use their own service key (vk-eval) so they never spend an agent's key. | - |
| <a id="dec-27"></a>**DEC-27** No truncation by default | 2026-10-30 | The owner, with a screenshot of cut log lines on the Swarm sync page: "I hate truncation. Do not truncate user facing input. Whenever you truncate anything I need a clear justification." Text the owner reads is shown whole; his own input is refused with the limit named, never cut; any remaining cap names its reason in the code (ops/truncation.py enforces it) and shows the cut when it hits. AI-written code tends to truncate by habit; the rules in AGENTS.md (Truncation) and the check exist so it cannot do so silently. | - |
| <a id="dec-28"></a>**DEC-28** Show the swarm as it works | 2026-10-30 | The owner found the Swarm page nearly unchanged after phases 4 and 5 and asked for timelines of when agents ran, interaction maps, the agents' conversations and a map of the agents; then "Actually I want all 4." The Board and the Ledger hold little (6 messages), while the runtime record holds every run and which run started which (a mission asked the scout 96 times in three days). So the four views are drawn from the runtime record first: Timeline, Map, the tool calls between agents in the Chatroom (marked as tool calls, not Board messages), and the team map in Agents. | - |
| <a id="dec-29"></a>**DEC-29** One list of ideas | 2026-10-30 | The review of 2026-10-30 found the stack's improvement ideas in three places: Code (The stack suggests: the crew's and the self-review's ideas), Agents (Improve: the Coach's agent changes) and Home (Stack upkeep, a count). The owner said "Yes, merge them". Both kinds are Ledger artifacts decided with the same Build it and Dismiss, so they are one ranked list under Agents → Ideas, each card marked with its kind; Code and Home only link to it. The numbers of the improvement loop (replay sets, failure classes, requirements in the Ledger) moved to Flow. | - |
| <a id="dec-30"></a>**DEC-30** The Chatroom is the team room, drawn from the agents' work | 2026-10-30 | The owner did not like the Chatroom (a list of rooms, most "no messages yet") and asked for a common place to read how the agents coordinate, with filters by agent and topic, without forcing agents to contribute, each agent responsible for its own lane first and stepping into another's only with something its owner would not see. Research (MetaGPT, Magentic-One, Anthropic's research system, Claude Code agent teams, the MAST failure study, stigmergy) and the stack's own numbers (6 Board messages ever, one cross-lane step, about 100 tool-call asks a day) say coordination works best through the shared work, with rare targeted messages. Part 1 (built): the Chatroom draws one timeline from the work with filters, and marks work on another agent's artifact as help. Part 2 (built, "Yes, build part 2"): a team roster and the lane rule at the start of every run, and tools to look into another agent's work (SWM-108). Part 3 (built, "Yes, build part 3": SWM-109) (lane discipline: a step into another lane names why its owner would not see it, a daily budget, and the owner agent's verdict feeding the scorecards) wait for the owner. | - |
| <a id="dec-31"></a>**DEC-31** The stack's memories as one system | 2026-10-30 | The owner asked for an overview of every memory system, a coherent design, the wikis' bubble map on the Wiki tab and where it is seen which agent uses which wiki, and asked whether one wiki should hold all the others. The review found ten stores (vault notes, vault wiki, agent wikis, journals, mission notes, Hindsight, the Ledger, skills, run records, the search index) with overlaps and no curator for three. Following CoALA (episodic, semantic, procedural), the LLM-wiki pattern (raw sources, one curator, ingest, query, lint) and scoped stores: no wiki that merges all the others (it would mix topics and owners), the vault wiki as the top layer with hub pages for shared subjects, one writer per wiki, reading scoped by the card, Hindsight for personal facts only, and every store with a limit, a curator and a view. Part 1 (built, "Yes, build part 1"): the inventory, the Memory section on Stack, the wikis' map and who reads and writes which wiki. Part 2 (built, "Yes, build part 2"): one opening per run with the swarm's pack inside, the working state in the journal, reading scoped by the card, one writer per wiki folding in helps, no journal copies in Hindsight (MEM-03 to MEM-06). Part 3 (built, "Yes, build part 3"): the Librarian (MEM-08, MEM-12, DEC-32). Part 4 (built, "Yes, build part 4"): prompts as files, tested (MEM-11, DEC-33). | - |
| <a id="dec-32"></a>**DEC-32** The Librarian tidies every wiki and adds nothing | 2026-10-30 | The owner said "Yes, build part 3". The memory curator, which already tidies the journals and long-term memory, becomes the Librarian, with no new agent or key: once a week it lints every agent wiki, marks pages not confirmed for 90 days as stale (agent wikis hold news; the vault wiki keeps its own 180-day rule), and asks one model call per wiki which candidate pairs are one subject and which claims contradict. It is the one change to a wiki besides its writer's (MEM-03), and it only rearranges: a merge keeps every link of both pages and leaves a stub that points on, a contradiction is a callout with both sources already on the page, and nothing is deleted except its own hub pages whose subject is no longer shared. Hubs are the top layer the owner asked about: one vault-wiki page per subject two or more wikis share, linking each wiki's page, instead of one wiki holding all the others. It also runs qmd cleanup. Each pass is one vault commit; a store over its limit for 8 days alerts its curator. | - |
| <a id="dec-33"></a>**DEC-33** Prompts are files, versioned by their text and changed only after a replay | 2026-10-30 | The owner said "Yes, build part 4". The 42 agent prompts of ops/ and the Hermes configure scripts moved verbatim into prompts/<owner>.md, one file per card (or service, or shared.md), each a marked section; the code loads them by name and shell scripts render them. A prompt's version is the sha of its text, so it can never be forgotten or bumped wrongly; each send records it, and the card hash follows the file. A change is accepted by a replay instead of a reviewer's taste: the prompt's last live cases (or committed cases), the approved and the new text on the agent's own model, a pairwise judge in random order, passing when the new text is valid as often and the judge prefers the old answer no more often. The verbatim move is each prompt's baseline, accepted only as its first record. The swarm's agent changes (SWM-18) keep their own scored replay of proposals. The desk's, the scout's and the privacy check's prompts followed ("Yes, do MEM-13"): their containers read prompts/ through a read-only mount (MEM-13). | - |
| <a id="dec-34"></a>**DEC-34** What the stack may do without asking | 2026-10-31 | The owner accepted the roadmap's proposed default ("Accept the default"). Without asking, the stack plans, staffs and runs tasks and may create temporary agents within read-only and sandboxed grants; it asks before it messages other people, spends money, deploys code, deletes data or acts on the AI phone. A task given in a sentence starts as soon as it is planned unless the owner chose "Show me the plan first"; every door keeps its own approvals (the deploy tap, purchases, the AI phone, Gmail's Trash). | - |
| <a id="dec-35"></a>**DEC-35** The plan graph lives with the tasks, not in the Ledger | 2026-10-31 | Building phase 2 ("Start phase 2"), the Conductor's plan graph and step journal went into the runner's task database (task_steps beside tasks, in the nightly backup) instead of the swarm's Ledger, as the roadmap first said. The Ledger's task model belongs to the idea-to-build flow (one plan per idea, builders claim its tasks) in a service capped at 256 MB, while a step has to be recorded in the same transaction that starts it, so a restart can never run a finished step again. The trace is the runtime record's (one run per task, a run per step inside it). A one-off research, analysis or errand task whose plan uses two or more agents goes to the Conductor; every other task keeps its door. | - |
| <a id="dec-36"></a>**DEC-36** Every planned task goes to the Conductor | 2026-10-31 | The owner asked for every task through the Conductor, recurring ones included ("Implement all properly", docs/SWARM-DESIGN.md). Since phase 6a every task the intake plans runs as a plan graph, one step or many, once or on a schedule, with a coding task's builder step as a build assignment and a code question as a Claude step; it replaces DEC-35's last sentence (only multi-agent one-off work went to the Conductor). Briefings, price watches and event-triggered tasks keep their mission until phase 6b, because the Topic Desk and the price watch are not yet steps. | - |
| <a id="dec-37"></a>**DEC-37** A price watch is its shopping agent's check; purchases stay the agent's runs | 2026-11-01 | The owner asked for price watches and event-triggered tasks under the Conductor ("Please do these two properly"). A price watch task holds its shopping agent (ops/shopping.py: limits, proposals, approvals, the order's tracking), made once with no schedule of its own; each occurrence of the task is one check of that agent (offers, a proposal or watching), and a waiting proposal that reaches its price or date starts the task's next occurrence. The purchase after the owner's tap and the order tracking stay the agent's own runs: they are the narrow deterministic path of docs/SHOPPING.md, with its own approval, and a plan step must not be able to start them. An event-triggered task runs each firing as an occurrence (its event in every step's prompt; events that come meanwhile wait, at most five); a briefing on an event is the desk's alert, then its delivery. Completes DEC-36's last sentence. | - |
| <a id="dec-38"></a>**DEC-38** One OK per phone task; free mode asks only for private data | 2026-11-01 | The owner found an approval for every tap too much ("I want to give it permission to use the phone"). An agent asks once for a whole task (its apps, the exact texts it types, up to an hour); the owner's time window becomes a free mode where typing and opening apps and addresses run without asking unless the text holds an e-mail address, phone number, IBAN or key; a request already waiting goes ahead when the permission comes. Money, passwords, blocked apps and Stop are unchanged. | DEC-23 |
| <a id="dec-39"></a>**DEC-39** SQLite for the swarm | 2026-11-02 | The owner confirmed SQLite for the swarm's Ledger ("keep SQLite for the swarm database ... Yes do all"): one writer (the Swarm service), WAL, a nightly snapshot the restore drill checks, 7 MB. Postgres would add a second failure point and buy nothing on one machine. Closes design decision 2 (SWM-73). | - |
| <a id="dec-43"></a>**DEC-43** The stack asks Windows; Windows fetches | 2026-11-06 | The owner wanted the stack to talk to Claude Code outside WSL. WSL stays cut off (interop and automount off, no port): a small script on Windows fetches requests from a folder and treats them as data, the way the host probe fetches a disk scan. Status needs no approval; a question goes to Claude with reading tools only, in folders listed on Windows where WSL cannot write; only a turn of the owner's own chat can ask. Changes on Windows would need the owner's tap on the whole job and are not built. Build 27, a second Claude inside WSL, was rejected: it could not reach the Windows sessions. | - |
| <a id="dec-42"></a>**DEC-42** The AI phone stays on Wi-Fi | 2026-11-04 | Asked whether the S22 will ever run without Wi-Fi, on a SIM only, the owner answered "No, always Wi-Fi". Any Wi-Fi counts, the S25 hotspot included, so ADB over Wi-Fi and Tailscale stay the only way in and the agent app of docs/PHONE-ANYWHERE.md Stage 2 is not built. | - |
| <a id="dec-41"></a>**DEC-41** The AI phone has no single-step approvals | 2026-11-04 | The owner again found approving single actions too much ("I do not want to allow every singel action on AI Phone. I want to allow more macro."). DEC-38 had added one OK per task but left a card per action as the fallback when an agent did not ask for a task. That fallback is gone: taps, swipes, keys, typing and opening apps or addresses run only inside an approved job or free mode, and otherwise are refused with the instruction to ask for the job. Money, settings, deleting, private text in free mode, passwords, blocked apps and Stop are unchanged. | - |
| <a id="dec-40"></a>**DEC-40** The Memory Keeper fixes the memory by rules | 2026-11-02 | The owner asked for one dedicated agent that runs periodically and fixes the memory systems, contradictions and false information included, designed carefully to best practice, and answered D2 ("Yes do all"): the memory agent may retire duplicates, test noise and facts on agents' runs, translate facts into English and date passing states itself, softly and restorably, with each change listed and undoable, while its answer-key trial passes; contradictions of his own facts stay his to decide; world knowledge is corrected only through research with sources (docs/MEMORY.md, the Memory Keeper; MEM-21). | DEC-32 |
